  62: 
  63: export interface AttackEnvelopeConfigV2 {
  64:   envelopeHalfWidthPx: number; // default 48
  65:   envelopeMinBelowPx: number;  // default 100
  66:   envelopeMaxBelowPx: number;  // default 260
  67:   envelopeTargetBelowPx: number; // default 160
  68: }
  69: 
  70: export const DEFAULT_ATTACK_ENVELOPE_CONFIG_V2: AttackEnvelopeConfigV2 = {
  71:   envelopeHalfWidthPx: 48,
  72:   envelopeMinBelowPx: 100,
  73:   envelopeMaxBelowPx: 260,
  74:   envelopeTargetBelowPx: 160
  75: };
  76: 
  77: export function isBotInsideAttackEnvelope(
  78:   botPos: Vec2,
  79:   playerPos: Vec2,
  80:   inflatedObstacles: Rect[] = [],
  81:   config: AttackEnvelopeConfigV2 = DEFAULT_ATTACK_ENVELOPE_CONFIG_V2
  82: ): { isInside: boolean; dx: number; dy: number; reason?: string } {
  83:   const dx = Math.abs(botPos.x - playerPos.x);
  84:   const dy = botPos.y - playerPos.y; // Positive if bot is below player (larger Y)
  85: 
  86:   if (dx > config.envelopeHalfWidthPx) {
  87:     return { isInside: false, dx, dy, reason: `Horizontal distance ${dx.toFixed(1)}px exceeds half-width ${config.envelopeHalfWidthPx}px` };
  88:   }
  89: 
  90:   if (dy < config.envelopeMinBelowPx || dy > config.envelopeMaxBelowPx) {
  91:     return { isInside: false, dx, dy, reason: `Vertical distance ${dy.toFixed(1)}px outside range [${config.envelopeMinBelowPx}, ${config.envelopeMaxBelowPx}]` };
  92:   }
  93: 
  94:   // Check vertical platform blockage directly between bot and player target zone
  95:   if (inflatedObstacles.length > 0) {
  96:     const minX = Math.min(botPos.x, playerPos.x) - 4;
  97:     const maxX = Math.max(botPos.x, playerPos.x) + 4;
  98:     const minY = Math.min(botPos.y, playerPos.y);
  99:     const maxY = Math.max(botPos.y, playerPos.y);
 100: 
 101:     for (const obs of inflatedObstacles) {
 102:       if (
 103:         obs.left < maxX &&
 104:         obs.right > minX &&
 105:         obs.top < maxY &&
 106:         obs.bottom > minY
 107:       ) {
 108:         return { isInside: false, dx, dy, reason: `Platform obstacle blocks direct attack envelope line of sight` };
 109:       }
 110:     }
 111:   }
 112: 
 113:   return { isInside: true, dx, dy };
 114: }

FILE: src/games/circuit-climb/bot-ai-v2/BotSensingV2.ts
================================================================================
   1: import { BotWorldSnapshotV2 } from './BotTypesV2';
   2: import { BOT_PROFILES_V2, BOT_CONFIG_V2 } from './BotConfigV2';
   3: 
   4: export function getEdgeGap(snapshot: BotWorldSnapshotV2): number {
   5:   const dx = snapshot.playerPosition.x - snapshot.botPosition.x;
   6:   const dy = snapshot.playerPosition.y - snapshot.botPosition.y;
   7:   const centerDist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
   8:   return centerDist - snapshot.botRadius - snapshot.playerRadius;
   9: }
  10: 
  11: export function checkNearSensor(snapshot: BotWorldSnapshotV2): boolean {
  12:   const profile = BOT_PROFILES_V2[snapshot.difficulty] || BOT_PROFILES_V2.NORMAL;
  13:   const edgeGap = getEdgeGap(snapshot);
  14:   return edgeGap <= profile.nearDetectionGapPx;
  15: }
  16: 
  17: export function checkRadarSensor(snapshot: BotWorldSnapshotV2, radarTimerMs: number): boolean {
  18:   const profile = BOT_PROFILES_V2[snapshot.difficulty] || BOT_PROFILES_V2.NORMAL;
  19:   const cycleTime = radarTimerMs % profile.radarPeriodMs;
  20:   
  21:   if (cycleTime > BOT_CONFIG_V2.radarDurationMs) {
  22:     return false;
  23:   }
  24:   
  25:   const progress = cycleTime / BOT_CONFIG_V2.radarDurationMs;
  26:   const currentRadarRadius = progress * profile.radarMaxRadiusPx;
  27:   
  28:   const edgeGap = getEdgeGap(snapshot);
  29:   
  30:   // A hit occurs if the radar wave overlaps the gap
  31:   // Actually, radar is drawn as a ring of some thickness.
  32:   // We'll say it hits if the ring touches the player physical bounds.
  33:   // Center distance is bot to player center. 
  34:   // Radar radius is from bot center.
  35:   // Hit if: currentRadarRadius >= (centerDist - playerRadius) AND currentRadarRadius <= (centerDist + playerRadius)
  36:   // which is equivalent to edgeGap <= currentRadarRadius - botRadius?
  37:   
  38:   const dx = snapshot.playerPosition.x - snapshot.botPosition.x;
  39:   const dy = snapshot.playerPosition.y - snapshot.botPosition.y;
  40:   const centerDist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
  41:   
  42:   const playerNearEdge = centerDist - snapshot.playerRadius;
  43:   const playerFarEdge = centerDist + snapshot.playerRadius;
  44:   
  45:   // Check if currentRadarRadius is within the player's bounds, plus some thickness
  46:   const radarMin = currentRadarRadius - BOT_CONFIG_V2.radarWaveThicknessPx / 2;
  47:   const radarMax = currentRadarRadius + BOT_CONFIG_V2.radarWaveThicknessPx / 2;
  48:   
  49:   return radarMax >= playerNearEdge && radarMin <= playerFarEdge;
  50: }


================================================================================
FILE: src/games/circuit-climb/bot-ai-v2/BotAwarenessV2.ts
================================================================================
   1: import { AwarenessEpisodeV2, BotWorldSnapshotV2, BotStateContextV2 } from './BotTypesV2';
   2: import { BOT_PROFILES_V2 } from './BotConfigV2';
   3: import { checkNearSensor, checkRadarSensor, getEdgeGap } from './BotSensingV2';
   4: import { BotFlightRecorder } from './BotFlightRecorderV2';
   5: 
   6: let nextEpisodeId = 1;
   7: 
   8: export function updateAwareness(snapshot: BotWorldSnapshotV2, context: BotStateContextV2): void {
   9:   const recorder = BotFlightRecorder.getInstance();
  10:   const profile = BOT_PROFILES_V2[snapshot.difficulty] || BOT_PROFILES_V2.NORMAL;
  11: 
  12:   const isNear = checkNearSensor(snapshot);
  13:   const isRadar = checkRadarSensor(snapshot, context.radarTimerMs);
  14:   const detected = isNear || isRadar;
  15: 
  16:   const edgeGap = getEdgeGap(snapshot);
  17:   const currentPulseId = Math.floor(context.radarTimerMs / profile.radarPeriodMs);
  18:   const cycleTime = context.radarTimerMs % profile.radarPeriodMs;
  19:   const isRadarActive = cycleTime <= 680; // radar duration is 680ms
  20: 
  21:   // Detect and log radar pulse started
  22:   if (typeof (context as any)._lastPulseId === 'undefined' || (context as any)._lastPulseId !== currentPulseId) {
  23:     (context as any)._lastPulseId = currentPulseId;
  24:     recorder.recordEvent(
  25:       snapshot.simTimeMs,
  26:       'GREENFIELD_V2',
  27:       context.currentState,
  28:       'NONE',
  29:       'SENSING',
  30:       'RADAR_PULSE_STARTED',
  31:       `Radar wave initiated. Period: ${profile.radarPeriodMs}ms. Max Radius: ${profile.radarMaxRadiusPx}px`,
  32:       snapshot.botPosition,
  33:       snapshot.playerPosition,
  34:       snapshot.botRadius,
  35:       snapshot.playerRadius,
  36:       context.awareness?.id || 0,
  37:       snapshot.obstacleRevision,
  38:       context.debug.plannerStatus,
  39:       context.pathIndex,
  40:       { pulseId: currentPulseId, maxRadius: profile.radarMaxRadiusPx }
  41:     );
  42:   }
  43: 
  44:   // Throttle sampling logs to avoid ring buffer flood (every ~300ms)
  45:   const shouldLogSample = snapshot.simTimeMs % 300 < snapshot.deltaMs;
  46: 
  47:   if (shouldLogSample) {
  48:     recorder.recordEvent(
  49:       snapshot.simTimeMs,
  50:       'GREENFIELD_V2',
  51:       context.currentState,
  52:       'NONE',
  53:       'SENSING',
  54:       'NEAR_SENSOR_CHECK_SAMPLE',
  55:       `Checking near sensor. Gap: ${edgeGap.toFixed(1)} px. Limit: ${profile.nearDetectionGapPx} px`,
  56:       snapshot.botPosition,
  57:       snapshot.playerPosition,
  58:       snapshot.botRadius,
  59:       snapshot.playerRadius,
  60:       context.awareness?.id || 0,
  61:       snapshot.obstacleRevision,
  62:       context.debug.plannerStatus,
  63:       context.pathIndex,
  64:       { isNear, edgeGap }
  65:     );
  66: 
  67:     if (isRadarActive) {
  68:       const progress = cycleTime / 680;
  69:       const currentRadius = progress * profile.radarMaxRadiusPx;
  70:       const dx = snapshot.playerPosition.x - snapshot.botPosition.x;
  71:       const dy = snapshot.playerPosition.y - snapshot.botPosition.y;
  72:       const playerDist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
  73:       recorder.recordEvent(
  74:         snapshot.simTimeMs,
  75:         'GREENFIELD_V2',
  76:         context.currentState,
  77:         'NONE',
  78:         'SENSING',
  79:         'RADAR_EXPANSION_SAMPLE',
  80:         `Radar wave expanding. Radius: ${currentRadius.toFixed(1)} px. Player distance: ${playerDist.toFixed(1)} px`,
  81:         snapshot.botPosition,
  82:         snapshot.playerPosition,
  83:         snapshot.botRadius,
  84:         snapshot.playerRadius,
  85:         context.awareness?.id || 0,
  86:         snapshot.obstacleRevision,
  87:         context.debug.plannerStatus,
  88:         context.pathIndex,
  89:         { currentRadius, playerDist }
  90:       );
  91:     }
  92:   }
  93: 
  94:   // Specific hits
  95:   if (isNear) {
  96:     recorder.recordEvent(
  97:       snapshot.simTimeMs,
  98:       'GREENFIELD_V2',
  99:       context.currentState,
 100:       'NONE',
 101:       'SENSING',
 102:       'NEAR_SENSOR_HIT',
 103:       `Near sensor HIT! Gap ${edgeGap.toFixed(1)} px is within near limit ${profile.nearDetectionGapPx} px`,
 104:       snapshot.botPosition,
 105:       snapshot.playerPosition,
 106:       snapshot.botRadius,
 107:       snapshot.playerRadius,
 108:       context.awareness?.id || 0,
 109:       snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 110:       context.debug.plannerStatus,
 111:       context.pathIndex,
 112:       { edgeGap }
 113:     );
 114:   }
 115: 
 116:   if (isRadar) {
 117:     recorder.recordEvent(
 118:       snapshot.simTimeMs,
 119:       'GREENFIELD_V2',
 120:       context.currentState,
 121:       'NONE',
 122:       'SENSING',
 123:       'RADAR_SENSOR_HIT',
 124:       `Radar wave HIT player! Overlaps at current wave front`,
 125:       snapshot.botPosition,
 126:       snapshot.playerPosition,
 127:       snapshot.botRadius,
 128:       snapshot.playerRadius,
 129:       context.awareness?.id || 0,
 130:       snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 131:       context.debug.plannerStatus,
 132:       context.pathIndex,
 133:       { radarTimerMs: context.radarTimerMs }
 134:     );
 135:   }
 136: 
 137:   // Crossing detection (did wave radius cross player near edge?)
 138:   const dx = snapshot.playerPosition.x - snapshot.botPosition.x;
 139:   const dy = snapshot.playerPosition.y - snapshot.botPosition.y;
 140:   const playerDist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
 141:   const playerNearEdge = playerDist - snapshot.playerRadius;
 142:   const currentRadius = isRadarActive ? (cycleTime / 680) * profile.radarMaxRadiusPx : 0;
 143:   if (isRadarActive && currentRadius >= playerNearEdge) {
 144:     if (typeof (context as any)._lastPulseCrossedId === 'undefined' || (context as any)._lastPulseCrossedId !== currentPulseId) {
 145:       (context as any)._lastPulseCrossedId = currentPulseId;
 146:       recorder.recordEvent(
 147:         snapshot.simTimeMs,
 148:         'GREENFIELD_V2',
 149:         context.currentState,
 150:         'NONE',
 151:         'SENSING',
 152:         'RADAR_WAVE_CROSSED_PLAYER',
 153:         `Radar wave front (${currentRadius.toFixed(1)}px) crossed player's near edge (${playerNearEdge.toFixed(1)}px)`,
 154:         snapshot.botPosition,
 155:         snapshot.playerPosition,
 156:         snapshot.botRadius,
 157:         snapshot.playerRadius,
 158:         context.awareness?.id || 0,
 159:         snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 160:         context.debug.plannerStatus,
 161:         context.pathIndex,
 162:         { currentRadius, playerNearEdge }
 163:       );
 164:     }
 165:   }
 166: 
 167:   if (detected) {
 168:     if (!context.awareness) {
 169:       context.awareness = {
 170:         id: nextEpisodeId++,
 171:         openedAtMs: snapshot.simTimeMs,
 172:         lastConfirmedAtMs: snapshot.simTimeMs,
 173:         lastKnownPlayerPosition: { ...snapshot.playerPosition },
 174:         excitementPlayed: false
 175:       };
 176:       
 177:       recorder.recordEvent(
 178:         snapshot.simTimeMs,
 179:         'GREENFIELD_V2',
 180:         context.currentState,
 181:         'NONE',
 182:         'AWARENESS',
 183:         'AWARENESS_EPISODE_OPENED',
 184:         `Sensing confirmed. Opening awareness episode #${context.awareness.id}`,
 185:         snapshot.botPosition,
 186:         snapshot.playerPosition,
 187:         snapshot.botRadius,
 188:         snapshot.playerRadius,
 189:         context.awareness.id,
 190:         snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 191:         context.debug.plannerStatus,
 192:         context.pathIndex
 193:       );
 194:     } else {
 195:       context.awareness.lastConfirmedAtMs = snapshot.simTimeMs;
 196:       context.awareness.lastKnownPlayerPosition = { ...snapshot.playerPosition };
 197:     }
 198:   } else if (context.awareness) {
 199:     // Keep lastKnownPlayerPosition fresh during active pursuit/alert states
 200:     if (context.currentState === 'PURSUE' || context.currentState === 'FINAL_APPROACH' || context.currentState === 'ALERT') {
 201:       context.awareness.lastKnownPlayerPosition = { ...snapshot.playerPosition };
 202:     }
 203:   }
 204: 
 205:   // Check expiration
 206:   if (context.awareness) {
 207:     const timeSinceConfirmed = snapshot.simTimeMs - context.awareness.lastConfirmedAtMs;
 208:     
 209:     if (timeSinceConfirmed > profile.awarenessMemoryMs) {
 210:       if (edgeGap > profile.radarMaxRadiusPx) {
 211:         const expiredId = context.awareness.id;
 212:         context.awareness = null;
 213:         
 214:         recorder.recordEvent(
 215:           snapshot.simTimeMs,
 216:           'GREENFIELD_V2',
 217:           context.currentState,
 218:           'NONE',
 219:           'AWARENESS',
 220:           'AWARENESS_EPISODE_EXPIRED',
 221:           `No sensor confirmation for ${timeSinceConfirmed}ms and gap ${edgeGap.toFixed(1)}px is beyond radar radius ${profile.radarMaxRadiusPx}px. Closed episode #${expiredId}`,
 222:           snapshot.botPosition,
 223:           snapshot.playerPosition,
 224:           snapshot.botRadius,
 225:           snapshot.playerRadius,
 226:           0,
 227:           snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 228:           context.debug.plannerStatus,
 229:           context.pathIndex
 230:         );
 231:       }
 232:     }
 233:   }
 234: }


================================================================================
FILE: src/games/circuit-climb/bot-ai-v2/BotGoalSelectorV2.ts
================================================================================
   1: import { BotWorldSnapshotV2, Vec2, Rect, BotGoalV2, isBotInsideAttackEnvelope, DEFAULT_ATTACK_ENVELOPE_CONFIG_V2 } from './BotTypesV2';
   2: import { BOT_CONFIG_V2 } from './BotConfigV2';
   3: import { BotFlightRecorder } from './BotFlightRecorderV2';
   4: 
   5: function isPointInRect(pt: Vec2, r: Rect) {
   6:   return pt.x >= r.left && pt.x <= r.right && pt.y >= r.top && pt.y <= r.bottom;
   7: }
   8: 
   9: export function isPointInPlayerTransitCorridor(pt: Vec2, snapshot: BotWorldSnapshotV2): boolean {
  10:   if (snapshot.playerMovementState !== 'IN_TRANSIT' && snapshot.playerMovementState !== 'MOVE_STARTED' && snapshot.playerMovementState !== 'WRONG_RETURN') {
  11:     return false;
  12:   }
  13:   
  14:   if (!snapshot.playerRouteStartPosition || !snapshot.playerRouteDestination) {
  15:     return false;
  16:   }
  17:   
  18:   const xMin = Math.min(snapshot.playerRouteStartPosition.x, snapshot.playerRouteDestination.x) - 12;
  19:   const xMax = Math.max(snapshot.playerRouteStartPosition.x, snapshot.playerRouteDestination.x) + 12;
  20:   const yMin = Math.min(snapshot.playerRouteStartPosition.y, snapshot.playerRouteDestination.y) - 60;
  21:   const yMax = Math.max(snapshot.playerRouteStartPosition.y, snapshot.playerRouteDestination.y) + 60;
  22:   
  23:   return pt.x >= xMin && pt.x <= xMax && pt.y >= yMin && pt.y <= yMax;
  24: }
  25: 
  26: export function isCellBlocked(
  27:   cellX: number, 
  28:   cellY: number, 
  29:   inflatedObstacles: Rect[], 
  30:   navBounds: Rect,
  31:   snapshot?: BotWorldSnapshotV2
  32: ): boolean {
  33:   const pt = { x: cellX, y: cellY };
  34:   if (!isPointInRect(pt, navBounds)) return true;
  35:   for (const obs of inflatedObstacles) {
  36:     if (isPointInRect(pt, obs)) return true;
  37:   }
  38:   if (snapshot && isPointInPlayerTransitCorridor(pt, snapshot)) {
  39:     return true;
  40:   }
  41:   return false;
  42: }
  43: 
  44: export function snapToGrid(val: number): number {
  45:   return Math.round(val / BOT_CONFIG_V2.gridSize) * BOT_CONFIG_V2.gridSize;
  46: }
  47: 
  48: export function getSemanticGoalCandidatesV2(
  49:   snapshot: BotWorldSnapshotV2,
  50:   botPos: Vec2,
  51:   playerPos: Vec2,
  52:   inflatedObstacles: Rect[],
  53:   targetVersion: number,
  54:   recentCells: string[] = []
  55: ): BotGoalV2[] {
  56:   const recorder = BotFlightRecorder.getInstance();
  57:   const goals: BotGoalV2[] = [];
  58:   const gs = BOT_CONFIG_V2.gridSize;
  59: 
  60:   const botCell = { x: snapToGrid(botPos.x), y: snapToGrid(botPos.y) };
  61:   const targetEnvelopeCenter = {
  62:     x: playerPos.x,
  63:     y: playerPos.y + DEFAULT_ATTACK_ENVELOPE_CONFIG_V2.envelopeTargetBelowPx
  64:   };
  65:   const botDistToEnvelopeCenter = Math.hypot(botPos.x - targetEnvelopeCenter.x, botPos.y - targetEnvelopeCenter.y);
  66: 
  67:   // Helper to add goal with start cell check
  68:   const tryAddGoal = (
  69:     cell: Vec2,
  70:     purpose: BotGoalV2['purpose'],
  71:     validForNextState: boolean
  72:   ) => {
  73:     // Check basic cell blockage (by platform/bounds)
  74:     if (isCellBlocked(cell.x, cell.y, inflatedObstacles, snapshot.navigationBounds)) {
  75:       return;
  76:     }
  77: 
  78:     const isStartCell = cell.x === botCell.x && cell.y === botCell.y;
  79:     const insideEnvelope = isBotInsideAttackEnvelope(botPos, playerPos, inflatedObstacles).isInside;
  80: 
  81:     if (isStartCell) {
  82:       if (purpose === 'ATTACK_READY' && insideEnvelope) {
  83:         // Valid attack ready goal at start cell!
  84:         const goalId = `${purpose}_${cell.x}_${cell.y}_v${targetVersion}`;
  85:         goals.push({
  86:           id: goalId,
  87:           cell,
  88:           worldPosition: { x: cell.x, y: cell.y },
  89:           purpose,
  90:           targetVersion,
  91:           validForNextState: true,
  92:           progressTowardEnvelopePx: 0
  93:         });
  94:         recorder.recordEvent(
  95:           snapshot.simTimeMs,
  96:           'GREENFIELD_V2',
  97:           'PURSUE',
  98:           'NONE',
  99:           'GOAL_SELECTION',
 100:           'GOAL_CANDIDATE_ACCEPTED',
 101:           `Start cell (${cell.x}, ${cell.y}) accepted as ATTACK_READY because bot is inside attack envelope.`,
 102:           botPos,
 103:           playerPos,
 104:           snapshot.botRadius,
 105:           snapshot.playerRadius,
 106:           0,
 107:           snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 108:           '',
 109:           0,
 110:           { goalId }
 111:         );
 112:       } else {
 113:         // Start cell rejected because semantic predicate is false!
 114:         recorder.recordEvent(
 115:           snapshot.simTimeMs,
 116:           'GREENFIELD_V2',
 117:           'PURSUE',
 118:           'NONE',
 119:           'GOAL_SELECTION',
 120:           'GOAL_CANDIDATE_REJECTED',
 121:           `Start cell (${cell.x}, ${cell.y}) rejected for purpose ${purpose}. Reason: CURRENT_CELL_NO_PROGRESS (inside envelope: ${insideEnvelope})`,
 122:           botPos,
 123:           playerPos,
 124:           snapshot.botRadius,
 125:           snapshot.playerRadius,
 126:           0,
 127:           snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 128:           '',
 129:           0,
 130:           { cell, purpose, reason: 'CURRENT_CELL_NO_PROGRESS' }
 131:         );
 132:       }
 133:       return;
 134:     }
 135: 
 136:     const goalDistToCenter = Math.hypot(cell.x - targetEnvelopeCenter.x, cell.y - targetEnvelopeCenter.y);
 137:     const progressTowardEnvelopePx = botDistToEnvelopeCenter - goalDistToCenter;
 138: 
 139:     // For approach goals, require measurable progress toward envelope
 140:     if (purpose === 'APPROACH_PROGRESS' && progressTowardEnvelopePx < gs) {
 141:       return;
 142:     }
 143: 
 144:     const goalId = `${purpose}_${cell.x}_${cell.y}_v${targetVersion}`;
 145:     goals.push({
 146:       id: goalId,
 147:       cell,
 148:       worldPosition: { x: cell.x, y: cell.y },
 149:       purpose,
 150:       targetVersion,
 151:       validForNextState,
 152:       progressTowardEnvelopePx
 153:     });
 154:   };
 155: 
 156:   // 1. Generate ATTACK_READY candidates inside envelope
 157:   const minEnvX = snapToGrid(playerPos.x - DEFAULT_ATTACK_ENVELOPE_CONFIG_V2.envelopeHalfWidthPx);
 158:   const maxEnvX = snapToGrid(playerPos.x + DEFAULT_ATTACK_ENVELOPE_CONFIG_V2.envelopeHalfWidthPx);
 159:   const minEnvY = snapToGrid(playerPos.y + DEFAULT_ATTACK_ENVELOPE_CONFIG_V2.envelopeMinBelowPx);
 160:   const maxEnvY = snapToGrid(playerPos.y + DEFAULT_ATTACK_ENVELOPE_CONFIG_V2.envelopeMaxBelowPx);
 161: 
 162:   for (let x = minEnvX; x <= maxEnvX; x += gs) {
 163:     for (let y = minEnvY; y <= maxEnvY; y += gs) {
 164:       tryAddGoal({ x, y }, 'ATTACK_READY', true);
 165:     }
 166:   }
 167: 
 168:   // 2. Generate APPROACH_PROGRESS candidates in rings around target envelope center
 169:   const targetX = snapToGrid(targetEnvelopeCenter.x);
 170:   const targetY = snapToGrid(targetEnvelopeCenter.y);
 171: 
 172:   for (let r = 0; r <= 8; r++) {
 173:     for (let dx = -r; dx <= r; dx++) {
 174:       for (let dy = -r; dy <= r; dy++) {
 175:         if (Math.abs(dx) === r || Math.abs(dy) === r || r === 0) {
 176:           const cx = targetX + dx * gs;
 177:           const cy = targetY + dy * gs;
 178:           tryAddGoal({ x: cx, y: cy }, 'APPROACH_PROGRESS', false);
 179:         }
 180:       }
 181:     }
 182:     if (goals.filter(g => g.purpose === 'APPROACH_PROGRESS').length >= 12) break;
 183:   }
 184: 
 185:   // Deduplicate goals by ID
 186:   const uniqueGoals: BotGoalV2[] = [];
 187:   const seenIds = new Set<string>();
 188:   for (const g of goals) {
 189:     if (!seenIds.has(g.id)) {
 190:       seenIds.add(g.id);
 191:       uniqueGoals.push(g);
 192:     }
 193:   }
 194: 
 195:   return uniqueGoals;
 196: }
 197: 
 198: export function getBelowPlayerAnchor(target: Vec2, snapshot: BotWorldSnapshotV2, inflatedObstacles: Rect[]): Vec2 {
 199:   const anchorX = target.x;
 200:   const anchorY = target.y + (snapshot.botBaseOffsetRows * snapshot.rowGap);
 201:   
 202:   let testX = snapToGrid(anchorX);
 203:   let testY = snapToGrid(anchorY);
 204:   
 205:   if (!isCellBlocked(testX, testY, inflatedObstacles, snapshot.navigationBounds, snapshot)) {
 206:     return { x: testX, y: testY };
 207:   }
 208:   
 209:   const gs = BOT_CONFIG_V2.gridSize;
 210:   const maxSearchRings = 10;
 211:   for (let r = 1; r <= maxSearchRings; r++) {
 212:     const steps = [
 213:       { dx: 0, dy: r * gs },
 214:       { dx: 0, dy: -r * gs },
 215:       { dx: -r * gs, dy: 0 },
 216:       { dx: r * gs, dy: 0 },
 217:       { dx: -r * gs, dy: r * gs },
 218:       { dx: r * gs, dy: r * gs },
 219:       { dx: -r * gs, dy: -r * gs },
 220:       { dx: r * gs, dy: -r * gs }
 221:     ];
 222:     for (const step of steps) {
 223:       const cx = testX + step.dx;
 224:       const cy = testY + step.dy;
 225:       if (!isCellBlocked(cx, cy, inflatedObstacles, snapshot.navigationBounds, snapshot)) {
 226:         return { x: cx, y: cy };
 227:       }
 228:     }
 229:   }
 230:   
 231:   return { x: testX, y: testY };
 232: }
 233: 
 234: export function getGoalCandidates(snapshot: BotWorldSnapshotV2, targetPos: Vec2, inflatedObstacles: Rect[]): Vec2[] {
 235:   const candidates: Vec2[] = [];
 236:   const startX = snapToGrid(targetPos.x);
 237:   const startY = snapToGrid(targetPos.y);
 238:   
 239:   const MAX_RINGS = 6;
 240:   const gs = BOT_CONFIG_V2.gridSize;
 241: 
 242:   for (let r = 1; r <= MAX_RINGS; r++) {
 243:     // Generate deterministic ring cells
 244:     for (let dx = -r; dx <= r; dx++) {
 245:       const topCell = { x: startX + dx * gs, y: startY - r * gs };
 246:       const bottomCell = { x: startX + dx * gs, y: startY + r * gs };
 247:       
 248:       [topCell, bottomCell].forEach(c => {
 249:         // Filter out by transit corridor protection
 250:         if (isPointInPlayerTransitCorridor(c, snapshot)) {
 251:           BotFlightRecorder.getInstance().recordEvent(
 252:             snapshot.simTimeMs,
 253:             'GREENFIELD_V2',
 254:             'CHASE',
 255:             'NONE',
 256:             'GOAL_SELECTION',
 257:             'PLAN_NODE_FILTERED_BY_TRANSIT_PROTECTION',
 258:             `Goal candidate at (${c.x}, ${c.y}) filtered by transit protection. Corridor limits: X[${(Math.min(snapshot.playerRouteStartPosition!.x, snapshot.playerRouteDestination!.x) - 12).toFixed(0)} - ${(Math.max(snapshot.playerRouteStartPosition!.x, snapshot.playerRouteDestination!.x) + 12).toFixed(0)}], Y[${(Math.min(snapshot.playerRouteStartPosition!.y, snapshot.playerRouteDestination!.y) - 60).toFixed(0)} - ${(Math.max(snapshot.playerRouteStartPosition!.y, snapshot.playerRouteDestination!.y) + 60).toFixed(0)}]. Player state: ${snapshot.playerMovementState}`,
 259:             snapshot.botPosition,
 260:             snapshot.playerPosition,
 261:             snapshot.botRadius,
 262:             snapshot.playerRadius,
 263:             0,
 264:             snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 265:             '',
 266:             0,
 267:             { coordinate: c, corridor: { xMin: Math.min(snapshot.playerRouteStartPosition!.x, snapshot.playerRouteDestination!.x) - 12, xMax: Math.max(snapshot.playerRouteStartPosition!.x, snapshot.playerRouteDestination!.x) + 12, yMin: Math.min(snapshot.playerRouteStartPosition!.y, snapshot.playerRouteDestination!.y) - 60, yMax: Math.max(snapshot.playerRouteStartPosition!.y, snapshot.playerRouteDestination!.y) + 60 } }
 268:           );
 269:           return;
 270:         }
 271: 
 272:         // Filter out lateral camping candidates
 273:         if (Math.abs(c.y - snapshot.playerPosition.y) < snapshot.rowGap * 0.85) {
 274:           return;
 275:         }
 276: 
 277:         if (!isCellBlocked(c.x, c.y, inflatedObstacles, snapshot.navigationBounds, snapshot)) {
 278:           candidates.push(c);
 279:         }
 280:       });
 281:     }
 282: 
 283:     for (let dy = -r + 1; dy <= r - 1; dy++) {
 284:       const leftCell = { x: startX - r * gs, y: startY + dy * gs };
 285:       const rightCell = { x: startX + r * gs, y: startY + dy * gs };
 286:       
 287:       [leftCell, rightCell].forEach(c => {
 288:         // Filter out by transit corridor protection
 289:         if (isPointInPlayerTransitCorridor(c, snapshot)) {
 290:           BotFlightRecorder.getInstance().recordEvent(
 291:             snapshot.simTimeMs,
 292:             'GREENFIELD_V2',
 293:             'CHASE',
 294:             'NONE',
 295:             'GOAL_SELECTION',
 296:             'PLAN_NODE_FILTERED_BY_TRANSIT_PROTECTION',
 297:             `Goal candidate at (${c.x}, ${c.y}) filtered by transit protection. Corridor limits: X[${(Math.min(snapshot.playerRouteStartPosition!.x, snapshot.playerRouteDestination!.x) - 12).toFixed(0)} - ${(Math.max(snapshot.playerRouteStartPosition!.x, snapshot.playerRouteDestination!.x) + 12).toFixed(0)}], Y[${(Math.min(snapshot.playerRouteStartPosition!.y, snapshot.playerRouteDestination!.y) - 60).toFixed(0)} - ${(Math.max(snapshot.playerRouteStartPosition!.y, snapshot.playerRouteDestination!.y) + 60).toFixed(0)}]. Player state: ${snapshot.playerMovementState}`,
 298:             snapshot.botPosition,
 299:             snapshot.playerPosition,
 300:             snapshot.botRadius,
 301:             snapshot.playerRadius,
 302:             0,
 303:             snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 304:             '',
 305:             0,
 306:             { coordinate: c, corridor: { xMin: Math.min(snapshot.playerRouteStartPosition!.x, snapshot.playerRouteDestination!.x) - 12, xMax: Math.max(snapshot.playerRouteStartPosition!.x, snapshot.playerRouteDestination!.x) + 12, yMin: Math.min(snapshot.playerRouteStartPosition!.y, snapshot.playerRouteDestination!.y) - 60, yMax: Math.max(snapshot.playerRouteStartPosition!.y, snapshot.playerRouteDestination!.y) + 60 } }
 307:           );
 308:           return;
 309:         }
 310: 
 311:         // Filter out lateral camping candidates
 312:         if (Math.abs(c.y - snapshot.playerPosition.y) < snapshot.rowGap * 0.85) {
 313:           return;
 314:         }
 315: 
 316:         if (!isCellBlocked(c.x, c.y, inflatedObstacles, snapshot.navigationBounds, snapshot)) {
 317:           candidates.push(c);
 318:         }
 319:       });
 320:     }
 321:     
 322:     if (candidates.length >= 8) {
 323:       break;
 324:     }
 325:   }
 326: 
 327:   // Sort by deterministic distance to targetPos
 328:   candidates.sort((a, b) => {
 329:     const da = Math.abs(a.x - targetPos.x) + Math.abs(a.y - targetPos.y);
 330:     const db = Math.abs(b.x - targetPos.x) + Math.abs(b.y - targetPos.y);
 331:     if (da !== db) return da - db;
 332:     if (a.x !== b.x) return a.x - b.x;
 333:     return a.y - b.y;
 334:   });
 335: 
 336:   const unique: Vec2[] = [];
 337:   const seen = new Set<string>();
 338:   for (const c of candidates) {
 339:     const key = `${c.x},${c.y}`;
 340:     if (!seen.has(key)) {
 341:       seen.add(key);
 342:       unique.push(c);
 343:       if (unique.length >= 8) break;
 344:     }
 345:   }
 346: 
 347:   return unique;
 348: }


================================================================================
FILE: src/games/circuit-climb/bot-ai-v2/BotPlannerV2.ts
================================================================================
   1: import { Vec2, Rect, BotWorldSnapshotV2, BotGoalV2, BotPlanResultV2, isBotInsideAttackEnvelope } from './BotTypesV2';
   2: import { BOT_CONFIG_V2 } from './BotConfigV2';
   3: import { isCellBlocked, snapToGrid, isPointInPlayerTransitCorridor } from './BotGoalSelectorV2';
   4: 
   5: interface AStarNode {
   6:   x: number;
   7:   y: number;
   8:   g: number;
   9:   h: number;
  10:   f: number;
  11:   parent: AStarNode | null;
  12: }
  13: 
  14: function heuristic(a: Vec2, b: Vec2) {
  15:   return Math.abs(a.x - b.x) + Math.abs(a.y - b.y);
  16: }
  17: 
  18: export interface PlannerResult {
  19:   path: Vec2[] | null;
  20:   type: 'FULL' | 'PARTIAL' | null;
  21:   nodesExpanded: number;
  22:   selectedGoal: Vec2 | null;
  23: }
  24: 
  25: export function planPathV2Simplified(
  26:   start: Vec2,
  27:   goals: BotGoalV2[],
  28:   inflatedObstacles: Rect[],
  29:   navBounds: Rect,
  30:   searchBounds: Rect,
  31:   maxNodes: number,
  32:   snapshot: BotWorldSnapshotV2
  33: ): BotPlanResultV2 {
  34:   if (goals.length === 0) {
  35:     return {
  36:       outcome: 'UNREACHABLE',
  37:       path: null,
  38:       selectedGoal: null,
  39:       nodesExpanded: 0,
  40:       progressPx: 0,
  41:       reason: 'NO_GOALS_PROVIDED'
  42:     };
  43:   }
  44: 
  45:   const startX = snapToGrid(start.x);
  46:   const startY = snapToGrid(start.y);
  47: 
  48:   if (isCellBlocked(startX, startY, inflatedObstacles, navBounds)) {
  49:     return {
  50:       outcome: 'UNREACHABLE',
  51:       path: null,
  52:       selectedGoal: null,
  53:       nodesExpanded: 0,
  54:       progressPx: 0,
  55:       reason: 'START_CELL_BLOCKED'
  56:     };
  57:   }
  58: 
  59:   const openList: AStarNode[] = [];
  60:   const closedSet = new Set<string>();
  61:   const gs = BOT_CONFIG_V2.gridSize;
  62: 
  63:   const getMinH = (pt: Vec2) => Math.min(...goals.map(g => heuristic(pt, g.cell)));
  64: 
  65:   const startNode: AStarNode = {
  66:     x: startX,
  67:     y: startY,
  68:     g: 0,
  69:     h: getMinH({ x: startX, y: startY }),
  70:     f: 0,
  71:     parent: null
  72:   };
  73:   startNode.f = startNode.h;
  74:   openList.push(startNode);
  75: 
  76:   let nodesExpanded = 0;
  77:   let bestPartial: AStarNode = startNode;
  78:   let bestGoal: BotGoalV2 | null = goals[0] || null;
  79: 
  80:   while (openList.length > 0 && nodesExpanded < maxNodes) {
  81:     let bestIdx = 0;
  82:     for (let i = 1; i < openList.length; i++) {
  83:       if (openList[i].f < openList[bestIdx].f) bestIdx = i;
  84:       else if (openList[i].f === openList[bestIdx].f && openList[i].h < openList[bestIdx].h) bestIdx = i;
  85:     }
  86:     const current = openList.splice(bestIdx, 1)[0];
  87:     const key = `${current.x},${current.y}`;
  88: 
  89:     if (closedSet.has(key)) continue;
  90:     closedSet.add(key);
  91:     nodesExpanded++;
  92: 
  93:     // Check if goal reached
  94:     const reachedGoal = goals.find(g => g.cell.x === current.x && g.cell.y === current.y);
  95:     if (reachedGoal) {
  96:       const isStartCell = current.x === startX && current.y === startY;
  97:       const insideEnvelope = isBotInsideAttackEnvelope(start, snapshot.playerPosition, inflatedObstacles).isInside;
  98: 
  99:       if (isStartCell && reachedGoal.purpose !== 'ATTACK_READY') {
 100:         // Start cell match for non-attack goal => NOT a valid success!
 101:         // Continue search without returning 1-node path
 102:       } else if (isStartCell && reachedGoal.purpose === 'ATTACK_READY' && !insideEnvelope) {
 103:         // Start cell match for attack ready but predicate is false => NOT a valid success!
 104:         // Continue search without returning 1-node path
 105:       } else {
 106:         const path: Vec2[] = [];
 107:         let curr: AStarNode | null = current;
 108:         while (curr) {
 109:           path.push({ x: curr.x, y: curr.y });
 110:           curr = curr.parent;
 111:         }
 112:         const outcome: BotPlanResultV2['outcome'] =
 113:           reachedGoal.purpose === 'ATTACK_READY'
 114:             ? 'REACHED_ATTACK_READY_GOAL'
 115:             : 'REACHED_APPROACH_PROGRESS_GOAL';
 116: 
 117:         return {
 118:           outcome,
 119:           path: path.reverse(),
 120:           selectedGoal: reachedGoal,
 121:           nodesExpanded,
 122:           progressPx: Math.hypot(current.x - startX, current.y - startY),
 123:           reason: `GOAL_REACHED_${reachedGoal.purpose}`
 124:         };
 125:       }
 126:     }
 127: 
 128:     // Track best partial node
 129:     const currentH = getMinH(current);
 130:     const bestPartialH = getMinH(bestPartial);
 131:     if (currentH < bestPartialH) {
 132:       bestPartial = current;
 133:       let bg = goals[0];
 134:       let bgh = heuristic(current, goals[0].cell);
 135:       for (const g of goals) {
 136:         const h = heuristic(current, g.cell);
 137:         if (h < bgh) { bgh = h; bg = g; }
 138:       }
 139:       bestGoal = bg;
 140:     }
 141: 
 142:     const dirs = [
 143:       { dx: 0, dy: -gs },
 144:       { dx: gs, dy: 0 },
 145:       { dx: 0, dy: gs },
 146:       { dx: -gs, dy: 0 }
 147:     ];
 148: 
 149:     for (const d of dirs) {
 150:       const nx = current.x + d.dx;
 151:       const ny = current.y + d.dy;
 152:       const nKey = `${nx},${ny}`;
 153: 
 154:       if (closedSet.has(nKey)) continue;
 155:       if (nx < searchBounds.left || nx > searchBounds.right || ny < searchBounds.top || ny > searchBounds.bottom) continue;
 156: 
 157:       if (isCellBlocked(nx, ny, inflatedObstacles, navBounds)) continue;
 158: 
 159:       let stepCost = gs;
 160:       // Soft transit corridor cost penalty
 161:       if (isPointInPlayerTransitCorridor({ x: nx, y: ny }, snapshot)) {
 162:         stepCost += BOT_CONFIG_V2.corridorSoftCost;
 163:       }
 164: 
 165:       const g = current.g + stepCost;
 166:       const h = getMinH({ x: nx, y: ny });
 167: 
 168:       openList.push({ x: nx, y: ny, g, h, f: g + h, parent: current });
 169:     }
 170:   }
 171: 
 172:   // Handle partial path if bestPartial is distinct from startNode
 173:   if (bestPartial !== startNode) {
 174:     const path: Vec2[] = [];
 175:     let curr: AStarNode | null = bestPartial;
 176:     while (curr) {
 177:       path.push({ x: curr.x, y: curr.y });
 178:       curr = curr.parent;
 179:     }
 180:     const pathRev = path.reverse();
 181:     const progressPx = Math.hypot(bestPartial.x - startX, bestPartial.y - startY);
 182: 
 183:     if (progressPx >= gs) {
 184:       return {
 185:         outcome: 'PARTIAL_PROGRESS',
 186:         path: pathRev,
 187:         selectedGoal: bestGoal,
 188:         nodesExpanded,
 189:         progressPx,
 190:         reason: 'BEST_PARTIAL_PATH_FOUND'
 191:       };
 192:     }
 193:   }
 194: 
 195:   return {
 196:     outcome: 'NO_PROGRESS',
 197:     path: null,
 198:     selectedGoal: null,
 199:     nodesExpanded,
 200:     progressPx: 0,
 201:     reason: 'SEARCH_EXHAUSTED_NO_PROGRESS'
 202:   };
 203: }
 204: 
 205: export function planPathV2(
 206:   start: Vec2,
 207:   goals: Vec2[],
 208:   inflatedObstacles: Rect[],
 209:   navBounds: Rect,
 210:   searchBounds: Rect,
 211:   maxNodes: number,
 212:   snapshot?: BotWorldSnapshotV2
 213: ): PlannerResult {
 214:   if (goals.length === 0) return { path: null, type: null, nodesExpanded: 0, selectedGoal: null };
 215: 
 216:   const startX = snapToGrid(start.x);
 217:   const startY = snapToGrid(start.y);
 218:   
 219:   if (isCellBlocked(startX, startY, inflatedObstacles, navBounds, snapshot)) {
 220:     // If start is blocked, try to find nearest unblocked neighbor or just fail
 221:     // For now we'll just fail to let recovery handle it
 222:     return { path: null, type: null, nodesExpanded: 0, selectedGoal: null };
 223:   }
 224: 
 225:   const openList: AStarNode[] = [];
 226:   const closedSet = new Set<string>();
 227:   const gs = BOT_CONFIG_V2.gridSize;
 228: 
 229:   const startNode: AStarNode = {
 230:     x: startX,
 231:     y: startY,
 232:     g: 0,
 233:     h: Math.min(...goals.map(g => heuristic({x: startX, y: startY}, g))),
 234:     f: 0,
 235:     parent: null
 236:   };
 237:   startNode.f = startNode.h;
 238:   openList.push(startNode);
 239: 
 240:   let nodesExpanded = 0;
 241:   let bestPartial: AStarNode = startNode;
 242:   let bestGoal: Vec2 = goals[0];
 243: 
 244:   while (openList.length > 0 && nodesExpanded < maxNodes) {
 245:     // Pop best
 246:     let bestIdx = 0;
 247:     for (let i = 1; i < openList.length; i++) {
 248:       if (openList[i].f < openList[bestIdx].f) bestIdx = i;
 249:       else if (openList[i].f === openList[bestIdx].f && openList[i].h < openList[bestIdx].h) bestIdx = i; // Tie break on h
 250:     }
 251:     const current = openList.splice(bestIdx, 1)[0];
 252:     const key = `${current.x},${current.y}`;
 253: 
 254:     if (closedSet.has(key)) continue;
 255:     closedSet.add(key);
 256:     nodesExpanded++;
 257: 
 258:     // Check if goal reached
 259:     const reachedGoal = goals.find(g => g.x === current.x && g.y === current.y);
 260:     if (reachedGoal) {
 261:       const path: Vec2[] = [];
 262:       let curr: AStarNode | null = current;
 263:       while (curr) {
 264:         path.push({ x: curr.x, y: curr.y });
 265:         curr = curr.parent;
 266:       }
 267:       return { path: path.reverse(), type: 'FULL', nodesExpanded, selectedGoal: reachedGoal };
 268:     }
 269: 
 270:     // Track best partial (lowest h to any goal)
 271:     const currentH = Math.min(...goals.map(g => heuristic(current, g)));
 272:     const bestPartialH = Math.min(...goals.map(g => heuristic(bestPartial, g)));
 273:     if (currentH < bestPartialH) {
 274:       bestPartial = current;
 275:       // approximate best goal for partial
 276:       let bg = goals[0];
 277:       let bgh = heuristic(current, goals[0]);
 278:       for (const g of goals) {
 279:         const h = heuristic(current, g);
 280:         if (h < bgh) { bgh = h; bg = g; }
 281:       }
 282:       bestGoal = bg;
 283:     }
 284: 
 285:     // Neighbors (deterministic order: up, right, down, left)
 286:     const dirs = [
 287:       { dx: 0, dy: -gs },
 288:       { dx: gs, dy: 0 },
 289:       { dx: 0, dy: gs },
 290:       { dx: -gs, dy: 0 }
 291:     ];
 292: 
 293:     for (const d of dirs) {
 294:       const nx = current.x + d.dx;
 295:       const ny = current.y + d.dy;
 296:       const nKey = `${nx},${ny}`;
 297:       
 298:       if (closedSet.has(nKey)) continue;
 299:       
 300:       // Check search bounds
 301:       if (nx < searchBounds.left || nx > searchBounds.right || ny < searchBounds.top || ny > searchBounds.bottom) continue;
 302: 
 303:       if (isCellBlocked(nx, ny, inflatedObstacles, navBounds, snapshot)) continue;
 304: 
 305:       const g = current.g + gs;
 306:       const h = Math.min(...goals.map(g => heuristic({x: nx, y: ny}, g)));
 307:       
 308:       openList.push({ x: nx, y: ny, g, h, f: g + h, parent: current });
 309:     }
 310:   }
 311: 
 312:   // If we exhaust or hit maxNodes, return partial
 313:   if (bestPartial !== startNode) {
 314:     const path: Vec2[] = [];
 315:     let curr: AStarNode | null = bestPartial;
 316:     while (curr) {
 317:       path.push({ x: curr.x, y: curr.y });
 318:       curr = curr.parent;
 319:     }
 320:     return { path: path.reverse(), type: 'PARTIAL', nodesExpanded, selectedGoal: bestGoal };
 321:   }
 322: 
 323:   return { path: null, type: null, nodesExpanded, selectedGoal: null };
 324: }
 325: 
 326: // Function to simplify collinear points
 327: export function simplifyPath(path: Vec2[]): Vec2[] {
 328:   if (path.length <= 2) return path;
 329:   const res: Vec2[] = [path[0]];
 330:   for (let i = 1; i < path.length - 1; i++) {
 331:     const prev = res[res.length - 1];
 332:     const curr = path[i];
 333:     const next = path[i+1];
 334:     
 335:     // Check if collinear
 336:     if ((curr.x - prev.x) * (next.y - curr.y) === (curr.y - prev.y) * (next.x - curr.x)) {
 337:       continue; // Skip collinear
 338:     }
 339:     res.push(curr);
 340:   }
 341:   res.push(path[path.length - 1]);
 342:   return res;
 343: }


================================================================================

   1: import { BotWorldSnapshotV2, BotStateContextV2, Rect } from './BotTypesV2';
   2: import { BOT_CONFIG_V2 } from './BotConfigV2';

   4: import { checkProgress } from './BotProgressMonitorV2';

   6: import { planPathV2, simplifyPath } from './BotPlannerV2';

   9: export function inflateObstacles(snapshot: BotWorldSnapshotV2): Rect[] {
  10:   const margin = snapshot.botRadius + BOT_CONFIG_V2.navMarginPx;
  11:   return snapshot.platforms.map(p => ({
  12:     left: p.rect.left - margin,
  13:     right: p.rect.right + margin,
  14:     top: p.rect.top - margin,
  15:     bottom: p.rect.bottom + margin,
  16:   }));
  17: }
  18: 
  19: export function updateStateMachine(snapshot: BotWorldSnapshotV2, context: BotStateContextV2): void {
  20:   const recorder = BotFlightRecorder.getInstance();
  21:   
  22:   if (typeof context.lastObstacleRevision === 'undefined') {
  23:     context.lastObstacleRevision = snapshot.obstacleRevision;
  24:   }
  25:   // 1. Update Awareness
  26:   updateAwareness(snapshot, context);
  27: 
  28:   // If we just gained awareness, trigger ALERT
  29:   if (context.awareness && context.currentState === 'SEARCH') {
  30:     if (snapshot.simTimeMs <= context.alertCooldownUntilMs) {
  31:       // Record rejected transition
  32:       recorder.recordEvent(
  33:         snapshot.simTimeMs,
  34:         'GREENFIELD_V2',
  35:         context.currentState,

 102:     }
 103:   }

 105:   // 2. Check collision for capture
 106:   const dx = snapshot.playerPosition.x - snapshot.botPosition.x;
 107:   const dy = snapshot.playerPosition.y - snapshot.botPosition.y;

 125:         snapshot.botRadius,
 126:         snapshot.playerRadius,
 127:         context.awareness?.id || 0,
 128:         snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 129:         context.debug.plannerStatus,
 130:         context.pathIndex
 131:       );
 132: 
 133:       recorder.recordEvent(
 134:         snapshot.simTimeMs,
 135:         'GREENFIELD_V2',
 136:         context.currentState,
 137:         oldState,

 139:         'CAPTURED_ENTERED',

 143:         snapshot.botRadius,
 144:         snapshot.playerRadius,

 148:         context.pathIndex
 149:       );

 356:       }

 381:       }
 382:     }
 383:

 385:     if (!progressOk) {
 386:       const oldState = context.currentState;
 387:       context.currentState = 'RECOVER';
 388:       context.progress.recoveryStartTimeMs = snapshot.simTimeMs;
 389:       context.progress.recoveryRung = 1;
 390:

 395:         oldState,

 397:         'NO_PROGRESS_DETECTED',
 398:         `Forward progress stalled, waypoint stuck, or oscillation detected! Initiating Recovery State.`,
 399:         snapshot.botPosition,
 400:         snapshot.playerPosition,
 401:         snapshot.botRadius,
 402:         snapshot.playerRadius,
 403:         context.awareness.id,
 404:         snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 405:         context.debug.plannerStatus,
 406:         context.pathIndex
 407:       );
 408: 
 409:       recorder.recordEvent(
 410:         snapshot.simTimeMs,
 411:         'GREENFIELD_V2',
 412:         context.currentState,
 413:         oldState,
 414:         'STATE_MACHINE',
 415:         'CHASE_TO_RECOVER',
 416:         `Stall detected. Transitioned to RECOVER.`,
 417:         snapshot.botPosition,
 418:         snapshot.playerPosition,
 419:         snapshot.botRadius,
 420:         snapshot.playerRadius,
 421:         context.awareness.id,

 423:         context.debug.plannerStatus,

 425:       );
 426: 
 427:       recorder.recordEvent(
 428:         snapshot.simTimeMs,
 429:         'GREENFIELD_V2',
 430:         context.currentState,
 431:         oldState,
 432:         'STATE_MACHINE',
 433:         'RECOVER_ENTERED',
 434:         `Entered RECOVER state. Rung 1: Discard path & attempt local full-grid expanded replan.`,
 435:         snapshot.botPosition,
 436:         snapshot.playerPosition,
 437:         snapshot.botRadius,
 438:         snapshot.playerRadius,
 439:         context.awareness.id,
 440:         snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 441:         context.debug.plannerStatus,
 442:         context.pathIndex
 443:       );
 444: 
 445:       // Rung 1: discard and replan expanded
 446:       requestReplan(snapshot, context, inflatedObstacles, 'EXPANDED');
 447:       if (context.currentPathType === 'FULL') {
 448:          const lastState = context.currentState;
 449:          context.currentState = 'CHASE';
 450:          
 451:          recorder.recordEvent(
 452:            snapshot.simTimeMs,
 453:            'GREENFIELD_V2',
 454:            context.currentState,
 455:            lastState,
 456:            'PROGRESS_RECOVERY',
 457:            'RECOVER_TO_CHASE',
 458:            `Rung 1 recovery succeeded! Full path planned during recovery, returning to CHASE.`,
 459:            snapshot.botPosition,
 460:            snapshot.playerPosition,
 461:            snapshot.botRadius,
 462:            snapshot.playerRadius,
 463:            context.awareness.id,
 464:            snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 465:            context.debug.plannerStatus,
 466:            context.pathIndex
 467:          );
 468:       }
 469:       return;
 470:     }
 471: 
 472:     if (shouldReplan(snapshot, context)) {
 473:       requestReplan(snapshot, context, inflatedObstacles, 'LOCAL');
 474:     }
 475: 
 476:     const nextState = context.currentPathType === 'PARTIAL' ? 'HOLD' : 'CHASE';
 477:     if (context.currentState !== nextState) {
 478:       const oldState = context.currentState;
 479:       context.currentState = nextState;
 480: 
 481:       recorder.recordEvent(
 482:         snapshot.simTimeMs,
 483:         'GREENFIELD_V2',
 484:         context.currentState,
 485:         oldState,
 486:         'STATE_MACHINE',
 487:         nextState === 'HOLD' ? 'CHASE_TO_HOLD' : 'HOLD_TO_CHASE',
 488:         `Path type changed to ${context.currentPathType}. Transitioning from ${oldState} to ${nextState}.`,
 489:         snapshot.botPosition,
 490:         snapshot.playerPosition,
 491:         snapshot.botRadius,
 492:         snapshot.playerRadius,
 493:         context.awareness.id,
 494:         snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 495:         context.debug.plannerStatus,
 496:         context.pathIndex
 497:       );
 498:     }
 499:   }
 500: 
 501:   if (context.currentState !== 'CHASE' && context.currentState !== 'HOLD') {
 502:     context.attackSubState = 'NONE';
 503:     context.stageTimerMs = 0;
 504:     context.strikeTarget = null;
 505:   }
 506: }
 507: 
 508: export function shouldReplan(snapshot: BotWorldSnapshotV2, context: BotStateContextV2): boolean {
 509:   if (context.attackSubState === 'STAGE' || context.attackSubState === 'STRIKE') {
 510:     return false;
 511:   }
 512: 
 513:   const recorder = BotFlightRecorder.getInstance();
 514: 
 515:   if (snapshot.playerMovementState === 'MOVE_STARTED') {
 516:     recorder.recordEvent(
 517:       snapshot.simTimeMs,
 518:       'GREENFIELD_V2',
 519:       context.currentState,
 520:       'NONE',
 521:       'PLANNING',
 522:       'RETARGETING_TRIGGERED',
 523:       `Player transit initiated. Bypassing replan interval to retarget player's new destination platform.`,
 524:       snapshot.botPosition,
 525:       snapshot.playerPosition,
 526:       snapshot.botRadius,
 527:       snapshot.playerRadius,
 528:       context.awareness?.id || 0,
 529:       snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 530:       context.debug.plannerStatus,
 531:       context.pathIndex
 532:     );
 533:     return true;
 534:   }
 535:   
 536:   if (typeof context.lastObstacleRevision !== 'undefined' && snapshot.obstacleRevision !== context.lastObstacleRevision) {
 537:     const prev = context.lastObstacleRevision;
 538:     context.lastObstacleRevision = snapshot.obstacleRevision;
 539:     
 540:     recorder.recordEvent(
 541:       snapshot.simTimeMs,
 542:       'GREENFIELD_V2',
 543:       context.currentState,
 544:       'NONE',
 545:       'PLANNING',
 546:       'OBSTACLE_REVISION_CHANGED',
 547:       `Obstacle revision updated from ${prev} to ${snapshot.obstacleRevision}. Rebuilding nav grid.`,
 548:       snapshot.botPosition,
 549:       snapshot.playerPosition,
 550:       snapshot.botRadius,
 551:       snapshot.playerRadius,
 552:       context.awareness?.id || 0,
 553:       snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 554:       context.debug.plannerStatus,
 555:       context.pathIndex
 556:     );
 557:     return true;
 558:   }
 559:   if (!context.currentPath || context.currentPath.length === 0) return true;
 560:   
 561:   const age = snapshot.simTimeMs - context.currentPathGeneratedAtMs;
 562:   if (age > BOT_CONFIG_V2.pathStaleAgeMs) {
 563:     recorder.recordEvent(
 564:       snapshot.simTimeMs,
 565:       'GREENFIELD_V2',
 566:       context.currentState,
 567:       'NONE',
 568:       'PLANNING',
 569:       'PATH_BECAME_STALE',
 570:       `Path generated ${age.toFixed(0)}ms ago has exceeded stale age limit of ${BOT_CONFIG_V2.pathStaleAgeMs}ms. Replanning required.`,
 571:       snapshot.botPosition,
 572:       snapshot.playerPosition,
 573:       snapshot.botRadius,
 574:       snapshot.playerRadius,
 575:       context.awareness?.id || 0,
 576:       snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 577:       context.debug.plannerStatus,
 578:       context.pathIndex
 579:     );
 580:     return true;
 581:   }
 582:   if (context.currentGoalAnchor) {
 583:     const pdx = snapshot.playerPosition.x - context.currentGoalAnchor.x;
 584:     const pdy = snapshot.playerPosition.y - context.currentGoalAnchor.y;
 585:     const playerMovedDist = Math.sqrt(pdx*pdx + pdy*pdy);
 586:     if (playerMovedDist > BOT_CONFIG_V2.playerMoveReplanDistPx) {
 587:       recorder.recordEvent(
 588:         snapshot.simTimeMs,
 589:         'GREENFIELD_V2',
 590:         context.currentState,
 591:         'NONE',
 592:         'PLANNING',
 593:         'GOAL_TARGET_VERSION_CHANGED',
 594:         `Player moved ${playerMovedDist.toFixed(1)}px from goal anchor. Goal anchor version updated. Requesting local replan.`,
 595:         snapshot.botPosition,
 596:         snapshot.playerPosition,
 597:         snapshot.botRadius,
 598:         snapshot.playerRadius,
 599:         context.awareness?.id || 0,
 600:         snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 601:         context.debug.plannerStatus,
 602:         context.pathIndex
 603:       );
 604:       return true;
 605:     }
 606:   }
 607:   return false;
 608: }
 609: 
 610: export function requestReplan(snapshot: BotWorldSnapshotV2, context: BotStateContextV2, inflatedObstacles: Rect[], forceStage: 'LOCAL'|'EXPANDED' = 'LOCAL') {
 611:   if (!context.awareness) return;
 612: 
 613:   const recorder = BotFlightRecorder.getInstance();
 614:   const target = getBelowPlayerAnchor(context.awareness.lastKnownPlayerPosition, snapshot, inflatedObstacles);
 615: 
 616:   recorder.recordEvent(
 617:     snapshot.simTimeMs,
 618:     'GREENFIELD_V2',
 619:     context.currentState,
 620:     'NONE',
 621:     'GOAL_SELECTION',
 622:     'GOAL_SELECTION_STARTED',
 623:     `Planning path to below-player anchor: (${target.x.toFixed(0)}, ${target.y.toFixed(0)}).`,
 624:     snapshot.botPosition,
 625:     snapshot.playerPosition,
 626:     snapshot.botRadius,
 627:     snapshot.playerRadius,
 628:     context.awareness.id,
 629:     snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 630:     context.debug.plannerStatus,
 631:     context.pathIndex
 632:   );
 633: 
 634:   // Filter blocked candidate cells
 635:   const rawCandidates = [
 636:     { x: snapToGrid(target.x), y: snapToGrid(target.y) },
 637:     { x: snapToGrid(target.x), y: snapToGrid(target.y) - BOT_CONFIG_V2.gridSize },
 638:     { x: snapToGrid(target.x), y: snapToGrid(target.y) + BOT_CONFIG_V2.gridSize },
 639:     { x: snapToGrid(target.x) - BOT_CONFIG_V2.gridSize, y: snapToGrid(target.y) },
 640:     { x: snapToGrid(target.x) + BOT_CONFIG_V2.gridSize, y: snapToGrid(target.y) }
 641:   ];
 642: 
 643:   rawCandidates.forEach((c, idx) => {
 644:     const isBlocked = isCellBlocked(c.x, c.y, inflatedObstacles, snapshot.navigationBounds, snapshot);
 645:     const label = idx === 0 ? 'PLAYER_CENTER_CELL' : `ADJACENT_NEIGHBOR_${idx}`;
 646:     if (isBlocked) {
 647:       recorder.recordEvent(
 648:         snapshot.simTimeMs,
 649:         'GREENFIELD_V2',
 650:         context.currentState,
 651:         'NONE',
 652:         'GOAL_SELECTION',
 653:         idx === 0 ? 'PLAYER_CENTER_CELL_BLOCKED' : 'GOAL_CANDIDATE_REJECTED',
 654:         `Goal candidate ${label} at (${c.x}, ${c.y}) rejected. Reason: cell blocked or overlaps obstacle.`,
 655:         snapshot.botPosition,
 656:         snapshot.playerPosition,
 657:         snapshot.botRadius,
 658:         snapshot.playerRadius,
 659:         context.awareness!.id,
 660:         snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 661:         context.debug.plannerStatus,
 662:         context.pathIndex,
 663:         { cell: c, reason: 'blocked' }
 664:       );
 665:     } else {
 666:       recorder.recordEvent(
 667:         snapshot.simTimeMs,
 668:         'GREENFIELD_V2',
 669:         context.currentState,
 670:         'NONE',
 671:         'GOAL_SELECTION',
 672:         'GOAL_CANDIDATE_ACCEPTED',
 673:         `Goal candidate ${label} at (${c.x}, ${c.y}) accepted.`,
 674:         snapshot.botPosition,
 675:         snapshot.playerPosition,
 676:         snapshot.botRadius,
 677:         snapshot.playerRadius,
 678:         context.awareness!.id,
 679:         snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 680:         context.debug.plannerStatus,
 681:         context.pathIndex,
 682:         { cell: c }
 683:       );
 684:     }
 685:   });
 686: 
 687:   const candidates = getGoalCandidates(snapshot, target, inflatedObstacles);
 688: 
 689:   recorder.recordEvent(
 690:     snapshot.simTimeMs,
 691:     'GREENFIELD_V2',
 692:     context.currentState,
 693:     'NONE',
 694:     'GOAL_SELECTION',
 695:     'GOAL_SET_CREATED',
 696:     `Created candidate target goal set. Total unblocked goals: ${candidates.length}`,
 697:     snapshot.botPosition,
 698:     snapshot.playerPosition,
 699:     snapshot.botRadius,
 700:     snapshot.playerRadius,
 701:     context.awareness.id,
 702:     snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 703:     context.debug.plannerStatus,
 704:     context.pathIndex,
 705:     { count: candidates.length }
 706:   );
 707: 
 708:   if (candidates.length === 0) {
 709:     recorder.recordEvent(
 710:       snapshot.simTimeMs,
 711:       'GREENFIELD_V2',
 712:       context.currentState,
 713:       'NONE',
 714:       'GOAL_SELECTION',
 715:       'NO_LEGAL_GOALS',
 716:       `No valid, unblocked goal locations could be selected around the target. Path planning aborted.`,
 717:       snapshot.botPosition,
 718:       snapshot.playerPosition,
 719:       snapshot.botRadius,
 720:       snapshot.playerRadius,
 721:       context.awareness.id,
 722:       snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 723:       context.debug.plannerStatus,
 724:       context.pathIndex
 725:     );
 726:     return;
 727:   }
 728: 
 729:   recorder.recordEvent(
 730:     snapshot.simTimeMs,
 731:     'GREENFIELD_V2',
 732:     context.currentState,
 733:     'NONE',
 734:     'PLANNING',
 735:     'PLAN_REQUESTED',
 736:     `Requesting path plan. Start cell: (${snapToGrid(snapshot.botPosition.x)}, ${snapToGrid(snapshot.botPosition.y)}). Goal count: ${candidates.length}. ForceStage: ${forceStage}`,
 737:     snapshot.botPosition,
 738:     snapshot.playerPosition,
 739:     snapshot.botRadius,
 740:     snapshot.playerRadius,
 741:     context.awareness.id,
 742:     snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 743:     context.debug.plannerStatus,
 744:     context.pathIndex
 745:   );
 746: 
 747:   let searchBounds = { ...snapshot.navigationBounds };
 748:   if (forceStage === 'LOCAL') {
 749:     const minX = Math.min(snapshot.botPosition.x, target.x) - 160;
 750:     const maxX = Math.max(snapshot.botPosition.x, target.x) + 160;
 751:     const minY = Math.min(snapshot.botPosition.y, target.y) - 160;
 752:     const maxY = Math.max(snapshot.botPosition.y, target.y) + 160;
 753:     searchBounds = { left: minX, right: maxX, top: minY, bottom: maxY };
 754:   }
 755: 
 756:   recorder.recordEvent(
 757:     snapshot.simTimeMs,
 758:     'GREENFIELD_V2',
 759:     context.currentState,
 760:     'NONE',
 761:     'PLANNING',
 762:     'PLAN_STAGE_1_STARTED',
 763:     `A* Stage 1 (LOCAL) started. Max Nodes: ${BOT_CONFIG_V2.plannerLocalMaxNodes}. Bounds: L:${searchBounds.left.toFixed(0)} R:${searchBounds.right.toFixed(0)} T:${searchBounds.top.toFixed(0)} B:${searchBounds.bottom.toFixed(0)}`,
 764:     snapshot.botPosition,
 765:     snapshot.playerPosition,
 766:     snapshot.botRadius,
 767:     snapshot.playerRadius,
 768:     context.awareness.id,
 769:     snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 770:     context.debug.plannerStatus,
 771:     context.pathIndex
 772:   );
 773: 
 774:   let res = planPathV2(snapshot.botPosition, candidates, inflatedObstacles, snapshot.navigationBounds, searchBounds, BOT_CONFIG_V2.plannerLocalMaxNodes, snapshot);
 775:   context.debug.nodesExpanded = res.nodesExpanded;
 776: 
 777:   if (res.type !== 'FULL') {
 778:     recorder.recordEvent(
 779:       snapshot.simTimeMs,
 780:       'GREENFIELD_V2',
 781:       context.currentState,
 782:       'NONE',
 783:       'PLANNING',
 784:       res.type === 'PARTIAL' ? 'PLAN_STAGE_1_PARTIAL' : 'PLAN_STAGE_1_FAILED',
 785:       `A* Stage 1 (LOCAL) did not find full path. Result: ${res.type || 'FAILED'}. Nodes expanded: ${res.nodesExpanded}`,
 786:       snapshot.botPosition,
 787:       snapshot.playerPosition,
 788:       snapshot.botRadius,
 789:       snapshot.playerRadius,
 790:       context.awareness.id,
 791:       snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 792:       context.debug.plannerStatus,
 793:       context.pathIndex,
 794:       { nodesExpanded: res.nodesExpanded }
 795:     );
 796: 
 797:     // Stage 2 expanded
 798:     context.debug.plannerStage = 'EXPANDED';
 799: 
 800:     recorder.recordEvent(
 801:       snapshot.simTimeMs,
 802:       'GREENFIELD_V2',
 803:       context.currentState,
 804:       'NONE',
 805:       'PLANNING',
 806:       'PLAN_STAGE_2_STARTED',
 807:       `A* Stage 2 (EXPANDED) fallback initiated. Max Nodes: ${BOT_CONFIG_V2.plannerExpandedMaxNodes}. Bounds: Full viewport`,
 808:       snapshot.botPosition,
 809:       snapshot.playerPosition,
 810:       snapshot.botRadius,
 811:       snapshot.playerRadius,
 812:       context.awareness.id,
 813:       snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 814:       context.debug.plannerStatus,
 815:       context.pathIndex
 816:     );
 817: 
 818:     res = planPathV2(snapshot.botPosition, candidates, inflatedObstacles, snapshot.navigationBounds, snapshot.navigationBounds, BOT_CONFIG_V2.plannerExpandedMaxNodes, snapshot);
 819:     context.debug.nodesExpanded += res.nodesExpanded;
 820: 
 821:     if (res.path) {
 822:       recorder.recordEvent(
 823:         snapshot.simTimeMs,
 824:         'GREENFIELD_V2',
 825:         context.currentState,
 826:         'NONE',
 827:         'PLANNING',
 828:         res.type === 'FULL' ? 'PLAN_STAGE_2_FULL' : 'PLAN_STAGE_2_PARTIAL',
 829:         `A* Stage 2 (EXPANDED) completed. Result: ${res.type}. Path length: ${res.path.length}. Nodes expanded this stage: ${res.nodesExpanded}`,
 830:         snapshot.botPosition,
 831:         snapshot.playerPosition,
 832:         snapshot.botRadius,
 833:         snapshot.playerRadius,
 834:         context.awareness.id,
 835:         snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 836:         context.debug.plannerStatus,
 837:         context.pathIndex,
 838:         { pathType: res.type, nodesExpanded: res.nodesExpanded }
 839:       );
 840:     } else {
 841:       recorder.recordEvent(
 842:         snapshot.simTimeMs,
 843:         'GREENFIELD_V2',
 844:         context.currentState,
 845:         'NONE',
 846:         'PLANNING',
 847:         'PLAN_STAGE_2_FAILED',
 848:         `A* Stage 2 (EXPANDED) failed to find any path to goal. Nodes expanded: ${res.nodesExpanded}`,
 849:         snapshot.botPosition,
 850:         snapshot.playerPosition,
 851:         snapshot.botRadius,
 852:         snapshot.playerRadius,
 853:         context.awareness.id,
 854:         snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 855:         context.debug.plannerStatus,
 856:         context.pathIndex
 857:       );
 858:     }
 859:   } else {
 860:     recorder.recordEvent(
 861:       snapshot.simTimeMs,
 862:       'GREENFIELD_V2',
 863:       context.currentState,
 864:       'NONE',
 865:       'PLANNING',
 866:       'PLAN_STAGE_1_FULL',
 867:       `A* Stage 1 (LOCAL) found full path! Path length: ${res.path ? res.path.length : 0}. Nodes expanded: ${res.nodesExpanded}`,
 868:       snapshot.botPosition,
 869:       snapshot.playerPosition,
 870:       snapshot.botRadius,
 871:       snapshot.playerRadius,
 872:       context.awareness.id,
 873:       snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 874:       context.debug.plannerStatus,
 875:       context.pathIndex,
 876:       { pathType: 'FULL', nodesExpanded: res.nodesExpanded }
 877:     );
 878:     context.debug.plannerStage = 'LOCAL';
 879:   }
 880: 
 881:   if (res.path) {
 882:     context.currentPath = simplifyPath(res.path);
 883:     context.currentPathType = res.type;
 884:     context.pathIndex = 0;
 885:     context.currentGoalAnchor = target;
 886:     context.currentPathGeneratedAtMs = snapshot.simTimeMs;
 887:     context.progress.plannerFailures = 0;
 888:     context.debug.plannerStatus = res.type === 'FULL' ? 'SUCCESS' : 'PARTIAL';
 889: 
 890:     recorder.recordEvent(
 891:       snapshot.simTimeMs,
 892:       'GREENFIELD_V2',
 893:       context.currentState,
 894:       'NONE',
 895:       'PLANNING',
 896:       'PATH_ADOPTED',
 897:       `Adopted planned path. Simplified waypoints: ${context.currentPath.length}. Goal coordinate: (${res.selectedGoal?.x}, ${res.selectedGoal?.y})`,
 898:       snapshot.botPosition,
 899:       snapshot.playerPosition,
 900:       snapshot.botRadius,
 901:       snapshot.playerRadius,
 902:       context.awareness.id,
 903:       snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 904:       context.debug.plannerStatus,
 905:       context.pathIndex,
 906:       { waypointsCount: context.currentPath.length }
 907:     );
 908:   } else {
 909:     context.progress.plannerFailures++;
 910:     context.debug.plannerStatus = 'FAILED';
 911: 
 912:     recorder.recordEvent(
 913:       snapshot.simTimeMs,
 914:       'GREENFIELD_V2',
 915:       context.currentState,
 916:       'NONE',
 917:       'PLANNING',
 918:       'PATH_DISCARDED',
 919:       `No path found in any stage. Current path cleared. Planner failure count: ${context.progress.plannerFailures}`,
 920:       snapshot.botPosition,
 921:       snapshot.playerPosition,
 922:       snapshot.botRadius,
 923:       snapshot.playerRadius,
 924:       context.awareness.id,
 925:       snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 926:       context.debug.plannerStatus,
 927:       context.pathIndex
 928:     );
 929:   }
 930:   
 931:   context.debug.goalCandidates = candidates;
 932:   context.debug.selectedGoal = res.selectedGoal;
 933: }
 934:
