   1: import { BotWorldSnapshotV2, BotStateContextV2, Rect, isBotInsideAttackEnvelope, DEFAULT_ATTACK_ENVELOPE_CONFIG_V2 } from './BotTypesV2';
   2: import { BOT_CONFIG_V2 } from './BotConfigV2';
   3: import { updateAwareness } from './BotAwarenessV2';
   4: import { checkProgressV2 } from './BotProgressMonitorV2';
   5: import { getSemanticGoalCandidatesV2, snapToGrid } from './BotGoalSelectorV2';
   6: import { planPathV2Simplified, simplifyPath } from './BotPlannerV2';
   7: import { executeRecovery } from './BotRecoveryV2';
   8: import { BotFlightRecorder } from './BotFlightRecorderV2';
   9: 
  10: export function inflateObstacles(snapshot: BotWorldSnapshotV2): Rect[] {
  11:   const margin = snapshot.botRadius + BOT_CONFIG_V2.navMarginPx;
  12:   return snapshot.platforms.map(p => ({
  13:     left: p.rect.left - margin,
  14:     right: p.rect.right + margin,
  15:     top: p.rect.top - margin,
  16:     bottom: p.rect.bottom + margin,
  17:   }));
  18: }
  19: 
  20: export function updateStateMachineSimplified(
  21:   snapshot: BotWorldSnapshotV2,
  22:   context: BotStateContextV2,
  23:   lastMovementMagnitude: number
  24: ): void {
  25:   const recorder = BotFlightRecorder.getInstance();
  26:   const inflatedObstacles = inflateObstacles(snapshot);
  27: 
  28:   // Normalize legacy state names for backward compatibility
  29:   if ((context.currentState as string) === 'CHASE' || (context.currentState as string) === 'HOLD') {
  30:     context.currentState = 'PURSUE';
  31:   } else if ((context.currentState as string) === 'STAGE' || (context.currentState as string) === 'STRIKE') {
  32:     context.currentState = 'FINAL_APPROACH';
  33:   }
  34: 
  35:   if (typeof context.lastObstacleRevision === 'undefined') {
  36:     context.lastObstacleRevision = snapshot.obstacleRevision;
  37:   }
  38: 
  39:   // Track target updates
  40:   if (typeof context.targetVersion === 'undefined') {
  41:     context.targetVersion = 1;
  42:   }
  43: 
  44:   const playerPlatformChanged = snapshot.playerSupportingPlatformId !== context.lastPlayerPlatformId;
  45:   const playerLandedOrMoved = snapshot.playerMovementState === 'MOVE_STARTED' || snapshot.playerMovementState === 'LANDING' || snapshot.playerMovementState === 'SETTLED';
  46:   const distFromLastPos = context.lastTargetPosition
  47:     ? Math.hypot(snapshot.playerPosition.x - context.lastTargetPosition.x, snapshot.playerPosition.y - context.lastTargetPosition.y)
  48:     : 0;
  49: 
  50:   if ((playerPlatformChanged && snapshot.playerSupportingPlatformId !== null) || (playerLandedOrMoved && distFromLastPos > 24)) {
  51:     context.targetVersion++;
  52:     context.lastPlayerPlatformId = snapshot.playerSupportingPlatformId;
  53:     context.lastTargetPosition = { ...snapshot.playerPosition };
  54:   }
  55: 
  56:   // 1. Update Awareness
  57:   updateAwareness(snapshot, context);
  58: 
  59:   // If we just gained awareness, trigger ALERT
  60:   if (context.awareness && context.currentState === 'SEARCH') {
  61:     if (snapshot.simTimeMs <= context.alertCooldownUntilMs) {
  62:       recorder.recordEvent(
  63:         snapshot.simTimeMs,
  64:         'GREENFIELD_V2',
  65:         context.currentState,
  66:         'NONE',
  67:         'STATE_MACHINE',
  68:         'STATE_TRANSITION_REJECTED',
  69:         `Transition SEARCH to ALERT rejected due to active cooldown (${context.alertCooldownUntilMs - snapshot.simTimeMs}ms remaining).`,
  70:         snapshot.botPosition,
  71:         snapshot.playerPosition,
  72:         snapshot.botRadius,
  73:         snapshot.playerRadius,
  74:         context.awareness.id,
  75:         snapshot.obstacleRevision,
  76:         context.debug.plannerStatus,
  77:         context.pathIndex
  78:       );
  79:     } else {
  80:       const oldState = context.currentState;
  81:       context.currentState = 'ALERT';
  82:       context.awareness.openedAtMs = snapshot.simTimeMs;
  83: 
  84:       recorder.recordEvent(
  85:         snapshot.simTimeMs,
  86:         'GREENFIELD_V2',
  87:         context.currentState,
  88:         oldState,
  89:         'STATE_MACHINE',
  90:         'SEARCH_TO_ALERT',
  91:         `Gained awareness. Entered ALERT state.`,
  92:         snapshot.botPosition,
  93:         snapshot.playerPosition,
  94:         snapshot.botRadius,
  95:         snapshot.playerRadius,
  96:         context.awareness.id,
  97:         snapshot.obstacleRevision,
  98:         context.debug.plannerStatus,
  99:         context.pathIndex
 100:       );
 101: 
 102:       // Reset progress tracking
 103:       context.progress = {
 104:         lastPos: { ...snapshot.botPosition },
 105:         lastPosTimeMs: snapshot.simTimeMs,
 106:         lastWaypointIndex: 0,
 107:         waypointStallTimeMs: snapshot.simTimeMs,
 108:         recentCells: [],
 109:         plannerFailures: 0,
 110:         recoveryRung: 0,
 111:         recoveryStartTimeMs: 0
 112:       };
 113:     }
 114:   }
 115: 
 116:   // 2. Check collision for capture
 117:   const dx = snapshot.playerPosition.x - snapshot.botPosition.x;
 118:   const dy = snapshot.playerPosition.y - snapshot.botPosition.y;
 119:   const centerDist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
 120:   if (centerDist < snapshot.botRadius + snapshot.playerRadius - BOT_CONFIG_V2.captureRadiusDiff) {
 121:     if (context.currentState !== 'CAPTURED') {
 122:       const oldState = context.currentState;
 123:       context.events.push({ type: 'CAPTURE' });
 124:       context.currentState = 'CAPTURED';
 125: 
 126:       recorder.recordEvent(
 127:         snapshot.simTimeMs,
 128:         'GREENFIELD_V2',
 129:         context.currentState,
 130:         oldState,
 131:         'LIFECYCLE',
 132:         'BOT_CAPTURED',
 133:         `Bot captured player at distance ${centerDist.toFixed(1)}px.`,
 134:         snapshot.botPosition,
 135:         snapshot.playerPosition,
 136:         snapshot.botRadius,
 137:         snapshot.playerRadius,
 138:         context.awareness?.id || 0,
 139:         snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 140:         context.debug.plannerStatus,
 141:         context.pathIndex
 142:       );
 143:     }
 144:   }
 145: 
 146:   if (context.currentState === 'CAPTURED') return;
 147: 
 148:   // 3. ALERT state handling
 149:   if (context.currentState === 'ALERT') {
 150:     if (!context.awareness?.excitementPlayed) {
 151:       context.events.push({ type: 'PLAY_EXCITEMENT_SOUND' });
 152:       context.events.push({ type: 'SHOW_ALERT_REACTION' });
 153:       if (context.awareness) context.awareness.excitementPlayed = true;
 154:     }
 155: 
 156:     const timeInAlert = snapshot.simTimeMs - (context.awareness?.openedAtMs || 0);
 157:     if (timeInAlert > BOT_CONFIG_V2.excitementDurationMs) {
 158:       const oldState = context.currentState;
 159:       context.currentState = 'PURSUE';
 160: 
 161:       recorder.recordEvent(
 162:         snapshot.simTimeMs,
 163:         'GREENFIELD_V2',
 164:         context.currentState,
 165:         oldState,
 166:         'STATE_MACHINE',
 167:         'ALERT_TO_PURSUE',
 168:         `Alert duration finished. Entered PURSUE state.`,
 169:         snapshot.botPosition,
 170:         snapshot.playerPosition,
 171:         snapshot.botRadius,
 172:         snapshot.playerRadius,
 173:         context.awareness?.id || 0,
 174:         snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 175:         context.debug.plannerStatus,
 176:         context.pathIndex
 177:       );
 178: 
 179:       requestReplanSimplified(snapshot, context, inflatedObstacles);
 180:     }
 181:     return;
 182:   }
 183: 
 184:   // 4. PURSUE state handling
 185:   if (context.currentState === 'PURSUE') {
 186:     if (!context.awareness) {
 187:       context.currentState = 'SEARCH';
 188:       recorder.recordEvent(
 189:         snapshot.simTimeMs,
 190:         'GREENFIELD_V2',
 191:         'SEARCH',
 192:         'PURSUE',
 193:         'STATE_MACHINE',
 194:         'PURSUE_TO_SEARCH',
 195:         `Awareness expired. Reverting to SEARCH.`,
 196:         snapshot.botPosition,
 197:         snapshot.playerPosition,
 198:         snapshot.botRadius,
 199:         snapshot.playerRadius,
 200:         0,
 201:         snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 202:         context.debug.plannerStatus,
 203:         context.pathIndex
 204:       );
 205:       return;
 206:     }
 207: 
 208:     // Check if bot is inside Attack Envelope -> Transition to FINAL_APPROACH
 209:     const envCheck = isBotInsideAttackEnvelope(snapshot.botPosition, snapshot.playerPosition, inflatedObstacles);
 210:     if (envCheck.isInside) {
 211:       context.currentState = 'FINAL_APPROACH';
 212:       context.currentPath = null;
 213:       context.currentPathType = null;
 214: 
 215:       recorder.recordEvent(
 216:         snapshot.simTimeMs,
 217:         'GREENFIELD_V2',
 218:         'FINAL_APPROACH',
 219:         'PURSUE',
 220:         'STATE_MACHINE',
 221:         'PURSUE_TO_FINAL_APPROACH',
 222:         `Bot entered attack envelope (dx=${envCheck.dx.toFixed(1)}px, dy=${envCheck.dy.toFixed(1)}px). Transitioned to FINAL_APPROACH.`,
 223:         snapshot.botPosition,
 224:         snapshot.playerPosition,
 225:         snapshot.botRadius,
 226:         snapshot.playerRadius,
 227:         context.awareness.id,
 228:         snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 229:         context.debug.plannerStatus,
 230:         context.pathIndex
 231:       );
 232:       return;
 233:     }
 234: 
 235:     // Check progress monitor
 236:     const progressOk = checkProgressV2(snapshot, context, lastMovementMagnitude, inflatedObstacles);
 237:     if (!progressOk) {
 238:       context.currentState = 'RECOVER';
 239:       context.progress.recoveryStartTimeMs = snapshot.simTimeMs;
 240:       context.progress.recoveryRung = 1;
 241: 
 242:       recorder.recordEvent(
 243:         snapshot.simTimeMs,
 244:         'GREENFIELD_V2',
 245:         'RECOVER',
 246:         'PURSUE',
 247:         'STATE_MACHINE',
 248:         'PURSUE_TO_RECOVER',
 249:         `Progress check failed in PURSUE. Transitioned to RECOVER.`,
 250:         snapshot.botPosition,
 251:         snapshot.playerPosition,
 252:         snapshot.botRadius,
 253:         snapshot.playerRadius,
 254:         context.awareness.id,
 255:         snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 256:         context.debug.plannerStatus,
 257:         context.pathIndex
 258:       );
 259:       return;
 260:     }
 261: 
 262:     // Check if replanning needed
 263:     if (shouldReplanSimplified(snapshot, context)) {
 264:       requestReplanSimplified(snapshot, context, inflatedObstacles);
 265:     }
 266:   }
 267: 
 268:   // 5. FINAL_APPROACH state handling
 269:   if (context.currentState === 'FINAL_APPROACH') {
 270:     if (!context.awareness) {
 271:       context.currentState = 'SEARCH';
 272:       return;
 273:     }
 274: 
 275:     const envCheck = isBotInsideAttackEnvelope(snapshot.botPosition, snapshot.playerPosition, inflatedObstacles);
 276:     const absDx = Math.abs(snapshot.botPosition.x - snapshot.playerPosition.x);
 277:     const dy = snapshot.botPosition.y - snapshot.playerPosition.y;
 278: 
 279:     // Check if player moved outside approach envelope bounds
 280:     const maxAllowedHalfWidth = DEFAULT_ATTACK_ENVELOPE_CONFIG_V2.envelopeHalfWidthPx * 1.5;
 281:     const minAllowedBelow = DEFAULT_ATTACK_ENVELOPE_CONFIG_V2.envelopeMinBelowPx - 20;
 282:     const maxAllowedBelow = DEFAULT_ATTACK_ENVELOPE_CONFIG_V2.envelopeMaxBelowPx + 40;
 283: 
 284:     if (absDx > maxAllowedHalfWidth || dy < minAllowedBelow || dy > maxAllowedBelow) {
 285:       context.currentState = 'PURSUE';
 286:       recorder.recordEvent(
 287:         snapshot.simTimeMs,
 288:         'GREENFIELD_V2',
 289:         'PURSUE',
 290:         'FINAL_APPROACH',
 291:         'STATE_MACHINE',
 292:         'FINAL_APPROACH_TO_PURSUE',
 293:         `Player moved outside valid approach envelope relationship (dx=${absDx.toFixed(1)}px, dy=${dy.toFixed(1)}px). Returning to PURSUE.`,
 294:         snapshot.botPosition,
 295:         snapshot.playerPosition,
 296:         snapshot.botRadius,
 297:         snapshot.playerRadius,
 298:         context.awareness.id,
 299:         snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 300:         context.debug.plannerStatus,
 301:         context.pathIndex
 302:       );
 303:       requestReplanSimplified(snapshot, context, inflatedObstacles);
 304:       return;
 305:     }
 306: 
 307:     const progressOk = checkProgressV2(snapshot, context, lastMovementMagnitude, inflatedObstacles);
 308:     if (!progressOk) {
 309:       context.currentState = 'RECOVER';
 310:       context.progress.recoveryStartTimeMs = snapshot.simTimeMs;
 311:       context.progress.recoveryRung = 1;
 312: 
 313:       recorder.recordEvent(
 314:         snapshot.simTimeMs,
 315:         'GREENFIELD_V2',
 316:         'RECOVER',
 317:         'FINAL_APPROACH',
 318:         'STATE_MACHINE',
 319:         'FINAL_APPROACH_TO_RECOVER',
 320:         `Stall or zero movement in FINAL_APPROACH. Transitioned to RECOVER.`,
 321:         snapshot.botPosition,
 322:         snapshot.playerPosition,
 323:         snapshot.botRadius,
 324:         snapshot.playerRadius,
 325:         context.awareness.id,
 326:         snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 327:         context.debug.plannerStatus,
 328:         context.pathIndex
 329:       );
 330:       return;
 331:     }
 332:   }
 333: 
 334:   // 6. RECOVER state handling
 335:   if (context.currentState === 'RECOVER') {
 336:     // INVARIANT 5: Same-frame recovery exit forbidden!
 337:     if (snapshot.simTimeMs === context.progress.recoveryStartTimeMs) {
 338:       return;
 339:     }
 340: 
 341:     // Execute recovery steps
 342:     executeRecovery(snapshot, context, inflatedObstacles);
 343: 
 344:     // INVARIANT 4: RECOVER can exit to PURSUE only if material condition changed
 345:     const botMovedDist = Math.hypot(
 346:       snapshot.botPosition.x - context.progress.lastPos.x,
 347:       snapshot.botPosition.y - context.progress.lastPos.y
 348:     );
 349:     const playerMoved = snapshot.playerMovementState === 'MOVE_STARTED' || snapshot.playerMovementState === 'IN_TRANSIT';
 350:     const obstacleChanged = snapshot.obstacleRevision !== context.lastObstacleRevision;
 351: 
 352:     const materialConditionChanged = botMovedDist >= 16 || playerMoved || obstacleChanged || context.progress.recoveryRung >= 2 || context.pathTargetVersion !== context.targetVersion || !context.currentPath;
 353: 
 354:     if (materialConditionChanged) {
 355:       // Attempt replan to see if valid pursuit plan is available
 356:       const goals = getSemanticGoalCandidatesV2(
 357:         snapshot,
 358:         snapshot.botPosition,
 359:         snapshot.playerPosition,
 360:         inflatedObstacles,
 361:         context.targetVersion,
 362:         context.progress.recentCells
 363:       );
 364: 
 365:       const planRes = planPathV2Simplified(
 366:         snapshot.botPosition,
 367:         goals,
 368:         inflatedObstacles,
 369:         snapshot.navigationBounds,
 370:         snapshot.navigationBounds,
 371:         BOT_CONFIG_V2.plannerExpandedMaxNodes,
 372:         snapshot
 373:       );
 374: 
 375:       if (planRes.outcome === 'REACHED_ATTACK_READY_GOAL' || planRes.outcome === 'REACHED_APPROACH_PROGRESS_GOAL' || planRes.outcome === 'PARTIAL_PROGRESS') {
 376:         context.currentState = 'PURSUE';
 377:         context.currentPath = planRes.path ? simplifyPath(planRes.path) : null;
 378:         context.currentPathType = planRes.outcome === 'PARTIAL_PROGRESS' ? 'PARTIAL' : 'FULL';
 379:         context.pathIndex = 0;
 380:         context.currentPathGeneratedAtMs = snapshot.simTimeMs;
 381:         context.pathTargetVersion = context.targetVersion;
 382:         context.currentGoalAnchor = planRes.selectedGoal ? { ...planRes.selectedGoal.worldPosition } : { ...snapshot.playerPosition };
 383:         context.progress.plannerFailures = 0;
 384: 
 385:         recorder.recordEvent(
 386:           snapshot.simTimeMs,
 387:           'GREENFIELD_V2',
 388:           'PURSUE',
 389:           'RECOVER',
 390:           'PROGRESS_RECOVERY',
 391:           'RECOVER_TO_PURSUE',
 392:           `Recovery succeeded with outcome ${planRes.outcome}. Material condition met (moved: ${botMovedDist.toFixed(1)}px). Transitioned to PURSUE.`,
 393:           snapshot.botPosition,
 394:           snapshot.playerPosition,
 395:           snapshot.botRadius,
 396:           snapshot.playerRadius,
 397:           context.awareness?.id || 0,
 398:           snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 399:           context.debug.plannerStatus,
 400:           context.pathIndex
 401:         );
 402:       }
 403:     }
 404:   }
 405: }
 406: 
 407: export function shouldReplanSimplified(snapshot: BotWorldSnapshotV2, context: BotStateContextV2): boolean {
 408:   if (snapshot.playerMovementState === 'MOVE_STARTED') {
 409:     return true;
 410:   }
 411: 
 412:   if (typeof context.lastObstacleRevision !== 'undefined' && snapshot.obstacleRevision !== context.lastObstacleRevision) {
 413:     context.lastObstacleRevision = snapshot.obstacleRevision;
 414:     return true;
 415:   }
 416: 
 417:   // Target version updated (player changed platform, landed, or moved > 24px)
 418:   if (typeof context.pathTargetVersion !== 'undefined' && context.pathTargetVersion !== context.targetVersion) {
 419:     return true;
 420:   }
 421: 
 422:   // Path absent, empty, or completed
 423:   if (!context.currentPath || context.currentPath.length === 0) return true;
 424:   if (context.pathIndex >= context.currentPath.length) return true;
 425: 
 426:   // Player position moved significantly from current goal anchor
 427:   if (context.currentGoalAnchor) {
 428:     const dist = Math.hypot(snapshot.playerPosition.x - context.currentGoalAnchor.x, snapshot.playerPosition.y - context.currentGoalAnchor.y);
 429:     if (dist > 48) return true;
 430:   }
 431: 
 432:   const age = snapshot.simTimeMs - context.currentPathGeneratedAtMs;
 433:   if (age > BOT_CONFIG_V2.pathStaleAgeMs) return true;
 434: 
 435:   return false;
 436: }
 437: 
 438: export function requestReplanSimplified(
 439:   snapshot: BotWorldSnapshotV2,
 440:   context: BotStateContextV2,
 441:   inflatedObstacles: Rect[]
 442: ): void {
 443:   if (!context.awareness) return;
 444: 
 445:   const recorder = BotFlightRecorder.getInstance();
 446:   const goals = getSemanticGoalCandidatesV2(
 447:     snapshot,
 448:     snapshot.botPosition,
 449:     snapshot.playerPosition,
 450:     inflatedObstacles,
 451:     context.targetVersion,
 452:     context.progress.recentCells
 453:   );
 454: 
 455:   recorder.recordEvent(
 456:     snapshot.simTimeMs,
 457:     'GREENFIELD_V2',
 458:     context.currentState,
 459:     'NONE',
 460:     'GOAL_SELECTION',
 461:     'GOAL_SET_CREATED',
 462:     `Generated ${goals.length} semantic goals for target v${context.targetVersion} (ATTACK_READY: ${goals.filter(g => g.purpose === 'ATTACK_READY').length}, APPROACH: ${goals.filter(g => g.purpose === 'APPROACH_PROGRESS').length}).`,
 463:     snapshot.botPosition,
 464:     snapshot.playerPosition,
 465:     snapshot.botRadius,
 466:     snapshot.playerRadius,
 467:     context.awareness.id,
 468:     snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 469:     context.debug.plannerStatus,
 470:     context.pathIndex,
 471:     { count: goals.length, targetVersion: context.targetVersion }
 472:   );
 473: 
 474:   if (goals.length === 0) {
 475:     context.currentPath = null;
 476:     context.currentPathType = null;
 477:     return;
 478:   }
 479: 
 480:   const planRes = planPathV2Simplified(
 481:     snapshot.botPosition,
 482:     goals,
 483:     inflatedObstacles,
 484:     snapshot.navigationBounds,
 485:     snapshot.navigationBounds,
 486:     BOT_CONFIG_V2.plannerLocalMaxNodes,
 487:     snapshot
 488:   );
 489: 
 490:   if (planRes.path && planRes.path.length > 0) {
 491:     context.currentPath = simplifyPath(planRes.path);
 492:     context.currentPathType = planRes.outcome === 'PARTIAL_PROGRESS' ? 'PARTIAL' : 'FULL';
 493:     context.pathIndex = 0;
 494:     context.currentPathGeneratedAtMs = snapshot.simTimeMs;
 495:     context.pathTargetVersion = context.targetVersion;
 496:     context.currentGoalAnchor = planRes.selectedGoal ? { ...planRes.selectedGoal.worldPosition } : { ...snapshot.playerPosition };
 497:     context.progress.plannerFailures = 0;
 498:     context.debug.plannerStatus = planRes.outcome;
 499:   } else {
 500:     context.progress.plannerFailures++;
 501:     context.debug.plannerStatus = planRes.outcome;
 502:     context.currentState = 'RECOVER';
 503:     context.progress.recoveryStartTimeMs = snapshot.simTimeMs;
 504:   }
 505: }


================================================================================
FILE: src/games/circuit-climb/bot-ai-v2/BotControllerV2.ts

   1: import { BotWorldSnapshotV2, BotStateContextV2, BotUpdateResultV2, Vec2 } from './BotTypesV2';
   2: import { BOT_CONFIG_V2 } from './BotConfigV2';
   3: import { updateStateMachine, inflateObstacles } from './BotStateMachineV2';
   4: import { executeRecovery } from './BotRecoveryV2';
   5: import { getEdgeGap } from './BotSensingV2';

   9: export function createBotContextV2(): BotStateContextV2 {
  10:   searchSeq = 0;
  11:   
  12:   // Log V2 Context Creation
  13:   BotFlightRecorder.getInstance().recordEvent(
  14:     0,
  15:     'GREENFIELD_V2',
  16:     'SEARCH',
  17:     'NONE',
  18:     'LIFECYCLE',
  19:     'V2_CONTEXT_CREATED',
  20:     `Greenfield V2 AI state controller context initialized.`,
  21:     { x: 0, y: 0 },
  22:     { x: 0, y: 0 },
  23:     BOT_CONFIG_V2.gridSize,
  24:     BOT_CONFIG_V2.gridSize,
  25:     0,
  26:     0,
  27:     '',
  28:     0
  29:   );
  30: 
  31:   return {
  32:     currentState: 'SEARCH',
  33:     awareness: null,
  34:     radarTimerMs: 0,
  35:     alertCooldownUntilMs: 0,
  36:     currentPath: null,
  37:     currentPathType: null,
  38:     currentPathGeneratedAtMs: 0,
  39:     currentGoalAnchor: null,
  40:     pathIndex: 0,
  41:     searchDestination: null,
  42:     progress: {
  43:       lastPos: { x: 0, y: 0 },
  44:       lastPosTimeMs: 0,
  45:       lastWaypointIndex: 0,
  46:       waypointStallTimeMs: 0,
  47:       recentCells: [],
  48:       plannerFailures: 0,
  49:       recoveryRung: 0,
  50:       recoveryStartTimeMs: 0
  51:     },
  52:     attackSubState: 'NONE',
  53:     stageTimerMs: 0,
  54:     strikeTarget: null,
  55:     events: [],
  56:     targetVersion: 1,
  57:     pathTargetVersion: 0,
  58:     lastTargetPosition: null,
  59:     lastPlayerPlatformId: null,
  60:     debug: {
  61:       implementation: 'GREENFIELD_V2',
  62:       state: 'SEARCH',
  63:       nearHit: false,
  64:       radarHit: false,
  65:       edgeGap: 0,
  66:       radarRadius: 0,
  67:       awarenessId: 0,
  68:       awarenessRemainingMs: 0,
  69:       excitementPlayed: false,
  70:       plannerStage: 'NONE',
  71:       plannerStatus: '',
  72:       nodesExpanded: 0,
  73:       pathLength: 0,
  74:       recoveryRung: 0,
  75:       timeInRecoveryMs: 0,
  76:       goalCandidates: [],
  77:       selectedGoal: null,
  78:       currentPath: null,
  79:       activeWaypoint: null,
  80:       inflatedObstacles: [],
  81:       navBounds: { left: 0, right: 0, top: 0, bottom: 0 },
  82:       radarWaveRadius: null
  83:     }
  84:   };
  85: }

  89: export function updateBotV2(snapshot: BotWorldSnapshotV2, context: BotStateContextV2): BotUpdateResultV2 {
  90:   return updateBotV2Simplified(snapshot, context);
  91: }
  92: 
  93: export function updateBotV2Frozen(snapshot: BotWorldSnapshotV2, context: BotStateContextV2): BotUpdateResultV2 {
  94:   const recorder = BotFlightRecorder.getInstance();
  95:   
  96:   if (snapshot.paused) {
  97:     return { intendedDisplacement: { x: 0, y: 0 }, events: [], debug: context.debug };
  98:   }
  99: 
 100:   context.events = [];
 101:   context.radarTimerMs += snapshot.deltaMs;
 102: 
 103:   updateStateMachine(snapshot, context);
 104: 
 105:   if (context.currentState === 'RECOVER') {
 106:     executeRecovery(snapshot, context, inflateObstacles(snapshot));
 107:   }
 108: 
 109:   let dx = 0;
 110:   let dy = 0;
 111: 
 112:   if (context.currentState === 'CHASE' && context.attackSubState === 'STAGE') {
 113:     // STAGE: Stabilize beneath player, zero displacement
 114:     dx = 0;
 115:     dy = 0;
 116:   } else if (context.currentState === 'CHASE' && context.attackSubState === 'STRIKE') {
 117:     // STRIKE: Fast committed upward strike directly to strikeTarget
 118:     const strikeSpeed = 350; // px/s
 119:     const strikeDist = strikeSpeed * (snapshot.deltaMs / 1000);
 120:     const sdx = context.strikeTarget!.x - snapshot.botPosition.x;
 121:     const sdy = context.strikeTarget!.y - snapshot.botPosition.y;
 122:     const sdist = Math.sqrt(sdx * sdx + sdy * sdy);
 123:     if (sdist > 0) {
 124:       dx = (sdx / sdist) * strikeDist;
 125:       dy = (sdy / sdist) * strikeDist;
 126:     }
 127:   } else if (context.currentState === 'SEARCH') {
 128:     const speed = BOT_CONFIG_V2.patrolSpeed;
 129:     const dist = speed * (snapshot.deltaMs / 1000);
 130:     if (!context.searchDestination) {
 131:        const w = snapshot.navigationBounds.right - snapshot.navigationBounds.left;
 132:        const targets = [
 133:          snapshot.navigationBounds.left + w * 0.2,
 134:          snapshot.navigationBounds.left + w * 0.8,
 135:          snapshot.navigationBounds.left + w * 0.5
 136:        ];
 137:        searchSeq = (searchSeq + 1) % targets.length;
 138:        context.searchDestination = { x: targets[searchSeq], y: snapshot.botPosition.y };
 139:     } else {
 140:        const pdx = context.searchDestination.x - snapshot.botPosition.x;
 141:        if (Math.abs(pdx) < BOT_CONFIG_V2.waypointArrivalPx) {
 142:           context.searchDestination = null;
 143:        } else {
 144:           dx = Math.sign(pdx) * dist;
 145:        }
 146:     }
 147:   } else if ((context.currentState === 'CHASE' || context.currentState === 'HOLD' || context.currentState === 'RECOVER') && context.currentPath) {
 148:     const speed = BOT_CONFIG_V2.chaseSpeed;
 149:     const dist = speed * (snapshot.deltaMs / 1000);
 150:     let targetWp = context.currentPath[context.pathIndex];
 151:     if (targetWp) {
 152:       const wdx = targetWp.x - snapshot.botPosition.x;
 153:       const wdy = targetWp.y - snapshot.botPosition.y;
 154:       const wdist = Math.sqrt(wdx*wdx + wdy*wdy);
 155:       if (wdist <= BOT_CONFIG_V2.waypointArrivalPx) {
 156:         const oldIndex = context.pathIndex;
 157:         context.pathIndex++;
 158: 
 159:         recorder.recordEvent(
 160:           snapshot.simTimeMs,
 161:           'GREENFIELD_V2',
 162:           context.currentState,
 163:           'NONE',
 164:           'MOVEMENT',
 165:           'WAYPOINT_REACHED',
 166:           `Bot arrived at waypoint index ${oldIndex} (${targetWp.x.toFixed(0)}, ${targetWp.y.toFixed(0)}) within arrival tolerance ${BOT_CONFIG_V2.waypointArrivalPx}px`,
 167:           snapshot.botPosition,
 168:           snapshot.playerPosition,
 169:           snapshot.botRadius,
 170:           snapshot.playerRadius,
 171:           context.awareness?.id || 0,
 172:           snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 173:           context.debug.plannerStatus,
 174:           context.pathIndex,
 175:           { waypointIndex: oldIndex, coordinate: targetWp }
 176:         );
 177: 
 178:         if (context.pathIndex < context.currentPath.length) {
 179:           targetWp = context.currentPath[context.pathIndex];
 180:         }
 181:       }
 182:       
 183:       if (context.pathIndex < context.currentPath.length && targetWp) {
 184:         const ndx = targetWp.x - snapshot.botPosition.x;
 185:         const ndy = targetWp.y - snapshot.botPosition.y;
 186:         const ndist = Math.sqrt(ndx*ndx + ndy*ndy);
 187:         if (ndist > 0) {
 188:           dx = (ndx / ndist) * dist;
 189:           dy = (ndy / ndist) * dist;
 190:         }
 191:       }
 192:     }
 193:   }
 194: 
 195:   // Log zero movement intent if calculated in active states
 196:   if ((context.currentState === 'CHASE' || context.currentState === 'HOLD' || context.currentState === 'RECOVER') && dx === 0 && dy === 0 && context.attackSubState !== 'STAGE') {
 197:     recorder.recordEvent(
 198:       snapshot.simTimeMs,
 199:       'GREENFIELD_V2',
 200:       context.currentState,
 201:       'NONE',
 202:       'MOVEMENT',
 203:       'ZERO_MOVEMENT_INTENT',
 204:       `Zero movement intent calculated during active state ${context.currentState}. Waypoint index: ${context.pathIndex}/${context.currentPath?.length || 0}`,
 205:       snapshot.botPosition,
 206:       snapshot.playerPosition,
 207:       snapshot.botRadius,
 208:       snapshot.playerRadius,
 209:       context.awareness?.id || 0,
 210:       snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 211:       context.debug.plannerStatus,
 212:       context.pathIndex
 213:     );
 214:   }
 215: 
 216:   context.debug.state = context.currentState;
 217:   context.debug.edgeGap = getEdgeGap(snapshot);
 218:   context.debug.awarenessId = context.awareness?.id || 0;
 219:   context.debug.awarenessRemainingMs = context.awareness ? 2500 - (snapshot.simTimeMs - context.awareness.lastConfirmedAtMs) : 0;
 220:   context.debug.excitementPlayed = context.awareness?.excitementPlayed || false;
 221:   context.debug.currentPath = context.currentPath;
 222:   context.debug.pathLength = context.currentPath?.length || 0;
 223:   context.debug.activeWaypoint = context.currentPath ? context.currentPath[context.pathIndex] : null;
 224:   context.debug.inflatedObstacles = inflateObstacles(snapshot);
 225:   context.debug.navBounds = snapshot.navigationBounds;
 226:   
 227:   const cycleTime = context.radarTimerMs % 1500;
 228:   context.debug.radarWaveRadius = cycleTime <= BOT_CONFIG_V2.radarDurationMs ? (cycleTime / BOT_CONFIG_V2.radarDurationMs) * 280 : null;
 229: 
 230:   return {
 231:     intendedDisplacement: { x: dx, y: dy },
 232:     events: [...context.events],
 233:     debug: { ...context.debug }
 234:   };
 235: }

FILE: src/games/circuit-climb/bot-ai-v2/BotControllerV2Simplified.ts
================================================================================
   1: import { BotWorldSnapshotV2, BotStateContextV2, BotUpdateResultV2, isBotInsideAttackEnvelope, DEFAULT_ATTACK_ENVELOPE_CONFIG_V2 } from './BotTypesV2';
   2: import { BOT_CONFIG_V2 } from './BotConfigV2';
   3: import { updateStateMachineSimplified, inflateObstacles } from './BotStateMachineV2Simplified';
   4: import { executeRecovery } from './BotRecoveryV2';
   5: import { getEdgeGap } from './BotSensingV2';
   6: import { isPointInPlayerTransitCorridor } from './BotGoalSelectorV2';
   7: import { BotFlightRecorder } from './BotFlightRecorderV2';
   8: 
   9: let searchSeq = 0;
  10: let lastCalculatedMovementMagnitude = 0;
  11: 
  12: export function updateBotV2Simplified(
  13:   snapshot: BotWorldSnapshotV2,
  14:   context: BotStateContextV2
  15: ): BotUpdateResultV2 {
  16:   const recorder = BotFlightRecorder.getInstance();
  17: 
  18:   if (snapshot.paused) {
  19:     return { intendedDisplacement: { x: 0, y: 0 }, events: [], debug: context.debug };
  20:   }
  21: 
  22:   context.events = [];
  23:   context.radarTimerMs += snapshot.deltaMs;
  24: 
  25:   // Run simplified state machine
  26:   updateStateMachineSimplified(snapshot, context, lastCalculatedMovementMagnitude);
  27: 
  28:   let dx = 0;
  29:   let dy = 0;
  30: 
  31:   // Calculate intended movement displacement based on current state
  32:   if (context.currentState === 'SEARCH') {
  33:     const speed = BOT_CONFIG_V2.patrolSpeed;
  34:     const dist = speed * (snapshot.deltaMs / 1000);
  35:     if (!context.searchDestination) {
  36:       const w = snapshot.navigationBounds.right - snapshot.navigationBounds.left;
  37:       const targets = [
  38:         snapshot.navigationBounds.left + w * 0.2,
  39:         snapshot.navigationBounds.left + w * 0.8,
  40:         snapshot.navigationBounds.left + w * 0.5
  41:       ];
  42:       searchSeq = (searchSeq + 1) % targets.length;
  43:       context.searchDestination = { x: targets[searchSeq], y: snapshot.botPosition.y };
  44:     } else {
  45:       const pdx = context.searchDestination.x - snapshot.botPosition.x;
  46:       if (Math.abs(pdx) < BOT_CONFIG_V2.waypointArrivalPx) {
  47:         context.searchDestination = null;
  48:       } else {
  49:         dx = Math.sign(pdx) * dist;
  50:       }
  51:     }
  52:   } else if (context.currentState === 'PURSUE' && context.currentPath) {
  53:     const speed = BOT_CONFIG_V2.chaseSpeed;
  54:     const dist = speed * (snapshot.deltaMs / 1000);
  55:     let targetWp = context.currentPath[context.pathIndex];
  56: 
  57:     if (targetWp) {
  58:       const wdx = targetWp.x - snapshot.botPosition.x;
  59:       const wdy = targetWp.y - snapshot.botPosition.y;
  60:       const wdist = Math.hypot(wdx, wdy);
  61: 
  62:       if (wdist <= BOT_CONFIG_V2.waypointArrivalPx) {
  63:         context.pathIndex++;
  64:         if (context.pathIndex < context.currentPath.length) {
  65:           targetWp = context.currentPath[context.pathIndex];
  66:         }
  67:       }
  68: 
  69:       if (context.pathIndex < context.currentPath.length && targetWp) {
  70:         const ndx = targetWp.x - snapshot.botPosition.x;
  71:         const ndy = targetWp.y - snapshot.botPosition.y;
  72:         const ndist = Math.hypot(ndx, ndy);
  73:         if (ndist > 0) {
  74:           dx = (ndx / ndist) * dist;
  75:           dy = (ndy / ndist) * dist;
  76:         }
  77:       }
  78:     }
  79:   } else if (context.currentState === 'FINAL_APPROACH') {
  80:     // Local steering controller directly targeting (player.x, player.y + 160) with vertical bias
  81:     const speed = BOT_CONFIG_V2.chaseSpeed;
  82:     const distStep = speed * (snapshot.deltaMs / 1000);
  83: 
  84:     const targetX = snapshot.playerPosition.x;
  85:     const targetY = snapshot.playerPosition.y + DEFAULT_ATTACK_ENVELOPE_CONFIG_V2.envelopeTargetBelowPx;
  86: 
  87:     const rawDx = targetX - snapshot.botPosition.x;
  88:     const rawDy = targetY - snapshot.botPosition.y;
  89: 
  90:     // Apply vertical bias factor (~2.0)
  91:     let steerX = rawDx;
  92:     let steerY = rawDy * 2.0;
  93: 
  94:     // Add corridor repulsion if player is in transit
  95:     if (isPointInPlayerTransitCorridor(snapshot.botPosition, snapshot)) {
  96:       const repelDir = snapshot.botPosition.x >= snapshot.playerPosition.x ? 1 : -1;
  97:       steerX += repelDir * BOT_CONFIG_V2.corridorRepulsionGain * 30;
  98:     }
  99: 
 100:     const steerDist = Math.hypot(steerX, steerY);
 101:     if (steerDist > 0) {
 102:       dx = (steerX / steerDist) * distStep;
 103:       dy = (steerY / steerDist) * distStep;
 104:     }
 105:   } else if (context.currentState === 'RECOVER' && context.currentPath) {
 106:     const speed = BOT_CONFIG_V2.chaseSpeed;
 107:     const dist = speed * (snapshot.deltaMs / 1000);
 108:     const targetWp = context.currentPath[context.pathIndex];
 109: 
 110:     if (targetWp) {
 111:       const rdx = targetWp.x - snapshot.botPosition.x;
 112:       const rdy = targetWp.y - snapshot.botPosition.y;
 113:       const rdist = Math.hypot(rdx, rdy);
 114:       if (rdist > 0) {
 115:         dx = (rdx / rdist) * dist;
 116:         dy = (rdy / rdist) * dist;
 117:       }
 118:     }
 119:   }
 120: 
 121:   lastCalculatedMovementMagnitude = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
 122: 
 123:   // Debug snapshot
 124:   context.debug.state = context.currentState;
 125:   context.debug.edgeGap = getEdgeGap(snapshot);
 126:   context.debug.awarenessId = context.awareness?.id || 0;
 127:   context.debug.currentPath = context.currentPath;
 128:   context.debug.pathLength = context.currentPath?.length || 0;
 129:   context.debug.activeWaypoint = context.currentPath ? context.currentPath[context.pathIndex] : null;
 130:   context.debug.inflatedObstacles = inflateObstacles(snapshot);
 131:   context.debug.navBounds = snapshot.navigationBounds;
 132: 
 133:   return {
 134:     intendedDisplacement: { x: dx, y: dy },
 135:     events: [...context.events],
 136:     debug: { ...context.debug }
 137:   };
 138: }

   1: import { BotProgressV2, BotWorldSnapshotV2, BotStateContextV2, isBotInsideAttackEnvelope, Rect } from './BotTypesV2';

   6: export function checkProgressV2(
   7:   snapshot: BotWorldSnapshotV2,
   8:   context: BotStateContextV2,
   9:   intendedMovementMagnitude: number,
  10:   inflatedObstacles: Rect[] = []
  11: ): boolean {
  12:   if (context.currentState !== 'PURSUE' && context.currentState !== 'FINAL_APPROACH' && context.currentState !== 'CHASE' && context.currentState !== 'HOLD') {
  13:     return true; // only monitor active states
  14:   }
  15: 
  16:   const prog = context.progress;
  17:   const recorder = BotFlightRecorder.getInstance();
  18: 
  19:   if (context.attackSubState === 'STAGE' || context.attackSubState === 'STRIKE') {
  20:     prog.lastPos = { ...snapshot.botPosition };
  21:     prog.lastPosTimeMs = snapshot.simTimeMs;
  22:     prog.waypointStallTimeMs = snapshot.simTimeMs;
  23:     return true;
  24:   }
  25: 
  26:   // INVARIANT 6 / Section 10: SEMANTIC_ZERO_MOVEMENT
  27:   // If movement intent is zero and bot is NOT in attack envelope, trigger immediate failure!
  28:   const insideEnvelope = isBotInsideAttackEnvelope(snapshot.botPosition, snapshot.playerPosition, inflatedObstacles).isInside;
  29:   if (intendedMovementMagnitude === 0 && !insideEnvelope) {
  30:     recorder.recordEvent(
  31:       snapshot.simTimeMs,
  32:       'GREENFIELD_V2',
  33:       context.currentState,
  34:       'NONE',
  35:       'PROGRESS_RECOVERY',
  36:       'SEMANTIC_ZERO_MOVEMENT_DETECTED',
  37:       `Semantic Zero Movement: Bot movement intent magnitude is zero while outside attack envelope in state ${context.currentState}. Triggering immediate recovery.`,
  38:       snapshot.botPosition,
  39:       snapshot.playerPosition,
  40:       snapshot.botRadius,
  41:       snapshot.playerRadius,
  42:       context.awareness?.id || 0,
  43:       snapshot.obstacleRevision,
  44:       context.debug.plannerStatus,
  45:       context.pathIndex,
  46:       { intendedMovementMagnitude, insideEnvelope }
  47:     );
  48:     return false; // IMMEDIATE FAULT!
  49:   }
  50: 
  51:   const timeSinceLast = snapshot.simTimeMs - prog.lastPosTimeMs;
  52: 
  53:   if (timeSinceLast >= BOT_CONFIG_V2.monitorNoProgressWindowMs) {
  54:     const dx = snapshot.botPosition.x - prog.lastPos.x;
  55:     const dy = snapshot.botPosition.y - prog.lastPos.y;
  56:     const distMoved = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
  57: 
  58:     if (distMoved < BOT_CONFIG_V2.monitorNoProgressDistPx) {
  59:       recorder.recordEvent(
  60:         snapshot.simTimeMs,
  61:         'GREENFIELD_V2',
  62:         context.currentState,
  63:         'NONE',
  64:         'PROGRESS_RECOVERY',
  65:         'NO_PROGRESS_DETECTED',
  66:         `No forward progress: Moved only ${distMoved.toFixed(1)}px (limit: ${BOT_CONFIG_V2.monitorNoProgressDistPx}px) in window of ${timeSinceLast}ms`,
  67:         snapshot.botPosition,
  68:         snapshot.playerPosition,
  69:         snapshot.botRadius,
  70:         snapshot.playerRadius,
  71:         context.awareness?.id || 0,
  72:         snapshot.obstacleRevision,
  73:         context.debug.plannerStatus,
  74:         context.pathIndex,
  75:         { distMoved, windowMs: timeSinceLast }
  76:       );
  77:       return false; // NO PROGRESS
  78:     }
  79: 
  80:     prog.lastPos = { ...snapshot.botPosition };
  81:     prog.lastPosTimeMs = snapshot.simTimeMs;
  82:   }
  83: 
  84:   // Waypoint stall
  85:   if (context.pathIndex !== prog.lastWaypointIndex) {
  86:     prog.lastWaypointIndex = context.pathIndex;
  87:     prog.waypointStallTimeMs = snapshot.simTimeMs;
  88:   } else {
  89:     const stallTime = snapshot.simTimeMs - prog.waypointStallTimeMs;
  90:     if (stallTime >= BOT_CONFIG_V2.monitorWaypointStallMs) {
  91:       recorder.recordEvent(
  92:         snapshot.simTimeMs,
  93:         'GREENFIELD_V2',
  94:         context.currentState,
  95:         'NONE',
  96:         'PROGRESS_RECOVERY',
  97:         'WAYPOINT_STALL_DETECTED',
  98:         `Waypoint stall: Bot stuck on waypoint index ${context.pathIndex} for ${stallTime}ms (limit: ${BOT_CONFIG_V2.monitorWaypointStallMs}ms)`,
  99:         snapshot.botPosition,
 100:         snapshot.playerPosition,
 101:         snapshot.botRadius,
 102:         snapshot.playerRadius,
 103:         context.awareness?.id || 0,
 104:         snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 105:         context.debug.plannerStatus,
 106:         context.pathIndex,
 107:         { waypointIndex: context.pathIndex, stallTimeMs: stallTime }
 108:       );
 109:       return false; // WAYPOINT STALL
 110:     }
 111:   }
 112: 
 113:   // Two cell oscillation
 114:   const currentCell = `${snapToGrid(snapshot.botPosition.x)},${snapToGrid(snapshot.botPosition.y)}`;
 115:   if (prog.recentCells[prog.recentCells.length - 1] !== currentCell) {
 116:     prog.recentCells.push(currentCell);
 117:     if (prog.recentCells.length > 5) prog.recentCells.shift();
 118:     
 119:     if (prog.recentCells.length >= 4) {
 120:       const c1 = prog.recentCells[prog.recentCells.length - 1];
 121:       const c2 = prog.recentCells[prog.recentCells.length - 2];
 122:       const c3 = prog.recentCells[prog.recentCells.length - 3];
 123:       const c4 = prog.recentCells[prog.recentCells.length - 4];
 124:       if (c1 === c3 && c2 === c4 && c1 !== c2) {
 125:         recorder.recordEvent(
 126:           snapshot.simTimeMs,
 127:           'GREENFIELD_V2',
 128:           context.currentState,
 129:           'NONE',
 130:           'PROGRESS_RECOVERY',
 131:           'TWO_CELL_OSCILLATION_DETECTED',
 132:           `Two-cell oscillation: Bot trapped in ping-pong loop between ${c1} and ${c2}`,
 133:           snapshot.botPosition,
 134:           snapshot.playerPosition,
 135:           snapshot.botRadius,
 136:           snapshot.playerRadius,
 137:           context.awareness?.id || 0,
 138:           snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 139:           context.debug.plannerStatus,
 140:           context.pathIndex,
 141:           { recentCells: [...prog.recentCells] }
 142:         );
 143:         return false; // OSCILLATION
 144:       }
 145:     }
 146:   }
 147: 
 148:   return true;
 149: }
 150: 
 151: export function checkProgress(snapshot: BotWorldSnapshotV2, context: BotStateContextV2): boolean {
 152:   if (context.currentState !== 'CHASE' && context.currentState !== 'HOLD') {
 153:     return true; // only monitor during active pathing
 154:   }
 155: 
 156:   const prog = context.progress;
 157: 
 158:   if (context.attackSubState === 'STAGE' || context.attackSubState === 'STRIKE') {
 159:     prog.lastPos = { ...snapshot.botPosition };
 160:     prog.lastPosTimeMs = snapshot.simTimeMs;
 161:     prog.waypointStallTimeMs = snapshot.simTimeMs;
 162:     return true;
 163:   }
 164: 
 165:   const recorder = BotFlightRecorder.getInstance();
 166:   const timeSinceLast = snapshot.simTimeMs - prog.lastPosTimeMs;
 167: 
 168:   if (timeSinceLast >= BOT_CONFIG_V2.monitorNoProgressWindowMs) {
 169:     const dx = snapshot.botPosition.x - prog.lastPos.x;
 170:     const dy = snapshot.botPosition.y - prog.lastPos.y;
 171:     const distMoved = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
 172: 
 173:     if (distMoved < BOT_CONFIG_V2.monitorNoProgressDistPx) {
 174:       recorder.recordEvent(
 175:         snapshot.simTimeMs,
 176:         'GREENFIELD_V2',
 177:         context.currentState,
 178:         'NONE',
 179:         'PROGRESS_RECOVERY',
 180:         'NO_PROGRESS_DETECTED',
 181:         `No forward progress: Moved only ${distMoved.toFixed(1)}px (limit: ${BOT_CONFIG_V2.monitorNoProgressDistPx}px) in window of ${timeSinceLast}ms`,
 182:         snapshot.botPosition,
 183:         snapshot.playerPosition,
 184:         snapshot.botRadius,
 185:         snapshot.playerRadius,
 186:         context.awareness?.id || 0,
 187:         snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 188:         context.debug.plannerStatus,
 189:         context.pathIndex,
 190:         { distMoved, windowMs: timeSinceLast }
 191:       );
 192:       return false; // NO PROGRESS
 193:     }
 194: 
 195:     prog.lastPos = { ...snapshot.botPosition };
 196:     prog.lastPosTimeMs = snapshot.simTimeMs;
 197:   }
 198: 
 199:   // Waypoint stall
 200:   if (context.pathIndex !== prog.lastWaypointIndex) {
 201:     prog.lastWaypointIndex = context.pathIndex;
 202:     prog.waypointStallTimeMs = snapshot.simTimeMs;
 203:   } else {
 204:     const stallTime = snapshot.simTimeMs - prog.waypointStallTimeMs;
 205:     if (stallTime >= BOT_CONFIG_V2.monitorWaypointStallMs) {
 206:       recorder.recordEvent(
 207:         snapshot.simTimeMs,
 208:         'GREENFIELD_V2',
 209:         context.currentState,
 210:         'NONE',
 211:         'PROGRESS_RECOVERY',
 212:         'WAYPOINT_STALL_DETECTED',
 213:         `Waypoint stall: Bot stuck on waypoint index ${context.pathIndex} for ${stallTime}ms (limit: ${BOT_CONFIG_V2.monitorWaypointStallMs}ms)`,
 214:         snapshot.botPosition,
 215:         snapshot.playerPosition,
 216:         snapshot.botRadius,
 217:         snapshot.playerRadius,
 218:         context.awareness?.id || 0,
 219:         snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 220:         context.debug.plannerStatus,
 221:         context.pathIndex,
 222:         { waypointIndex: context.pathIndex, stallTimeMs: stallTime }
 223:       );
 224:       return false; // WAYPOINT STALL
 225:     }
 226:   }
 227: 
 228:   // Two cell oscillation
 229:   const currentCell = `${snapToGrid(snapshot.botPosition.x)},${snapToGrid(snapshot.botPosition.y)}`;
 230:   if (prog.recentCells[prog.recentCells.length - 1] !== currentCell) {
 231:     prog.recentCells.push(currentCell);
 232:     if (prog.recentCells.length > 5) prog.recentCells.shift();
 233:     
 234:     // Check if oscillating between two cells
 235:     if (prog.recentCells.length >= 4) {
 236:       const c1 = prog.recentCells[prog.recentCells.length - 1];
 237:       const c2 = prog.recentCells[prog.recentCells.length - 2];
 238:       const c3 = prog.recentCells[prog.recentCells.length - 3];
 239:       const c4 = prog.recentCells[prog.recentCells.length - 4];
 240:       if (c1 === c3 && c2 === c4 && c1 !== c2) {
 241:         recorder.recordEvent(
 242:           snapshot.simTimeMs,
 243:           'GREENFIELD_V2',
 244:           context.currentState,
 245:           'NONE',
 246:           'PROGRESS_RECOVERY',
 247:           'TWO_CELL_OSCILLATION_DETECTED',
 248:           `Two-cell oscillation: Bot trapped in ping-pong loop between ${c1} and ${c2}`,
 249:           snapshot.botPosition,
 250:           snapshot.playerPosition,
 251:           snapshot.botRadius,
 252:           snapshot.playerRadius,
 253:           context.awareness?.id || 0,
 254:           snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 255:           context.debug.plannerStatus,
 256:           context.pathIndex,
 257:           { recentCells: [...prog.recentCells] }
 258:         );
 259:         return false; // OSCILLATION
 260:       }
 261:     }
 262:   }
 263: 
 264:   // Thrash is handled in state machine
 265: 
 266:   return true;
 267: }


================================================================================
FILE: src/games/circuit-climb/bot-ai-v2/BotRecoveryV2.ts
================================================================================
   1: import { BotWorldSnapshotV2, BotStateContextV2, Vec2, Rect } from './BotTypesV2';
   2: import { BOT_CONFIG_V2 } from './BotConfigV2';
   3: import { snapToGrid, isCellBlocked } from './BotGoalSelectorV2';
   4: import { BotFlightRecorder } from './BotFlightRecorderV2';
   5: 
   6: export function executeRecovery(snapshot: BotWorldSnapshotV2, context: BotStateContextV2, inflatedObstacles: Rect[]): void {
   7:   const recorder = BotFlightRecorder.getInstance();
   8:   
   9:   // We're inside RECOVER state. Recovery ladder:
  10:   // Rung 1: Discard path, expanded stage 2 planning. (Handled in transition to RECOVER -> StateMachine checks if replan possible)
  11:   // Actually, state machine will try to transition to CHASE if replanning works.
  12:   // If we are in RECOVER, it means replanning failed or progress failed.
  13:   // We should just move to an escape cell (Rung 2/3).
  14:   
  15:   if (snapshot.simTimeMs - context.progress.recoveryStartTimeMs > BOT_CONFIG_V2.recoveryMaxDurationMs) {
  16:     // Rung 4: Fail completely
  17:     context.awareness = null;
  18:     context.currentState = 'SEARCH';
  19:     context.alertCooldownUntilMs = snapshot.simTimeMs + BOT_CONFIG_V2.alertCooldownMs;
  20:     context.currentPath = null;
  21:     context.currentPathType = null;
  22: 
  23:     recorder.recordEvent(
  24:       snapshot.simTimeMs,
  25:       'GREENFIELD_V2',
  26:       context.currentState,
  27:       'RECOVER',
  28:       'PROGRESS_RECOVERY',
  29:       'RECOVERY_EXHAUSTED',
  30:       `Recovery timer exceeded max duration of ${BOT_CONFIG_V2.recoveryMaxDurationMs}ms. Rung 4: Force clearing awareness and entering SEARCH cooldown.`,
  31:       snapshot.botPosition,
  32:       snapshot.playerPosition,
  33:       snapshot.botRadius,
  34:       snapshot.playerRadius,
  35:       0,
  36:       snapshot.obstacleRevision,
  37:       context.debug.plannerStatus,
  38:       context.pathIndex
  39:     );
  40:     return;
  41:   }
  42: 
  43:   // Generate escape cell (Rung 2)
  44:   if (!context.currentPath) {
  45:     recorder.recordEvent(
  46:       snapshot.simTimeMs,
  47:       'GREENFIELD_V2',
  48:       context.currentState,
  49:       'NONE',
  50:       'PROGRESS_RECOVERY',
  51:       'RECOVERY_RUNG_2_STARTED',
  52:       `Rung 1 (replanning) failed to recover. Starting Rung 2: Search for adjacent unblocked escape cell.`,
  53:       snapshot.botPosition,
  54:       snapshot.playerPosition,
  55:       snapshot.botRadius,
  56:       snapshot.playerRadius,
  57:       context.awareness?.id || 0,
  58:       snapshot.obstacleRevision,
  59:       context.debug.plannerStatus,
  60:       context.pathIndex
  61:     );
  62: 
  63:     const startX = snapToGrid(snapshot.botPosition.x);
  64:     const startY = snapToGrid(snapshot.botPosition.y);
  65:     const gs = BOT_CONFIG_V2.gridSize;
  66:     const dirs = [ {dx: -gs, dy: 0}, {dx: gs, dy: 0}, {dx: 0, dy: -gs}, {dx: 0, dy: gs} ];
  67:     let foundCell = false;
  68:     
  69:     for (const d of dirs) {
  70:       const nx = startX + d.dx;
  71:       const ny = startY + d.dy;
  72:       const nCell = `${nx},${ny}`;
  73:       if (!context.progress.recentCells.includes(nCell) && !isCellBlocked(nx, ny, inflatedObstacles, snapshot.navigationBounds, snapshot)) {
  74:         context.currentPath = [{ x: nx, y: ny }];
  75:         context.pathIndex = 0;
  76:         context.currentPathType = 'PARTIAL';
  77:         foundCell = true;
  78: 
  79:         recorder.recordEvent(
  80:           snapshot.simTimeMs,
  81:           'GREENFIELD_V2',
  82:           context.currentState,
  83:           'NONE',
  84:           'PROGRESS_RECOVERY',
  85:           'RECOVERY_ESCAPE_CELL_FOUND',
  86:           `Rung 2: Escape cell found at neighboring grid cell (${nx}, ${ny}). Pathing there.`,
  87:           snapshot.botPosition,
  88:           snapshot.playerPosition,
  89:           snapshot.botRadius,
  90:           snapshot.playerRadius,
  91:           context.awareness?.id || 0,
  92:           snapshot.obstacleRevision,
  93:           context.debug.plannerStatus,
  94:           context.pathIndex,
  95:           { cell: nCell }
  96:         );
  97:         break;
  98:       }
  99:     }
 100: 
 101:     if (!foundCell) {
 102:       recorder.recordEvent(
 103:         snapshot.simTimeMs,
 104:         'GREENFIELD_V2',
 105:         context.currentState,
 106:         'NONE',
 107:         'PROGRESS_RECOVERY',
 108:         'RECOVERY_RUNG_3_STARTED',
 109:         `Rung 2 failed: All adjacent grid cells are either blocked or recently visited. Initiating Rung 3 (Backtracking fallback).`,
 110:         snapshot.botPosition,
 111:         snapshot.playerPosition,
 112:         snapshot.botRadius,
 113:         snapshot.playerRadius,
 114:         context.awareness?.id || 0,
 115:         snapshot.obstacleRevision,
 116:         context.debug.plannerStatus,
 117:         context.pathIndex
 118:       );
 119:     }
 120:   }
 121: 
 122:   // If we have an escape path, just follow it. It's essentially HOLD behavior but in RECOVER.
 123: }


================================================================================
FILE: src/games/circuit-climb/bot-ai-v2/BotFlightRecorderV2.ts
================================================================================
   1: /**
   2:  * @license
   3:  * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
   4:  */
   5: 
   6: import { BotStateV2, Vec2 } from './BotTypesV2';
   7: import { CircuitClimbUnifiedRecorder, DiagnosticSource } from './CircuitClimbUnifiedRecorder';
   8: 
   9: export interface FlightRecordEvent {
  10:   seq: number;
  11:   simTimeMs: number;
  12:   realTimeMs: number;
  13:   activeAi: 'GREENFIELD_V2' | 'LEGACY';
  14:   currentState: BotStateV2;
  15:   previousState: BotStateV2 | 'NONE';
  16:   category: 'LIFECYCLE' | 'FRAME_INVARIANTS' | 'SEARCH' | 'SENSING' | 'AWARENESS' | 'STATE_MACHINE' | 'GOAL_SELECTION' | 'PLANNING' | 'MOVEMENT' | 'PROGRESS_RECOVERY' | 'ERROR';
  17:   eventName: string;
  18:   triggerReason: string;
  19:   botPos: Vec2;
  20:   playerPos: Vec2;
  21:   centerDist: number;
  22:   edgeGap: number;
  23:   awarenessEpisodeId: number;
  24:   targetVersion: number;
  25:   plannerStatus: string;
  26:   waypointIndex: number;
  27:   extraDetails?: Record<string, any>;
  28: }
  29: 
  30: export interface ProjectErrorRecord {
  31:   id: number;
  32:   timestamp: number;
  33:   message: string;
  34:   stack?: string;
  35:   sourceFile?: string;
  36:   line?: number;
  37:   snapshot?: string;
  38: }
  39: 
  40: export class BotFlightRecorder {
  41:   private static instance: BotFlightRecorder | null = null;
  42: 
  43:   public static getInstance(): BotFlightRecorder {
  44:     if (!BotFlightRecorder.instance) {
  45:       BotFlightRecorder.instance = new BotFlightRecorder();
  46:     }
  47:     return BotFlightRecorder.instance;
  48:   }
  49: 
  50:   // Ring Buffer
  51:   private readonly capacity = 1000;
  52:   private events: FlightRecordEvent[] = [];
  53:   private seqCounter = 0;
  54:   private isPaused = false;
  55: 
  56:   // Project Error Capture
  57:   private errors: ProjectErrorRecord[] = [];
  58:   private errorCounter = 0;
  59: 
  60:   // Counters for report summary
  61:   public counters = {
  62:     nearChecks: 0,
  63:     nearHits: 0,
  64:     radarPulses: 0,
  65:     radarHits: 0,
  66:     awarenessEpisodes: 0,
  67:     alertEntries: 0,
  68:     chaseEntries: 0,
  69:     holdEntries: 0,
  70:     recoverEntries: 0,
  71:     fullPlans: 0,
  72:     partialPlans: 0,
  73:     failedPlans: 0,
  74:     zeroMovementIntents: 0,
  75:     noProgressTriggers: 0,
  76:     oscillationTriggers: 0,
  77:     captures: 0,
  78:     legacyUpdatesInV2: 0,
  79:     radarMaxCrossedPlayer: 0,
  80:   };
  81: 
  82:   private constructor() {
  83:     this.setupGlobalErrorHandler();
  84:   }
  85: 
  86:   public recordEvent(
  87:     simTimeMs: number,
  88:     activeAi: 'GREENFIELD_V2' | 'LEGACY',
  89:     currentState: BotStateV2,
  90:     previousState: BotStateV2 | 'NONE',
  91:     category: FlightRecordEvent['category'],
  92:     eventName: string,
  93:     triggerReason: string,
  94:     botPos: Vec2,
  95:     playerPos: Vec2,
  96:     botRadius: number,
  97:     playerRadius: number,
  98:     awarenessEpisodeId: number,
  99:     targetVersion: number,
 100:     plannerStatus: string,
 101:     waypointIndex: number,
 102:     extraDetails?: Record<string, any>
 103:   ): void {
 104:     if (this.isPaused) return;
 105: 
 106:     const dx = playerPos.x - botPos.x;
 107:     const dy = playerPos.y - botPos.y;
 108:     const centerDist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
 109:     const edgeGap = centerDist - botRadius - playerRadius;
 110: 
 111:     this.seqCounter++;
 112: 
 113:     // Increment counters based on event
 114:     this.updateCounters(eventName, extraDetails);
 115: 
 116:     const event: FlightRecordEvent = {
 117:       seq: this.seqCounter,
 118:       simTimeMs,
 119:       realTimeMs: Date.now(),
 120:       activeAi,
 121:       currentState,
 122:       previousState,
 123:       category,
 124:       eventName,
 125:       triggerReason,
 126:       botPos: { ...botPos },
 127:       playerPos: { ...playerPos },
 128:       centerDist,
 129:       edgeGap,
 130:       awarenessEpisodeId,
 131:       targetVersion,
 132:       plannerStatus,
 133:       waypointIndex,
 134:       extraDetails,
 135:     };
 136: 
 137:     this.events.push(event);
 138:     if (this.events.length > this.capacity) {
 139:       this.events.shift();
 140:     }
 141: 
 142:     // Forward to unified recorder
 143:     let source: DiagnosticSource = 'BOT_STATE';
 144:     if (category === 'SEARCH' || category === 'SENSING') source = 'BOT_SENSOR';
 145:     else if (category === 'AWARENESS') source = 'BOT_AWARENESS';
 146:     else if (category === 'STATE_MACHINE') source = 'BOT_STATE';
 147:     else if (category === 'GOAL_SELECTION') source = 'BOT_TARGET';
 148:     else if (category === 'PLANNING') source = 'BOT_PLANNER';
 149:     else if (category === 'MOVEMENT') source = 'BOT_MOVEMENT';
 150:     else if (category === 'PROGRESS_RECOVERY') source = 'BOT_PROGRESS';
 151:     else if (category === 'LIFECYCLE') source = 'RUNTIME';
 152:     else if (category === 'ERROR') source = 'ERROR';
 153: 
 154:     CircuitClimbUnifiedRecorder.getInstance().recordEvent(source, eventName, {
 155:       simTimeMs,
 156:       reason: triggerReason,
 157:       playerPosition: { ...playerPos },
 158:       botPosition: { ...botPos },
 159:       botState: currentState,
 160:       botEngine: activeAi,
 161:       awarenessEpisodeId,
 162:       targetVersion,
 163:       data: extraDetails,
 164:     });
 165:   }
 166: 
 167:   private updateCounters(eventName: string, extraDetails?: Record<string, any>): void {
 168:     if (eventName === 'NEAR_SENSOR_CHECK_SAMPLE') this.counters.nearChecks++;
 169:     if (eventName === 'NEAR_SENSOR_HIT') this.counters.nearHits++;
 170:     if (eventName === 'RADAR_PULSE_STARTED') this.counters.radarPulses++;
 171:     if (eventName === 'RADAR_SENSOR_HIT') this.counters.radarHits++;
 172:     if (eventName === 'AWARENESS_EPISODE_OPENED') this.counters.awarenessEpisodes++;
 173:     if (eventName === 'ALERT_ENTERED') this.counters.alertEntries++;
 174:     if (eventName === 'CHASE_ENTERED') this.counters.chaseEntries++;
 175:     if (eventName === 'HOLD_ENTERED') this.counters.holdEntries++;
 176:     if (eventName === 'RECOVER_ENTERED') this.counters.recoverEntries++;
 177:     if (eventName === 'PLAN_STAGE_1_FULL' || eventName === 'PLAN_STAGE_2_FULL' || eventName === 'PATH_ADOPTED') {
 178:       if (extraDetails?.pathType === 'FULL' || eventName === 'PATH_ADOPTED') {
 179:         this.counters.fullPlans++;
 180:       } else if (extraDetails?.pathType === 'PARTIAL') {
 181:         this.counters.partialPlans++;
 182:       }
 183:     }
 184:     if (eventName === 'PLAN_STAGE_2_FAILED' || eventName === 'PLAN_STAGE_1_FAILED') {
 185:       this.counters.failedPlans++;
 186:     }
 187:     if (eventName === 'ZERO_MOVEMENT_INTENT') this.counters.zeroMovementIntents++;
 188:     if (eventName === 'NO_PROGRESS_DETECTED') this.counters.noProgressTriggers++;
 189:     if (eventName === 'TWO_CELL_OSCILLATION_DETECTED') this.counters.oscillationTriggers++;
 190:     if (eventName === 'BOT_CAPTURED' || eventName === 'CAPTURE') this.counters.captures++;
 191:     if (eventName === 'LEGACY_UPDATE_CALLED_WHILE_V2_SELECTED') this.counters.legacyUpdatesInV2++;
 192:     if (eventName === 'RADAR_WAVE_CROSSED_PLAYER') this.counters.radarMaxCrossedPlayer++;
 193:   }
 194: 
 195:   public getEvents(): FlightRecordEvent[] {
 196:     return [...this.events];
 197:   }
 198: 
 199:   public getErrors(): ProjectErrorRecord[] {
 200:     return [...this.errors];
 201:   }
 202: 
 203:   public clearLog(): void {
 204:     this.events = [];
 205:     this.seqCounter = 0;
 206:     Object.keys(this.counters).forEach((k) => {
 207:       (this.counters as any)[k] = 0;
 208:     });
 209:   }
 210: 
 211:   public setPaused(paused: boolean): void {
 212:     this.isPaused = paused;
 213:   }
 214: 
 215:   public getIsPaused(): boolean {
 216:     return this.isPaused;
 217:   }
 218: 
 219:   // Auto classification
 220:   public classify(): string {
 221:     const c = this.counters;
 222:     
 223:     // Check for invalid position
 224:     const hasInvalidPos = this.events.some(e => isNaN(e.botPos.x) || isNaN(e.botPos.y));
 225:     if (hasInvalidPos) return 'INVALID_POSITION';
 226: 
 227:     // Legacy conflict
 228:     if (c.legacyUpdatesInV2 > 0) return 'LEGACY_V2_STATE_CONFLICT';
 229: 
 230:     // Check errors
 231:     if (this.errors.length > 0) return 'RUNTIME_EXCEPTION';
 232: 
 233:     // State machine classifications
 234:     if (c.nearChecks === 0 && c.radarPulses === 0) return 'SEARCH_DID_NOT_RUN';
 235:     if (c.nearChecks > 0 && c.nearHits === 0) {
 236:       if (c.radarPulses > 0 && c.radarHits === 0) {
 237:         if (c.radarMaxCrossedPlayer === 0) {
 238:           return 'RADAR_NEVER_REACHED_PLAYER';
 239:         }
 240:         return 'NEAR_SENSOR_ALWAYS_MISSED';
 241:       }
 242:     }
 243:     
 244:     if (c.radarPulses > 0 && c.radarMaxCrossedPlayer > 0 && c.radarHits === 0 && c.nearHits === 0) {
 245:       return 'RADAR_CROSSED_BUT_DO_NOT_HIT';
 246:     }
 247: 
 248:     if ((c.nearHits > 0 || c.radarHits > 0) && c.awarenessEpisodes === 0) {
 249:       return 'SENSOR_HIT_BUT_NO_AWARENESS';
 250:     }
 251: 
 252:     if (c.awarenessEpisodes > 0 && c.alertEntries === 0) {
 253:       return 'AWARENESS_OPENED_BUT_NO_ALERT';
 254:     }
 255: 
 256:     if (c.alertEntries > 0 && c.chaseEntries === 0) {
 257:       return 'ALERT_ENTERED_BUT_NO_CHASE';
 258:     }
 259: 
 260:     if (c.chaseEntries > 0 && (c.fullPlans + c.partialPlans === 0)) {
 261:       return 'CHASE_ENTERED_BUT_NO_PLAN';
 262:     }
 263: 
 264:     if ((c.fullPlans + c.partialPlans > 0) && c.zeroMovementIntents > 40) {
 265:       return 'PLAN_FOUND_BUT_NO_MOVEMENT';
 266:     }
 267: 
 268:     if (c.noProgressTriggers > 0 || c.oscillationTriggers > 0) {
 269:       return 'MOVEMENT_COMMITTED_BUT_NO_PROGRESS';
 270:     }
 271: 
 272:     // Side to side movement in search
 273:     const onlySearch = this.events.every(e => e.currentState === 'SEARCH');
 274:     if (onlySearch && this.events.length > 10) {
 275:       return 'SEARCH_SIDE_TO_SIDE_ONLY';
 276:     }
 277: 
 278:     // Repeated state changes without holding CHASE
 279:     if (c.alertEntries > 3 && c.chaseEntries === 0) {
 280:       return 'REPEATED_STATE_RESET';
 281:     }
 282: 
 283:     return 'UNKNOWN';
 284:   }
 285: 
 286:   // Setup unhandled error capture
 287:   private setupGlobalErrorHandler(): void {
 288:     if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
 289: 
 290:     window.addEventListener('error', (event) => {
 291:       const msg = event.message || '';
 292:       const stack = event.error?.stack || '';
 293:       const filename = event.filename || '';
 294:       const lineno = event.lineno || 0;
 295: 
 296:       // Filter MakerSuite / AI Studio / WebSocket host noise
 297:       if (
 298:         msg.includes('makersuite') ||
 299:         msg.includes('google.alkali') ||
 300:         msg.includes('WebSocket') ||
 301:         msg.includes('vite') ||
 302:         msg.includes('hmr') ||
 303:         filename.includes('gstatic.com') ||
 304:         filename.includes('chrome-extension')
 305:       ) {
 306:         return; // Filter out host platform noise
 307:       }
 308: 
 309:       this.errorCounter++;
 310:       this.errors.push({
 311:         id: this.errorCounter,
 312:         timestamp: Date.now(),
 313:         message: msg,
 314:         stack,
 315:         sourceFile: filename,
 316:         line: lineno,
 317:         snapshot: JSON.stringify({
 318:           eventsCount: this.events.length,
 319:           lastState: this.events[this.events.length - 1]?.currentState || 'SEARCH',
 320:         }),
 321:       });
 322: 
 323:       if (this.errors.length > 50) {
 324:         this.errors.shift();
 325:       }
 326:     });
 327: 
 328:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection', (event) => {
 329:       const reason = event.reason;
 330:       const msg = reason?.message || String(reason);
 331:       const stack = reason?.stack || '';
 332: 
 333:       if (
 334:         msg.includes('makersuite') ||
 335:         msg.includes('google.alkali') ||
 336:         msg.includes('WebSocket')
 337:       ) {
 338:         return;
 339:       }
 340: 
 341:       this.errorCounter++;
 342:       this.errors.push({
 343:         id: this.errorCounter,
 344:         timestamp: Date.now(),
 345:         message: `Unhandled Promise Rejection: ${msg}`,
 346:         stack,
 347:         sourceFile: 'PromiseRejection',
 348:         snapshot: JSON.stringify({
 349:           eventsCount: this.events.length,
 350:         }),
 351:       });
 352: 
 353:       if (this.errors.length > 50) {
 354:         this.errors.shift();
 355:       }
 356:     });
 357:   }
 358: 
 359:   // Manual project-error log
 360:   public logProjectError(message: string, stack?: string, sourceFile?: string, line?: number): void {
 361:     this.errorCounter++;
 362:     this.errors.push({
 363:       id: this.errorCounter,
 364:       timestamp: Date.now(),
 365:       message,
 366:       stack,
 367:       sourceFile,
 368:       line,
 369:     });
 370:     if (this.errors.length > 50) {
 371:       this.errors.shift();
 372:     }
 373:   }
 374: }


================================================================================
FILE: src/games/circuit-climb/bot-ai-v2/CircuitClimbUnifiedRecorder.ts
