   1: /**
   2:  * @license
   3:  * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
   4:  */
   5: 
   6: import { Vec2 } from './BotTypesV2';
   7: 
   8: export type DiagnosticSource =
   9:   | 'USER'
  10:   | 'PLAYER'
  11:   | 'BOT_SENSOR'
  12:   | 'BOT_AWARENESS'
  13:   | 'BOT_STATE'
  14:   | 'BOT_TARGET'
  15:   | 'BOT_PLANNER'
  16:   | 'BOT_MOVEMENT'
  17:   | 'BOT_PROGRESS'
  18:   | 'BOT_RECOVERY'
  19:   | 'COLLISION'
  20:   | 'RUNTIME'
  21:   | 'ERROR';
  22: 
  23: export interface CircuitClimbDiagnosticEvent {
  24:   sequence: number;
  25:   simTimeMs: number;
  26:   realTimeMs?: number;
  27: 
  28:   source: DiagnosticSource;
  29:   event: string;
  30:   reason?: string;
  31: 
  32:   playerPosition?: Vec2;
  33:   botPosition?: Vec2;
  34: 
  35:   playerRow?: number;
  36:   playerPlatformId?: string | null;
  37:   playerMovementState?: string;
  38: 
  39:   botState?: string;
  40:   botEngine?: string;
  41: 
  42:   awarenessEpisodeId?: number | null;
  43:   targetVersion?: number;
  44:   pathTargetVersion?: number;
  45: 
  46:   currentGoalId?: string | null;
  47:   currentGoalPurpose?: string | null;
  48:   currentPathIndex?: number;
  49:   currentPathLength?: number;
  50: 
  51:   intendedMovement?: Vec2;
  52:   committedMovement?: Vec2;
  53: 
  54:   data?: Record<string, string | number | boolean | null>;
  55: }
  56: 
  57: export interface DiagnosticSummary {
  58:   session: {
  59:     engine: string;
  60:     difficulty: string;
  61:     viewport: { width: number; height: number };
  62:     durationMs: number;
  63:     frameCount: number;
  64:   };
  65:   user: {
  66:     platformSelections: number;
  67:     correctSelections: number;
  68:     wrongSelections: number;
  69:     restarts: number;
  70:   };
  71:   player: {
  72:     moves: number;
  73:     landings: number;
  74:     rowsClimbed: number;
  75:     finalRow: number;
  76:     finalPosition: Vec2;
  77:   };
  78:   bot: {
  79:     detectionEvents: number;
  80:     awarenessEpisodes: number;
  81:     alertCount: number;
  82:     pursueCount: number;
  83:     finalApproachCount: number;
  84:     recoverCount: number;
  85:     targetVersionChanges: number;
  86:     replansRequested: number;
  87:     replansSuppressed: number;
  88:     plansAdopted: number;
  89:     zeroIntentEvents: number;
  90:     staleTargetWarnings: number;
  91:     verticalProgressFailures: number;
  92:     captures: number;
  93:   };
  94:   failureClassification: string[];
  95: }
  96: 
  97: export type ConsoleOutputMode = 'OFF' | 'IMPORTANT' | 'VERBOSE';
  98: 
  99: export class CircuitClimbUnifiedRecorder {
 100:   private static instance: CircuitClimbUnifiedRecorder | null = null;
 101: 
 102:   public static getInstance(): CircuitClimbUnifiedRecorder {
 103:     if (!CircuitClimbUnifiedRecorder.instance) {
 104:       CircuitClimbUnifiedRecorder.instance = new CircuitClimbUnifiedRecorder();
 105:     }
 106:     return CircuitClimbUnifiedRecorder.instance;
 107:   }
 108: 
 109:   private sequenceCounter = 0;
 110:   private readonly capacity = 1000;
 111:   private ringBuffer: CircuitClimbDiagnosticEvent[] = [];
 112:   private isRecording = true;
 113: 
 114:   // Sampling timers/stamps
 115:   private lastMovementSampleMs = -1000;
 116:   private lastSensorMissSampleMs = -1000;
 117:   private lastHealthSampleMs = -1000;
 118:   private lastStateHeartbeatMs = -1000;
 119: 
 120:   // Target stale & landing check stamps
 121:   private lastPlayerLandingMs = -1000;
 122:   private lastPlayerLandingTargetVersion = -1;
 123:   private targetVersionChangedAfterLanding = false;
 124: 
 125:   // Progress reset thrash tracker
 126:   private monitorResetTimes: number[] = [];
 127: 
 128:   // Failure window state
 129:   private preservedFailureWindow: CircuitClimbDiagnosticEvent[] | null = null;
 130: 
 131:   // Console output mode
 132:   private consoleMode: ConsoleOutputMode = 'IMPORTANT';
 133: 
 134:   // Active session metadata
 135:   public activeEngine = 'V2_SIMPLIFIED';
 136:   public activeDifficulty = 'NORMAL';
 137:   public viewportWidth = 400;
 138:   public viewportHeight = 800;
 139:   public frameCount = 0;
 140: 
 141:   // Controllers update counters
 142:   public simplifiedUpdateCount = 0;
 143:   public frozenUpdateCount = 0;
 144:   public legacyUpdateCount = 0;
 145:   public simplifiedInstanceId = Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000000);
 146: 
 147:   // User & Player counters
 148:   public platformSelections = 0;
 149:   public correctSelections = 0;
 150:   public wrongSelections = 0;
 151:   public restarts = 0;
 152: 
 153:   private constructor() {
 154:     this.setupGlobalErrorCapture();
 155:   }
 156: 
 157:   public recordEvent(
 158:     source: DiagnosticSource,
 159:     eventName: string,
 160:     payload: Partial<Omit<CircuitClimbDiagnosticEvent, 'sequence' | 'source' | 'event'>> = {}
 161:   ): CircuitClimbDiagnosticEvent | null {
 162:     if (!this.isRecording) return null;
 163: 
 164:     this.sequenceCounter++;
 165: 
 166:     const event: CircuitClimbDiagnosticEvent = {
 167:       sequence: this.sequenceCounter,
 168:       simTimeMs: payload.simTimeMs ?? 0,
 169:       realTimeMs: Date.now(),
 170:       source,
 171:       event: eventName,
 172:       ...payload,
 173:     };
 174: 
 175:     this.ringBuffer.push(event);
 176:     if (this.ringBuffer.length > this.capacity) {
 177:       this.ringBuffer.shift();
 178:     }
 179: 
 180:     this.logToConsole(event);
 181: 
 182:     // Diagnostics checks trigger
 183:     this.evaluateAutomaticDiagnostics(event);
 184: 
 185:     return event;
 186:   }
 187: 
 188:   public resetRecorder(): void {
 189:     this.sequenceCounter = 0;
 190:     this.ringBuffer = [];
 191:     this.preservedFailureWindow = null;
 192:     this.lastMovementSampleMs = -1000;
 193:     this.lastSensorMissSampleMs = -1000;
 194:     this.lastHealthSampleMs = -1000;
 195:     this.lastStateHeartbeatMs = -1000;
 196:     this.lastPlayerLandingMs = -1000;
 197:     this.lastPlayerLandingTargetVersion = -1;
 198:     this.targetVersionChangedAfterLanding = false;
 199:     this.monitorResetTimes = [];
 200:     this.frameCount = 0;
 201:     this.simplifiedUpdateCount = 0;
 202:     this.frozenUpdateCount = 0;
 203:     this.legacyUpdateCount = 0;
 204:     this.platformSelections = 0;
 205:     this.correctSelections = 0;
 206:     this.wrongSelections = 0;
 207:     this.restarts = 0;
 208:   }
 209: 
 210:   public setRecording(recording: boolean): void {
 211:     this.isRecording = recording;
 212:   }
 213: 
 214:   public getIsRecording(): boolean {
 215:     return this.isRecording;
 216:   }
 217: 
 218:   public setConsoleMode(mode: ConsoleOutputMode): void {
 219:     this.consoleMode = mode;
 220:   }
 221: 
 222:   public getConsoleMode(): ConsoleOutputMode {
 223:     return this.consoleMode;
 224:   }
 225: 
 226:   public getEvents(filter: string = 'ALL'): CircuitClimbDiagnosticEvent[] {
 227:     if (filter === 'ALL') return [...this.ringBuffer];
 228:     const norm = filter.toUpperCase().replace('_', ' ');
 229:     return this.ringBuffer.filter((e) => {
 230:       if (norm === 'USER') return e.source === 'USER';
 231:       if (norm === 'PLAYER') return e.source === 'PLAYER';
 232:       if (norm === 'BOT SENSOR') return e.source === 'BOT_SENSOR';
 233:       if (norm === 'BOT AWARENESS') return e.source === 'BOT_AWARENESS';
 234:       if (norm === 'BOT STATE') return e.source === 'BOT_STATE';
 235:       if (norm === 'BOT TARGET') return e.source === 'BOT_TARGET';
 236:       if (norm === 'BOT PLAN' || norm === 'BOT PLANNER') return e.source === 'BOT_PLANNER';
 237:       if (norm === 'BOT MOVEMENT') return e.source === 'BOT_MOVEMENT';
 238:       if (norm === 'BOT RECOVERY' || norm === 'BOT PROGRESS') return e.source === 'BOT_RECOVERY' || e.source === 'BOT_PROGRESS';
 239:       if (norm === 'COLLISION') return e.source === 'COLLISION';
 240:       if (norm === 'ERRORS' || norm === 'ERROR') return e.source === 'ERROR' || e.source === 'RUNTIME';
 241:       return true;
 242:     });
 243:   }
 244: 
 245:   public triggerFailureWindowCapture(triggerEvent: CircuitClimbDiagnosticEvent): void {
 246:     if (this.preservedFailureWindow) return; // preserve the first failure window
 247: 
 248:     const triggerIdx = this.ringBuffer.findIndex((e) => e.sequence === triggerEvent.sequence);
 249:     if (triggerIdx === -1) return;
 250: 
 251:     const startIdx = Math.max(0, triggerIdx - 100);
 252:     // Keep window of preceding 100 + trigger + next 50
 253:     this.preservedFailureWindow = this.ringBuffer.slice(startIdx);
 254:   }
 255: 
 256:   public updateAfterTrigger(simTimeMs: number): void {
 257:     if (this.preservedFailureWindow) {
 258:       // Keep adding events up to 50 events after trigger
 259:       const currentFullBuffer = this.ringBuffer;
 260:       const triggerSeq = this.preservedFailureWindow.find((e) =>
 261:         [
 262:           'BOT_VERTICAL_PROGRESS_STOPPED',
 263:           'BOT_TARGET_STALE',
 264:           'BOT_LANDING_WITHOUT_TARGET_UPDATE',
 265:           'BOT_LANDING_WITHOUT_REPLAN',
 266:           'BOT_STALE_PLAN_ADOPTED',
 267:           'BOT_MOVEMENT_NOT_COMMITTED',
 268:           'BOT_PROGRESS_MONITOR_RESET_THRASH',
 269:           'PROJECT_ERROR',
 270:           'PROJECT_WARNING',
 271:           'PROJECT_ASSERTION_FAILED',
 272:         ].includes(e.event)
 273:       )?.sequence;
 274: 
 275:       if (triggerSeq) {
 276:         const triggerIdx = currentFullBuffer.findIndex((e) => e.sequence === triggerSeq);
 277:         if (triggerIdx !== -1) {
 278:           const startIdx = Math.max(0, triggerIdx - 100);
 279:           const endIdx = Math.min(currentFullBuffer.length, triggerIdx + 51);
 280:           this.preservedFailureWindow = currentFullBuffer.slice(startIdx, endIdx);
 281:         }
 282:       }
 283:     }
 284:   }
 285: 
 286:   public getFailureWindow(): CircuitClimbDiagnosticEvent[] {
 287:     if (this.preservedFailureWindow) {
 288:       return [...this.preservedFailureWindow];
 289:     }
 290:     // Fallback to last 150 events
 291:     return this.ringBuffer.slice(Math.max(0, this.ringBuffer.length - 150));
 292:   }
 293: 
 294:   // Sampling checks
 295:   public shouldSampleMovement(simTimeMs: number): boolean {
 296:     if (simTimeMs - this.lastMovementSampleMs >= 250) {
 297:       this.lastMovementSampleMs = simTimeMs;
 298:       return true;
 299:     }
 300:     return false;
 301:   }
 302: 
 303:   public shouldSampleSensorMiss(simTimeMs: number): boolean {
 304:     if (simTimeMs - this.lastSensorMissSampleMs >= 1000) {
 305:       this.lastSensorMissSampleMs = simTimeMs;
 306:       return true;
 307:     }
 308:     return false;
 309:   }
 310: 
 311:   public shouldSampleHealth(simTimeMs: number): boolean {
 312:     if (simTimeMs - this.lastHealthSampleMs >= 1000) {
 313:       this.lastHealthSampleMs = simTimeMs;
 314:       return true;
 315:     }
 316:     return false;
 317:   }
 318: 
 319:   public shouldSampleStateHeartbeat(simTimeMs: number): boolean {
 320:     if (simTimeMs - this.lastStateHeartbeatMs >= 1000) {
 321:       this.lastStateHeartbeatMs = simTimeMs;
 322:       return true;
 323:     }
 324:     return false;
 325:   }
 326: 
 327:   // Diagnostic logic
 328:   private evaluateAutomaticDiagnostics(event: CircuitClimbDiagnosticEvent): void {
 329:     if (event.event === 'PLAYER_LANDED') {
 330:       this.lastPlayerLandingMs = event.simTimeMs;
 331:       this.lastPlayerLandingTargetVersion = event.targetVersion ?? -1;
 332:       this.targetVersionChangedAfterLanding = false;
 333:     }
 334: 
 335:     if (event.event === 'BOT_TARGET_VERSION_CHANGED') {
 336:       this.targetVersionChangedAfterLanding = true;
 337:     }
 338: 
 339:     if (event.event === 'BOT_PROGRESS_MONITOR_RESET') {
 340:       this.monitorResetTimes.push(event.simTimeMs);
 341:       this.monitorResetTimes = this.monitorResetTimes.filter((t) => event.simTimeMs - t <= 1000);
 342:       if (this.monitorResetTimes.length > 5) {
 343:         const thrashEvent = this.recordEvent('BOT_PROGRESS', 'BOT_PROGRESS_MONITOR_RESET_THRASH', {
 344:           simTimeMs: event.simTimeMs,
 345:           reason: 'Monitor reset more than 5 times in 1 second without meaningful progress',
 346:           botPosition: event.botPosition,
 347:           playerPosition: event.playerPosition,
 348:         });
 349:         if (thrashEvent) this.triggerFailureWindowCapture(thrashEvent);
 350:       }
 351:     }
 352: 
 353:     if (
 354:       [
 355:         'BOT_VERTICAL_PROGRESS_STOPPED',
 356:         'BOT_TARGET_STALE',
 357:         'BOT_LANDING_WITHOUT_TARGET_UPDATE',
 358:         'BOT_LANDING_WITHOUT_REPLAN',
 359:         'BOT_STALE_PLAN_ADOPTED',
 360:         'BOT_MOVEMENT_NOT_COMMITTED',
 361:         'BOT_PROGRESS_MONITOR_RESET_THRASH',
 362:         'PROJECT_ERROR',
 363:         'PROJECT_WARNING',
 364:         'PROJECT_ASSERTION_FAILED',
 365:       ].includes(event.event)
 366:     ) {
 367:       this.triggerFailureWindowCapture(event);
 368:     }
 369:   }
 370: 
 371:   private logToConsole(event: CircuitClimbDiagnosticEvent): void {
 372:     if (this.consoleMode === 'OFF') return;
 373: 
 374:     const isSampledNoise =
 375:       event.event === 'BOT_MOVEMENT_SAMPLE' ||
 376:       event.event === 'BOT_NEAR_SENSOR_MISS_SAMPLE' ||
 377:       event.event === 'PLAYER_POSITION_SAMPLE' ||
 378:       event.event === 'BOT_CONTROLLER_UPDATE_HEALTH' ||
 379:       event.event === 'BOT_STATE_HEARTBEAT';
 380: 
 381:     if (this.consoleMode === 'IMPORTANT' && isSampledNoise) return;
 382: 
 383:     const sec = (event.simTimeMs / 1000).toFixed(3);
 384:     const seqStr = String(event.sequence).padStart(4, '0');
 385:     const prefix = `[CIRCUIT-BOT #${seqStr} ${sec}s ${event.source}]`;
 386: 
 387:     let details = `${event.event}`;
 388:     if (event.reason) details += ` | ${event.reason}`;
 389:     if (event.botState) details += ` | state=${event.botState}`;
 390:     if (event.targetVersion !== undefined) details += ` | tv=${event.targetVersion}`;
 391:     if (event.intendedMovement) {
 392:       details += ` | int=(${event.intendedMovement.x.toFixed(1)},${event.intendedMovement.y.toFixed(1)})`;
 393:     }
 394:     if (event.committedMovement) {
 395:       details += ` | com=(${event.committedMovement.x.toFixed(1)},${event.committedMovement.y.toFixed(1)})`;
 396:     }
 397: 
 398:     console.log(`${prefix} ${details}`);
 399:   }
 400: 
 401:   private setupGlobalErrorCapture(): void {
 402:     if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
 403: 
 404:     window.addEventListener('error', (e) => {
 405:       const msg = e.message || '';
 406:       const filename = e.filename || '';
 407:       if (
 408:         msg.includes('makersuite') ||
 409:         msg.includes('google.alkali') ||
 410:         msg.includes('WebSocket') ||
 411:         msg.includes('vite') ||
 412:         msg.includes('hmr') ||
 413:         filename.includes('gstatic.com') ||
 414:         filename.includes('chrome-extension')
 415:       ) {
 416:         return;
 417:       }
 418: 
 419:       this.recordEvent('ERROR', 'PROJECT_ERROR', {
 420:         simTimeMs: 0,
 421:         reason: msg,
 422:         data: {
 423:           stack: e.error?.stack || '',
 424:           filename,
 425:           lineno: e.lineno,
 426:         },
 427:       });
 428:     });
 429: 
 430:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection', (e) => {
 431:       const reason = e.reason;
 432:       const msg = reason?.message || String(reason);
 433:       if (msg.includes('makersuite') || msg.includes('google.alkali') || msg.includes('WebSocket')) return;
 434: 
 435:       this.recordEvent('ERROR', 'PROJECT_ERROR', {
 436:         simTimeMs: 0,
 437:         reason: `Unhandled Rejection: ${msg}`,
 438:         data: { stack: reason?.stack || '' },
 439:       });
 440:     });
 441:   }
 442: 
 443:   public buildSummary(): DiagnosticSummary {
 444:     const events = this.ringBuffer;
 445:     const lastEvent = events[events.length - 1];
 446: 
 447:     let alertCount = 0;
 448:     let pursueCount = 0;
 449:     let finalApproachCount = 0;
 450:     let recoverCount = 0;
 451:     let targetVersionChanges = 0;
 452:     let replansRequested = 0;
 453:     let replansSuppressed = 0;
 454:     let plansAdopted = 0;
 455:     let zeroIntentEvents = 0;
 456:     let staleTargetWarnings = 0;
 457:     let verticalProgressFailures = 0;
 458:     let captures = 0;
 459:     let detectionEvents = 0;
 460:     let awarenessEpisodes = 0;
 461: 
 462:     let finalPlayerRow = 0;
 463:     let finalPlayerPos = { x: 0, y: 0 };
 464:     let playerLandings = 0;
 465:     let playerMoves = 0;
 466: 
 467:     events.forEach((e) => {
 468:       if (e.event === 'BOT_STATE_TRANSITION_ACCEPTED') {
 469:         if (e.data?.requestedState === 'ALERT') alertCount++;
 470:         if (e.data?.requestedState === 'PURSUE') pursueCount++;
 471:         if (e.data?.requestedState === 'FINAL_APPROACH') finalApproachCount++;
 472:         if (e.data?.requestedState === 'RECOVER') recoverCount++;
 473:       }
 474:       if (e.event === 'BOT_TARGET_VERSION_CHANGED') targetVersionChanges++;
 475:       if (e.event === 'BOT_REPLAN_REQUESTED') replansRequested++;
 476:       if (e.event === 'BOT_REPLAN_SUPPRESSED') replansSuppressed++;
 477:       if (e.event === 'BOT_PLAN_ADOPTED') plansAdopted++;
 478:       if (e.event === 'BOT_ZERO_INTENT') zeroIntentEvents++;
 479:       if (e.event === 'BOT_TARGET_STALE') staleTargetWarnings++;
 480:       if (e.event === 'BOT_VERTICAL_PROGRESS_STOPPED') verticalProgressFailures++;
 481:       if (e.event === 'BOT_PLAYER_CONTACT' || e.event === 'PLAYER_CAPTURED') captures++;
 482:       if (e.event === 'BOT_NEAR_SENSOR_HIT' || e.event === 'BOT_RADAR_SENSOR_HIT') detectionEvents++;
 483:       if (e.event === 'BOT_AWARENESS_OPENED') awarenessEpisodes++;
 484: 
 485:       if (e.event === 'PLAYER_LANDED') {
 486:         playerLandings++;
 487:         if (e.playerRow) finalPlayerRow = Math.max(finalPlayerRow, e.playerRow);
 488:       }
 489:       if (e.event === 'PLAYER_MOVE_STARTED') playerMoves++;
 490:       if (e.playerPosition) finalPlayerPos = e.playerPosition;
 491:     });
 492: 
 493:     const failureClassification = this.classifyFailures();
 494: 
 495:     return {
 496:       session: {
 497:         engine: this.activeEngine,
 498:         difficulty: this.activeDifficulty,
 499:         viewport: { width: this.viewportWidth, height: this.viewportHeight },
 500:         durationMs: lastEvent ? lastEvent.simTimeMs : 0,
 501:         frameCount: this.frameCount,
 502:       },
 503:       user: {
 504:         platformSelections: this.platformSelections,
 505:         correctSelections: this.correctSelections,
 506:         wrongSelections: this.wrongSelections,
 507:         restarts: this.restarts,
 508:       },
 509:       player: {
 510:         moves: playerMoves,
 511:         landings: playerLandings,
 512:         rowsClimbed: finalPlayerRow,
 513:         finalRow: finalPlayerRow,
 514:         finalPosition: finalPlayerPos,
 515:       },
 516:       bot: {
 517:         detectionEvents,
 518:         awarenessEpisodes,
 519:         alertCount,
 520:         pursueCount,
 521:         finalApproachCount,
 522:         recoverCount,
 523:         targetVersionChanges,
 524:         replansRequested,
 525:         replansSuppressed,
 526:         plansAdopted,
 527:         zeroIntentEvents,
 528:         staleTargetWarnings,
 529:         verticalProgressFailures,
 530:         captures,
 531:       },
 532:       failureClassification,
 533:     };
 534:   }
 535: 
 536:   public classifyFailures(): string[] {
 537:     const events = this.ringBuffer;
 538:     const classifications = new Set<string>();
 539: 
 540:     const hasErrors = events.some((e) => e.source === 'ERROR');
 541:     if (hasErrors) classifications.add('PROJECT_RUNTIME_ERROR');
 542: 
 543:     const wrongController = events.some((e) => e.event === 'WRONG_CONTROLLER_CALLED');
 544:     if (wrongController) classifications.add('WRONG_CONTROLLER_ACTIVE');
 545: 
 546:     const detection = events.some((e) => e.event === 'BOT_NEAR_SENSOR_HIT' || e.event === 'BOT_RADAR_SENSOR_HIT');
 547:     if (!detection && events.length > 20) classifications.add('BOT_NEVER_DETECTED');
 548: 
 549:     const awarenessStale = events.some((e) => e.event === 'BOT_AWARENESS_POSITION_STALE');
 550:     if (awarenessStale) classifications.add('BOT_AWARENESS_STALE');
 551: 
 552:     const targetNotUpdated = events.some((e) => e.event === 'BOT_LANDING_WITHOUT_TARGET_UPDATE');
 553:     if (targetNotUpdated) classifications.add('BOT_TARGET_NOT_UPDATED');
 554: 
 555:     const noReplan = events.some((e) => e.event === 'BOT_LANDING_WITHOUT_REPLAN');
 556:     if (noReplan) classifications.add('BOT_REPLAN_NOT_REQUESTED');
 557: 
 558:     const replanSuppressed = events.some((e) => e.event === 'BOT_REPLAN_SUPPRESSED');
 559:     if (replanSuppressed) classifications.add('BOT_REPLAN_SUPPRESSED');
 560: 
 561:     const stalePlanAdopted = events.some((e) => e.event === 'BOT_STALE_PLAN_ADOPTED');
 562:     if (stalePlanAdopted) classifications.add('BOT_PLAN_TARGET_STALE');
 563: 
 564:     const zeroMove = events.some((e) => e.event === 'BOT_ZERO_INTENT');
 565:     if (zeroMove) classifications.add('BOT_ZERO_MOVEMENT');
 566: 
 567:     const notCommitted = events.some((e) => e.event === 'BOT_MOVEMENT_NOT_COMMITTED');
 568:     if (notCommitted) classifications.add('BOT_MOVEMENT_NOT_COMMITTED');
 569: 
 570:     const lateralOnly = events.some((e) => e.event === 'BOT_LATERAL_ONLY_MOVEMENT');
 571:     if (lateralOnly) classifications.add('BOT_LATERAL_ONLY');
 572: 
 573:     const resetThrash = events.some((e) => e.event === 'BOT_PROGRESS_MONITOR_RESET_THRASH');
 574:     if (resetThrash) classifications.add('BOT_PROGRESS_MONITOR_RESET');
 575: 
 576:     const vertStopped = events.some((e) => e.event === 'BOT_VERTICAL_PROGRESS_STOPPED');
 577:     if (vertStopped) classifications.add('BOT_PATH_COMPLETE_NO_EXIT');
 578: 
 579:     if (classifications.size === 0) {
 580:       classifications.add('NO_FAILURE_DETECTED');
 581:     }
 582: 
 583:     return Array.from(classifications);
 584:   }
 585: }

 130: // Pseudo-random number generator for determinism
 131: function deterministicRandom(seed: number): number {
 132:   const x = Math.sin(seed) * 10000;
 133:   return x - Math.floor(x);
 134: }
 135: 
 136: function clamp(val: number, min: number, max: number): number {
 137:   return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, val));
 138: }
 139: 
 140: function lerp(a: number, b: number, t: number): number {
 141:   return a + (b - a) * t;
 142: }

 207: // Check platform collisions with padding
 208: export function obstacleRectsNear(y0: number, y1: number, rows: any[], CONFIG: any) {
 209:   const padding = 6;
 210:   const rects: any[] = [];
 211:   const rowGap = CONFIG.rowGap || 205;
 212:   rows.forEach((row) => {
 213:     if (row.y < y0 - rowGap || row.y > y1 + rowGap) return;
 214:     row.platforms.forEach((platform: any) => {
 215:       if (platform.row === 0 && platform.column !== 1) return;
 216:       rects.push({
 217:         left: platform.x - platform.width / 2 - padding,
 218:         right: platform.x + platform.width / 2 + padding,
 219:         top: platform.y - padding,
 220:         bottom: platform.y + platform.height + padding,
 221:       });
 222:     });
 223:   });
 224:   return rects;
 225: }
 226: 
 227: export function cellBlocked(x: number, y: number, rects: any[]): boolean {
 228:   return rects.some((rect) => x > rect.left && x < rect.right && y > rect.top && y < rect.bottom);
 229: }
 230: 
 231: function pathMetrics(points: Array<{ x: number; y: number }>) {
 232:   const lengths: number[] = [];
 233:   let total = 0;
 234:   for (let i = 0; i < points.length - 1; i += 1) {
 235:     const d = Math.hypot(points[i + 1].x - points[i].x, points[i + 1].y - points[i].y);
 236:     lengths.push(d);
 237:     total += d;
 238:   }
 239:   return { lengths, total };
 240: }
 241: 
 242: function pointOnPath(currentTravel: BotWaypoints) {
 243:   let distance = currentTravel.distance;
 244:   for (let i = 0; i < currentTravel.lengths.length; i += 1) {
 245:     const length = currentTravel.lengths[i];
 246:     if (distance <= length || i === currentTravel.lengths.length - 1) {
 247:       const a = currentTravel.points[i];
 248:       const b = currentTravel.points[i + 1];
 249:       const amount = length === 0 ? 1 : clamp(distance / length, 0, 1);
 250:       return { x: lerp(a.x, b.x, amount), segment: i };
 251:     }
 252:     distance -= length;
 253:   }
 254:   const end = currentTravel.points[currentTravel.points.length - 1];
 255:   return { x: end.x, segment: currentTravel.lengths.length - 1 };
 256: }
 257: 
 258: // Dynamic Zigzag cosmetic personality layer (only for visual rendering)
 259: export function getZigzagOffset(botState: BotAIState, elapsed: number, rows: any[], player: any): number {
 260:   if (botState.mode !== 'CHASE') return 0;
 261:   
 262:   // Calculate raw zigzag offset
 263:   const rawOffset = Math.sin(elapsed * 0.008) * 6; // subtle 6px wave
 264: 
 265:   // Fade near target
 266:   const distanceToPlayer = Math.hypot(player.x - botState.x, player.y - botState.y);
 267:   let targetFade = clamp((distanceToPlayer - 40) / 40, 0, 1);
 268: 
 269:   // Fade near obstacles
 270:   const rects = obstacleRectsNear(botState.y - 40, botState.y + 40, rows, { rowGap: 205 });
 271:   let minDistanceToObstacle = 9999;
 272:   for (const rect of rects) {
 273:     const dx = Math.max(rect.left - botState.x, 0, botState.x - rect.right);
 274:     const dy = Math.max(rect.top - botState.y, 0, botState.y - rect.bottom);
 275:     const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
 276:     if (dist < minDistanceToObstacle) {
 277:       minDistanceToObstacle = dist;
 278:     }
 279:   }
 280:   let obstacleFade = clamp((minDistanceToObstacle - 20) / 20, 0, 1);
 281: 
 282:   return rawOffset * targetFade * obstacleFade;
 283: }
 284: 
 285: // A* Planner with multi-goal capability and staged bounds
 286: function findAStarPath(
 287:   startX: number,
 288:   startY: number,
 289:   goals: Array<{ x: number; y: number }>,
 290:   bounds: { left: number; right: number; top: number; bottom: number },
 291:   rects: any[],
 292:   CONFIG: any,
 293:   botState: BotAIState
 294: ): { points: Array<{ x: number; y: number }> | null; expanded: number } {
 295:   const unit = CONFIG.grid;
 296:   const snap = (val: number) => Math.round(val / unit) * unit;
 297:   
 298:   const sx = snap(startX);
 299:   const sy = snap(startY);
 300: 
 301:   const validGoals = goals.map(g => ({ x: snap(g.x), y: snap(g.y) })).filter(g => !cellBlocked(g.x, g.y, rects));
 302:   if (validGoals.length === 0) {
 303:     return { points: null, expanded: 0 };
 304:   }
 305: 
 306:   const key = (x: number, y: number) => `${x},${y}`;
 307:   const goalKeys = new Set(validGoals.map(g => key(g.x, g.y)));
 308: 
 309:   // Heuristic: Min Manhattan distance to any of the valid goals
 310:   const h = (cx: number, cy: number) => {
 311:     let minH = Infinity;
 312:     for (const g of validGoals) {
 313:       const dist = Math.abs(cx - g.x) + Math.abs(cy - g.y);
 314:       if (dist < minH) minH = dist;
 315:     }
 316:     return minH;
 317:   };
 318: 
 319:   // Node structures
 320:   const openSetList: Array<{ x: number; y: number; g: number; f: number }> = [];
 321:   const openSetMap = new Map<string, number>(); // key -> f
 322:   const closedSet = new Set<string>();
 323:   const cameFrom = new Map<string, string | null>();
 324: 
 325:   const startKey = key(sx, sy);
 326:   const startH = h(sx, sy);
 327:   
 328:   openSetList.push({ x: sx, y: sy, g: 0, f: startH });
 329:   openSetMap.set(startKey, startH);
 330:   cameFrom.set(startKey, null);
 331: 
 332:   let expanded = 0;
 333:   let foundKey: string | null = null;
 334:   const guard = 5000;
 335: 
 336:   // Helper to treat starting cell as unblocked if we get pushed inside
 337:   const isStart = (cx: number, cy: number) => cx === sx && cy === sy;
 338: 
 339:   while (openSetList.length > 0 && expanded < guard) {
 340:     expanded += 1;
 341: 
 342:     // Linear scan to find the node with minimum f
 343:     let minIdx = 0;
 344:     for (let i = 1; i < openSetList.length; i++) {
 345:       if (openSetList[i].f < openSetList[minIdx].f) {
 346:         minIdx = i;
 347:       }
 348:     }
 349: 
 350:     const current = openSetList[minIdx];
 351:     const currentKey = key(current.x, current.y);
 352: 
 353:     // Remove from open list
 354:     openSetList.splice(minIdx, 1);
 355:     openSetMap.delete(currentKey);
 356:     closedSet.add(currentKey);
 357: 
 358:     // Goal reached?
 359:     if (goalKeys.has(currentKey)) {
 360:       foundKey = currentKey;
 361:       break;
 362:     }
 363: 
 364:     const neighbors = [
 365:       [current.x + unit, current.y],
 366:       [current.x - unit, current.y],
 367:       [current.x, current.y + unit],
 368:       [current.x, current.y - unit],
 369:     ];
 370: 
 371:     for (const [nx, ny] of neighbors) {
 372:       if (nx < bounds.left || nx > bounds.right || ny < bounds.top || ny > bounds.bottom) continue;
 373:       
 374:       const nKey = key(nx, ny);
 375:       if (closedSet.has(nKey)) continue;
 376: 
 377:       // Start is always unblocked
 378:       if (!isStart(nx, ny) && cellBlocked(nx, ny, rects)) continue;
 379: 
 380:       const tentativeG = current.g + unit;
 381:       const existingG = openSetMap.has(nKey) ? (openSetMap.get(nKey)! - h(nx, ny)) : Infinity;
 382: 
 383:       if (tentativeG < existingG) {
 384:         cameFrom.set(nKey, currentKey);
 385:         const fScore = tentativeG + h(nx, ny);
 386:         openSetMap.set(nKey, fScore);
 387: 
 388:         // Update list or add new node
 389:         const nodeIdx = openSetList.findIndex(n => n.x === nx && n.y === ny);
 390:         if (nodeIdx >= 0) {
 391:           openSetList[nodeIdx].g = tentativeG;
 392:           openSetList[nodeIdx].f = fScore;
 393:         } else {
 394:           openSetList.push({ x: nx, y: ny, g: tentativeG, f: fScore });
 395:         }
 396:       }
 397:     }
 398:   }
 399: 
 400:   if (!foundKey) return { points: null, expanded };
 401: 
 402:   // Reconstruct path
 403:   const points: Array<{ x: number; y: number }> = [];
 404:   let cursor: string | null | undefined = foundKey;
 405:   while (cursor !== undefined && cursor !== null) {
 406:     const [cx, cy] = cursor.split(',').map(Number);
 407:     points.push({ x: cx, y: cy });
 408:     cursor = cameFrom.get(cursor);
 409:   }
 410:   points.reverse();
 411: 
 412:   // Path pruning (remove collinear redundant midpoints)
 413:   const cleaned = [points[0]];
 414:   for (let i = 1; i < points.length; i += 1) {
 415:     const point = points[i];
 416:     const previous = cleaned[cleaned.length - 1];
 417:     if (point.x === previous.x && point.y === previous.y) continue;
 418:     if (cleaned.length >= 2) {
 419:       const before = cleaned[cleaned.length - 2];
 420:       const sameX = before.x === previous.x && previous.x === point.x;
 421:       const sameY = before.y === previous.y && previous.y === point.y;
 422:       if (sameX || sameY) {
 423:         cleaned[cleaned.length - 1] = point;
 424:         continue;
 425:       }
 426:     }
 427:     cleaned.push(point);
 428:   }
 429: 
 430:   return { points: cleaned, expanded };
 431: }
 432: 
 433: // Generate deterministic goals near player platform
 434: function generateGoals(player: any, rows: any[], CONFIG: any): { goals: Array<{ x: number; y: number; label: string }> } {
 435:   const goals: Array<{ x: number; y: number; label: string }> = [];
 436: 
 437:   // Find nearest platform
 438:   let activePlatform = player.platform;
 439:   if (!activePlatform) {
 440:     let bestDist = Infinity;
 441:     rows.forEach(row => {
 442:       row.platforms.forEach((platform: any) => {
 443:         const dist = Math.hypot(platform.x - player.x, platform.y - player.y);
 444:         if (dist < bestDist) {
 445:           bestDist = dist;
 446:           activePlatform = platform;
 447:         }
 448:       });
 449:     });
 450:   }
 451: 
 452:   if (activePlatform) {
 453:     const platY = activePlatform.y;
 454:     const platL = activePlatform.x - activePlatform.width / 2;
 455:     const platR = activePlatform.x + activePlatform.width / 2;
 456:     
 457:     // 1. Left of platform
 458:     goals.push({ x: platL - 20, y: platY - 16, label: 'left-platform' });
 459:     // 2. Right of platform
 460:     goals.push({ x: platR + 20, y: platY - 16, label: 'right-platform' });
 461:   }
 462: 
 463:   // 3. Above player
 464:   goals.push({ x: player.x, y: player.y - 48, label: 'above-player' });
 465:   // 4. Below player
 466:   goals.push({ x: player.x, y: player.y + 48, label: 'below-player' });
 467:   // 5. Nearest player tile
 468:   goals.push({ x: player.x, y: player.y, label: 'nearest-player' });
 469: 
 470:   return { goals };
 471: }
 472: 
 473: // Central planPath function utilizing Staged Search Bounds
 474: function planPath(
 475:   botState: BotAIState,
 476:   player: any,
 477:   rows: any[],
 478:   CONFIG: any,
 479:   width: number,
 480:   height: number
 481: ): { points: Array<{ x: number; y: number }> | null; goalLabel: string } {
 482:   botState.plannerRetryCount += 1;
 483:   botState.lastPlanTime = botState.scanTime;
 484: 
 485:   const { goals } = generateGoals(player, rows, CONFIG);
 486:   const unit = CONFIG.grid;
 487: 
 488:   // Staged Search Bounds Setup
 489:   // Stage 1: Local Search
 490:   const stage1Bounds = {
 491:     left: Math.min(botState.x, player.x) - unit * 10,
 492:     right: Math.max(botState.x, player.x) + unit * 10,
 493:     top: Math.min(botState.y, player.y) - unit * 8,
 494:     bottom: Math.max(botState.y, player.y) + unit * 8,
 495:   };
 496: 
 497:   const rects1 = obstacleRectsNear(stage1Bounds.top, stage1Bounds.bottom, rows, CONFIG);
 498:   
 499:   botState.currentSearchBounds = stage1Bounds;
 500:   botState.plannerStatus = 'planning';
 501: 
 502:   let result = findAStarPath(botState.x, botState.y, goals, stage1Bounds, rects1, CONFIG, botState);
 503:   
 504:   if (result.points) {
 505:     botState.nodesExpanded = result.expanded;
 506:     botState.plannerStatus = 'success';
 507:     botState.consecutiveFailures = 0;
 508:     botState.planSucceededCount = (botState.planSucceededCount || 0) + 1;
 509:     
 510:     // Find which goal we hit
 511:     const end = result.points[result.points.length - 1];
 512:     const snap = (v: number) => Math.round(v / unit) * unit;
 513:     const hitGoal = goals.find(g => snap(g.x) === snap(end.x) && snap(g.y) === snap(end.y)) || { label: 'unknown' };
 514:     
 515:     return { points: result.points, goalLabel: hitGoal.label };
 516:   }
 517: 
 518:   // Stage 2: Global Search (Expand bounds to full visible arena span)
 519:   const stage2Bounds = {
 520:     left: 0,
 521:     right: width,
 522:     top: Math.min(botState.y, player.y) - unit * 28,
 523:     bottom: Math.max(botState.y, player.y) + unit * 28,
 524:   };
 525: 
 526:   const rects2 = obstacleRectsNear(stage2Bounds.top, stage2Bounds.bottom, rows, CONFIG);
 527:   botState.currentSearchBounds = stage2Bounds;
 528: 
 529:   result = findAStarPath(botState.x, botState.y, goals, stage2Bounds, rects2, CONFIG, botState);
 530:   botState.nodesExpanded = result.expanded;
 531: 
 532:   if (result.points) {
 533:     botState.plannerStatus = 'success';
 534:     botState.consecutiveFailures = 0;
 535:     botState.planPartialCount = (botState.planPartialCount || 0) + 1;
 536: 
 537:     const end = result.points[result.points.length - 1];
 538:     const snap = (v: number) => Math.round(v / unit) * unit;
 539:     const hitGoal = goals.find(g => snap(g.x) === snap(end.x) && snap(g.y) === snap(end.y)) || { label: 'unknown' };
 540: 
 541:     return { points: result.points, goalLabel: hitGoal.label };
 542:   }
 543: 
 544:   // Both stages failed
 545:   botState.plannerStatus = 'failed';
 546:   botState.consecutiveFailures += 1;
 547:   botState.plannerFailures += 1;
 548:   botState.planFailedCount = (botState.planFailedCount || 0) + 1;
 549: 
 550:   return { points: null, goalLabel: 'none' };
 551: }
 552: 
 553: // Main logic coordinator
 554: export function updateBotAI(
 555:   botState: BotAIState,
 556:   player: any,
 557:   rows: any[],
 558:   timerLineY: number,
 559:   CONFIG: any,
 560:   delta: number,
 561:   elapsed: number,
 562:   width: number,
 563:   height: number
 564: ): { state: BotAIState; movement: { x: number; y: number }; events: string[]; debug: any } {
 565:   const events: string[] = [];
 566:   
 567:   // 1. Decoupled Player Sighting & Detection Systems
 568:   const distance = Math.hypot(player.x - botState.x, player.y - botState.y);
 569: 
 570:   const previousCycle = Math.floor(botState.scanTime / CONFIG.scanPeriodMs);
 571:   botState.scanTime += delta;
 572:   const scanTimeInCycle = botState.scanTime % CONFIG.scanPeriodMs;
 573:   
 574:   if (Math.floor(botState.scanTime / CONFIG.scanPeriodMs) !== previousCycle) {
 575:     events.push('scan');
 576:   }
 577: 
 578:   // Expansion duration is always scanDurationMs (680ms)
 579:   const scanDurationMs = CONFIG.scanDurationMs || 680;
 580:   const scanRadius = scanTimeInCycle < scanDurationMs
 581:     ? (scanTimeInCycle / scanDurationMs) * CONFIG.scanMaxRadius
 582:     : -1;
 583: 
 584:   // Swept radial wavefront intersection
 585:   let scanHit = false;
 586:   const wavefrontThickness = CONFIG.radarWaveThicknessPx !== undefined ? CONFIG.radarWaveThicknessPx : 16;
 587:   const playerRadius = CONFIG.playerRadius || 32;
 588: 
 589:   if (scanRadius >= 0 && botState.previousScanRadius >= 0 && scanRadius >= botState.previousScanRadius) {
 590:     const minD = botState.previousScanRadius - wavefrontThickness - playerRadius;
 591:     const maxD = scanRadius + playerRadius;
 592:     if (distance >= minD && distance <= maxD) {
 593:       scanHit = true;
 594:     }
 595:   } else if (scanRadius >= 0 && botState.previousScanRadius < 0) {
 596:     const maxD = scanRadius + playerRadius;
 597:     if (distance <= maxD) {
 598:       scanHit = true;
 599:     }
 600:   }
 601:   botState.previousScanRadius = scanRadius;
 602: 
 603:   // Continuous Near-Range Proximity Detection (edge-based or center fallback)
 604:   let inProximity = false;
 605:   if (CONFIG.nearDetectionGapPx !== undefined) {
 606:     const botRadius = CONFIG.botRadius || 30;
 607:     const edgeGap = distance - playerRadius - botRadius;
 608:     inProximity = edgeGap <= CONFIG.nearDetectionGapPx;
 609:   } else {
 610:     inProximity = distance < CONFIG.proximityRadius;
 611:   }
 612: 
 613:   // Increment telemetry counters for sensor hits on active frame
 614:   if (inProximity) {
 615:     botState.nearSensorHits = (botState.nearSensorHits || 0) + 1;
 616:   }
 617:   if (scanHit) {
 618:     botState.radarSensorHits = (botState.radarSensorHits || 0) + 1;
 619:   }
 620: 
 621:   // Sensed event triggers if either sensor detects the player
 622:   const isSensed = inProximity || scanHit;
 623:   
 624:   if (isSensed) {
 625:     botState.lastDetectedAt = elapsed;
 626:     botState.detectedPosition = { x: player.x, y: player.y };
 627: 
 628:     if (!botState.detected) {
 629:       botState.detected = true;
 630:       botState.awarenessEpisodeId = elapsed;
 631:       botState.hasExcited = false; // Prepared to play excitement when cooldown permits
 632:       botState.awarenessOpenedCount = (botState.awarenessOpenedCount || 0) + 1;
 633:       events.push('awareness_opened');
 634:     } else {
 635:       botState.awarenessRefreshedCount = (botState.awarenessRefreshedCount || 0) + 1;
 636:     }
 637:   }
 638: 
 639:   // Awareness memory decay guard with distance limit
 640:   const awarenessMemoryMs = CONFIG.awarenessMemoryMs !== undefined ? CONFIG.awarenessMemoryMs : 3200;
 641:   const loseDistancePx = CONFIG.loseDistancePx !== undefined ? CONFIG.loseDistancePx : 450;
 642:   if (botState.detected && (elapsed - botState.lastDetectedAt > awarenessMemoryMs || distance > loseDistancePx)) {
 643:     botState.detected = false;
 644:     botState.detectedPosition = null;
 645:     botState.awarenessClosedCount = (botState.awarenessClosedCount || 0) + 1;
 646:     events.push('awareness_closed');
 647:   }
 648: 
 649:   // 2. FSM Loop implementation
 650:   const isCooldownActive = elapsed < botState.recoveryCooldownUntil;
 651: 
 652:   if (botState.mode === 'SEARCH') {
 653:     // Move horizontally at patrol speeds
 654:     botState.patrolY = timerLineY - CONFIG.botBaseOffsetRows * CONFIG.rowGap;
 655:     
 656:     if (Math.abs(botState.x - botState.patrolX) < CONFIG.grid && elapsed - botState.sweepAt > CONFIG.botSweepMs) {
 657:       // Deterministic Sweep column selection
 658:       const seed = Math.floor(elapsed) + Math.floor(botState.x);
 659:       const choices = CONFIG.columns
 660:         .map((fraction: number) => fraction * width)
 661:         .filter((x: number) => Math.abs(x - botState.patrolX) > 40);
 662:       
 663:       if (choices.length) {
 664:         const idx = Math.floor(deterministicRandom(seed) * choices.length);
 665:         botState.patrolX = choices[idx];
 666:       } else {
 667:         botState.patrolX = width * 0.5;
 668:       }
 669:       botState.sweepAt = elapsed;
 670:     }
 671: 
 672:     if (elapsed - botState.lastRepath > CONFIG.botRepathMs) {
 673:       botState.lastRepath = elapsed;
 674:       // Plan patrol route
 675:       const { points } = planPath(botState, { x: botState.patrolX, y: botState.patrolY }, rows, CONFIG, width, height);
 676:       if (points && points.length >= 2) {
 677:         const metrics = pathMetrics(points);
 678:         botState.travel = {
 679:           points,
 680:           lengths: metrics.lengths,
 681:           total: metrics.total,
 682:           distance: 0,
 683:           segment: 0,
 684:         };
 685:       }
 686:     }
 687: 
 688:     // Follow patrol waypoints
 689:     if (botState.travel) {
 690:       botState.travel.distance += CONFIG.botPatrolSpeed * delta;
 691:       const point = pointOnPath(botState.travel);
 692:       botState.x = point.x;
 693:       botState.travel.segment = point.segment;
 694:       if (botState.travel.distance >= botState.travel.total) {
 695:         botState.travel = null;
 696:       }
 697:     } else {
 698:       // Direct drift to target if untravelled
 699:       const dx = botState.patrolX - botState.x;
 700:       const step = CONFIG.botPatrolSpeed * delta;
 701:       if (Math.abs(dx) > step) {
 702:         botState.x += Math.sign(dx) * step;
 703:       } else {
 704:         botState.x = botState.patrolX;
 705:       }
 706:     }
 707:     // Track timeline height continuously
 708:     botState.y = botState.patrolY;
 709: 
 710:     // Detection transition to Alert/Chase
 711:     if (botState.detected) {
 712:       if (!isCooldownActive) {
 713:         if (!botState.hasExcited) {
 714:           botState.mode = 'ALERT';
 715:           botState.burstUntil = elapsed + 300;
 716:           botState.hasExcited = true;
 717:           botState.alertStartedCount = (botState.alertStartedCount || 0) + 1;
 718:           events.push('lock');
 719:         } else {
 720:           // Direct Chase/Hold if excitement already played in this awareness block
 721:           const { points, goalLabel } = planPath(botState, player, rows, CONFIG, width, height);
 722:           if (points && points.length >= 2) {
 723:             const metrics = pathMetrics(points);
 724:             botState.travel = {
 725:               points,
 726:               lengths: metrics.lengths,
 727:               total: metrics.total,
 728:               distance: 0,
 729:               segment: 0,
 730:             };
 731:             botState.currentGoal = goalLabel;
 732:             botState.currentTargetCell = points[points.length - 1];
 733:             botState.mode = 'CHASE';
 734:             botState.chaseStartedCount = (botState.chaseStartedCount || 0) + 1;
 735:           } else {
 736:             botState.mode = 'HOLD';
 737:             botState.holdEnteredCount = (botState.holdEnteredCount || 0) + 1;
 738:           }
 739:         }
 740:       } else {
 741:         // Cooldown active: Bypass freeze-frame ALERT and go straight to pursuit!
 742:         const { points, goalLabel } = planPath(botState, player, rows, CONFIG, width, height);
 743:         if (points && points.length >= 2) {
 744:           const metrics = pathMetrics(points);
 745:           botState.travel = {
 746:             points,
 747:             lengths: metrics.lengths,
 748:             total: metrics.total,
 749:             distance: 0,
 750:             segment: 0,
 751:           };
 752:           botState.currentGoal = goalLabel;
 753:           botState.currentTargetCell = points[points.length - 1];
 754:           botState.mode = 'CHASE';
 755:           botState.chaseStartedCount = (botState.chaseStartedCount || 0) + 1;
 756:         } else {
 757:           botState.mode = 'HOLD';
 758:           botState.holdEnteredCount = (botState.holdEnteredCount || 0) + 1;
 759:         }
 760:       }
 761:     }
 762: 
 763:   } else if (botState.mode === 'ALERT') {
 764:     // Jitter/Anticipation freeze frame
 765:     if (elapsed >= botState.burstUntil) {
 766:       // Transition out of freeze frame
 767:       const { points, goalLabel } = planPath(botState, player, rows, CONFIG, width, height);
 768:       if (points && points.length >= 2) {
 769:         const metrics = pathMetrics(points);
 770:         botState.travel = {
 771:           points,
 772:           lengths: metrics.lengths,
 773:           total: metrics.total,
 774:           distance: 0,
 775:           segment: 0,
 776:         };
 777:         botState.currentGoal = goalLabel;
 778:         botState.currentTargetCell = points[points.length - 1];
 779:         botState.mode = 'CHASE';
 780:         botState.chaseStartedCount = (botState.chaseStartedCount || 0) + 1;
 781:       } else {
 782:         // Pathfinding failure during discovery -> Enter HOLD instead of resetting
 783:         botState.mode = 'HOLD';
 784:         botState.holdEnteredCount = (botState.holdEnteredCount || 0) + 1;
 785:       }
 786:     }
 787: 
 788:   } else if (botState.mode === 'CHASE') {
 789:     // Periodic repathing
 790:     if (elapsed - botState.lastRepath > CONFIG.botRepathMs) {
 791:       botState.lastRepath = elapsed;
 792:       const { points, goalLabel } = planPath(botState, player, rows, CONFIG, width, height);
 793:       if (points && points.length >= 2) {
 794:         const metrics = pathMetrics(points);
 795:         botState.travel = {
 796:           points,
 797:           lengths: metrics.lengths,
 798:           total: metrics.total,
 799:           distance: 0,
 800:           segment: 0,
 801:         };
 802:         botState.currentGoal = goalLabel;
 803:         botState.currentTargetCell = points[points.length - 1];
 804:       } else {
 805:         // Pathing lost -> Transition to threat HOLD
 806:         botState.travel = null;
 807:         botState.mode = 'HOLD';
 808:         botState.holdEnteredCount = (botState.holdEnteredCount || 0) + 1;
 809:       }
 810:     }
 811: 
 812:     // Waypoint movement
 813:     if (botState.travel) {
 814:       botState.travel.distance += CONFIG.botLockSpeed * delta;
 815:       const point = pointOnPath(botState.travel);
 816:       botState.x = point.x;
 817:       // Vertically, climb continuously along path nodes to keep grid alignment
 818:       const currentSegment = point.segment;
 819:       botState.travel.segment = currentSegment;
 820:       
 821:       const pts = botState.travel.points;
 822:       const currentP = pts[currentSegment];
 823:       const nextP = pts[currentSegment + 1] || currentP;
 824:       const segLength = botState.travel.lengths[currentSegment] || 1;
 825:       
 826:       // Interpolate Y cleanly to avoid visual jumping
 827:       const segDistance = botState.travel.distance - pts.slice(0, currentSegment).reduce((acc, _, i) => acc + botState.travel!.lengths[i], 0);
 828:       const ratio = clamp(segDistance / segLength, 0, 1);
 829:       botState.y = lerp(currentP.y, nextP.y, ratio);
 830: 
 831:       botState.currentWaypoint = nextP;
 832: 
 833:       // Completed chase waypoint path
 834:       if (botState.travel.distance >= botState.travel.total) {
 835:         botState.travel = null;
 836:         if (botState.detected) {
 837:           botState.mode = 'HOLD';
 838:           botState.holdEnteredCount = (botState.holdEnteredCount || 0) + 1;
 839:         } else {
 840:           botState.mode = 'SEARCH';
 841:         }
 842:       }
 843:     } else {
 844:       botState.mode = 'HOLD';
 845:       botState.holdEnteredCount = (botState.holdEnteredCount || 0) + 1;
 846:     }
 847: 
 848:     // Direct transition back to search if awareness fully lost
 849:     if (!botState.detected) {
 850:       botState.mode = 'SEARCH';
 851:     }
 852: 
 853:   } else if (botState.mode === 'HOLD') {
 854:     // Keep pace with the rising timeline vertically
 855:     botState.patrolY = timerLineY - CONFIG.botBaseOffsetRows * CONFIG.rowGap;
 856:     const targetY = botState.patrolY;
 857:     
 858:     const dY = targetY - botState.y;
 859:     const stepY = CONFIG.botPatrolSpeed * delta;
 860:     if (Math.abs(dY) > stepY) {
 861:       botState.y += Math.sign(dY) * stepY;
 862:     } else {
 863:       botState.y = targetY;
 864:     }
 865: 
 866:     // Move horizontally towards player position, unless blocked
 867:     const directionX = Math.sign(player.x - botState.x);
 868:     if (directionX !== 0) {
 869:       const stepX = CONFIG.botPatrolSpeed * delta * directionX;
 870:       const nextX = botState.x + stepX;
 871:       
 872:       // Check for immediate obstacle block in the direction of movement
 873:       const padding = 6;
 874:       const rects = obstacleRectsNear(botState.y - 12, botState.y + 12, rows, CONFIG);
 875:       if (!cellBlocked(nextX, botState.y, rects)) {
 876:         botState.x = nextX;
 877:       }
 878:     }
 879: 
 880:     // Periodically retry pathing
 881:     if (elapsed - botState.lastRepath > CONFIG.botRepathMs) {
 882:       botState.lastRepath = elapsed;
 883:       const { points, goalLabel } = planPath(botState, player, rows, CONFIG, width, height);
 884:       if (points && points.length >= 2) {
 885:         const metrics = pathMetrics(points);
 886:         botState.travel = {
 887:           points,
 888:           lengths: metrics.lengths,
 889:           total: metrics.total,
 890:           distance: 0,
 891:           segment: 0,
 892:         };
 893:         botState.currentGoal = goalLabel;
 894:         botState.currentTargetCell = points[points.length - 1];
 895:         botState.mode = 'CHASE'; // Re-engage chase!
 896:         botState.chaseStartedCount = (botState.chaseStartedCount || 0) + 1;
 897:       }
 898:     }
 899: 
 900:     // Escape HOLD if awareness fully lost
 901:     if (!botState.detected) {
 902:       botState.mode = 'SEARCH';
 903:     }
 904: 
 905:   } else if (botState.mode === 'RECOVER') {
 906:     // Movement towards the recovery platform spot
 907:     const dX = botState.recoveryTargetX - botState.x;
 908:     const dY = botState.recoveryTargetY - botState.y;
 909:     const speed = CONFIG.botPatrolSpeed;
 910:     const step = speed * delta;
 911: 
 912:     if (Math.hypot(dX, dY) > step) {
 913:       const angle = Math.atan2(dY, dX);
 914:       botState.x += Math.cos(angle) * step;
 915:       botState.y += Math.sin(angle) * step;
 916:     } else {
 917:       botState.x = botState.recoveryTargetX;
 918:       botState.y = botState.recoveryTargetY;
 919:       
 920:       // Recovered successfully!
 921:       botState.mode = 'SEARCH';
 922:     }
 923: 
 924:     // Timeout recovery
 925:     if (elapsed - botState.lastDisplacementCheckAt > 3000) {
 926:       botState.mode = 'SEARCH';
 927:     }
 928:   }
 929: 
 930:   // 3. Progress Monitor implementation (protects from thrashes, deadlocks, stalls)
 931:   if (botState.mode === 'CHASE' || botState.mode === 'SEARCH') {
 932:     // A. Check for movement stalls (displacement)
 933:     const displacementInterval = 1500;
 934:     if (elapsed - botState.lastDisplacementCheckAt > displacementInterval) {
 935:       const distanceMoved = Math.hypot(botState.x - botState.lastDisplacementX, botState.y - botState.lastDisplacementY);
 936:       
 937:       if (distanceMoved < 8) {
 938:         // Stalled completely! Trigger Recovery mode
 939:         triggerRecovery(botState, elapsed, rows, width, CONFIG);
 940:         events.push('recover');
 941:       } else {
 942:         botState.lastDisplacementCheckAt = elapsed;
 943:         botState.lastDisplacementX = botState.x;
 944:         botState.lastDisplacementY = botState.y;
 945:       }
 946:     }
 947: 
 948:     // B. Check for oscillation (rapid flipping of direction)
 949:     if (botState.travel) {
 950:       const currentDirX = Math.sign(player.x - botState.x);
 951:       if (currentDirX !== 0 && currentDirX !== botState.lastDirectionX) {
 952:         if (botState.lastDirectionX !== 0) {
 953:           botState.oscillationCounter += 1;
 954:           if (botState.oscillationCounter > 4) {
 955:             triggerRecovery(botState, elapsed, rows, width, CONFIG);
 956:             events.push('recover');
 957:           }
 958:         }
 959:         botState.lastDirectionX = currentDirX;
 960:       }
 961:     }
 962: 
 963:     // C. Check for waypoint index lock
 964:     if (botState.travel) {
 965:       if (botState.travel.segment === botState.lastWaypointIndex) {
 966:         botState.waypointStallTime += delta;
 967:         if (botState.waypointStallTime > 1800) {
 968:           triggerRecovery(botState, elapsed, rows, width, CONFIG);
 969:           events.push('recover');
 970:         }
 971:       } else {
 972:         botState.lastWaypointIndex = botState.travel.segment;
 973:         botState.waypointStallTime = 0;
 974:       }
 975:     }
 976: 
 977:     // D. Planner thrashing guard
 978:     if (botState.consecutiveFailures > 3) {
 979:       triggerRecovery(botState, elapsed, rows, width, CONFIG);
 980:       events.push('recover');
 981:     }
 982:   }
 983: 
 984:   return {
 985:     state: botState,
 986:     movement: { x: botState.x, y: botState.y },
 987:     events,
 988:     debug: {
 989:       mode: botState.mode,
 990:       detected: botState.detected,
 991:       episodeId: botState.awarenessEpisodeId,
 992:       plannerStatus: botState.plannerStatus,
 993:       failures: botState.plannerFailures,
 994:       nodesExpanded: botState.nodesExpanded,
 995:       lastPlanTime: botState.lastPlanTime,
 996:       currentGoal: botState.currentGoal,
 997:       oscillation: botState.oscillationCounter,
 998:       bounds: botState.currentSearchBounds,
 999:       // Telemetry counters
1000:       nearSensorHits: botState.nearSensorHits,
1001:       radarSensorHits: botState.radarSensorHits,
1002:       awarenessOpenedCount: botState.awarenessOpenedCount,
1003:       awarenessRefreshedCount: botState.awarenessRefreshedCount,
1004:       alertStartedCount: botState.alertStartedCount,
1005:       chaseStartedCount: botState.chaseStartedCount,
1006:       planSucceededCount: botState.planSucceededCount,
1007:       planPartialCount: botState.planPartialCount,
1008:       planFailedCount: botState.planFailedCount,
1009:       holdEnteredCount: botState.holdEnteredCount,
1010:       recoverEnteredCount: botState.recoverEnteredCount,
1011:       awarenessClosedCount: botState.awarenessClosedCount,
1012:       captureContactCount: botState.captureContactCount,
1013:     }
1014:   };
1015: }
1016: 
1017: // Initiate Recovery Mode logic
1018: function triggerRecovery(botState: BotAIState, elapsed: number, rows: any[], width: number, CONFIG: any) {
1019:   botState.mode = 'RECOVER';
1020:   botState.recoverEnteredCount = (botState.recoverEnteredCount || 0) + 1;
1021:   botState.travel = null;
1022:   botState.oscillationCounter = 0;
1023:   botState.consecutiveFailures = 0;
1024:   botState.waypointStallTime = 0;
1025:   botState.lastDisplacementCheckAt = elapsed;
1026:   botState.lastDisplacementX = botState.x;
1027:   botState.lastDisplacementY = botState.y;
1028: 
1029:   // Recovery goal: Nearest clear column center at current altitude
1030:   let bestColX = width * 0.5;
1031:   let minColDist = Infinity;
1032:   const cols = CONFIG.columns.map((c: number) => c * width);
1033:   const rects = obstacleRectsNear(botState.y - 15, botState.y + 15, rows, CONFIG);
1034: 
1035:   for (const cx of cols) {
1036:     if (!cellBlocked(cx, botState.y, rects)) {
1037:       const dist = Math.abs(cx - botState.x);
1038:       if (dist < minColDist) {
1039:         minColDist = dist;
1040:         bestColX = cx;
1041:       }
1042:     }
1043:   }
1044: 
1045:   botState.recoveryTargetX = bestColX;
1046:   botState.recoveryTargetY = botState.y;
1047:   botState.recoveryCooldownUntil = elapsed + (CONFIG.reacquireCooldownMs || 2200); // Ignore sights temporarily
1048: }

   1: /**

   6: import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
   7: import { CircuitClimbUnifiedRecorder, ConsoleOutputMode } from '../bot-ai-v2/CircuitClimbUnifiedRecorder';
   8: 
   9: export const CircuitClimbUnifiedConsole: React.FC = () => {
  10:   const recorder = CircuitClimbUnifiedRecorder.getInstance();
  11: 
  12:   const [isRecording, setIsRecording] = useState(recorder.getIsRecording());
  13:   const [consoleMode, setConsoleMode] = useState<ConsoleOutputMode>(recorder.getConsoleMode());
  14:   const [filter, setFilter] = useState('ALL');
  15:   const [events, setEvents] = useState(recorder.getEvents(filter));
  16:   const [copyStatus, setCopyStatus] = useState<string | null>(null);
  17: 
  18:   const logContainerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  19: 
  20:   useEffect(() => {
  21:     const interval = setInterval(() => {
  22:       setIsRecording(recorder.getIsRecording());
  23:       setEvents(recorder.getEvents(filter));
  24:     }, 100);
  25:     return () => clearInterval(interval);
  26:   }, [filter]);
  27: 
  28:   useEffect(() => {
  29:     if (logContainerRef.current) {
  30:       logContainerRef.current.scrollTop = logContainerRef.current.scrollHeight;
  31:     }
  32:   }, [events]);
  33: 
  34:   const handleToggleRecording = () => {
  35:     const next = !isRecording;
  36:     recorder.setRecording(next);
  37:     setIsRecording(next);
  38:   };
  39: 
  40:   const handleClear = () => {
  41:     recorder.resetRecorder();
  42:     setEvents([]);
  43:   };
  44: 
  45:   const formatEventLine = (e: any) => {
  46:     const sec = (e.simTimeMs / 1000).toFixed(3);
  47:     const seqStr = String(e.sequence).padStart(4, '0');
  48:     let details = e.event;
  49:     if (e.reason) details += ` (${e.reason})`;
  50:     if (e.botState) details += ` [state=${e.botState}]`;
  51:     if (e.targetVersion !== undefined) details += ` [tv=${e.targetVersion}]`;
  52:     if (e.intendedMovement) {
  53:       details += ` int=(${e.intendedMovement.x.toFixed(1)},${e.intendedMovement.y.toFixed(1)})`;
  54:     }
  55:     if (e.committedMovement) {
  56:       details += ` com=(${e.committedMovement.x.toFixed(1)},${e.committedMovement.y.toFixed(1)})`;
  57:     }
  58:     return `#${seqStr} ${sec}s [${e.source}] ${details}`;
  59:   };
  60: 
  61:   const buildExportText = (eventsToExport: any[]) => {
  62:     const summary = recorder.buildSummary();
  63:     const eventLines = eventsToExport.map(formatEventLine).join('\n');
  64: 
  65:     const summaryBlock = `
  66: === CIRCUIT CLIMB DIAGNOSTIC SUMMARY ===
  67: Session Duration: ${(summary.session.durationMs / 1000).toFixed(2)}s (${summary.session.frameCount} frames)
  68: Active Engine: ${summary.session.engine} | Difficulty: ${summary.session.difficulty}
  69: Viewport: ${summary.session.viewport.width}x${summary.session.viewport.height}
  70: 
  71: User Input:
  72:   - Platform Selections: ${summary.user.platformSelections} (Correct: ${summary.user.correctSelections}, Wrong: ${summary.user.wrongSelections})
  73:   - Restarts: ${summary.user.restarts}
  74: 
  75: Player:
  76:   - Moves Started: ${summary.player.moves} | Landings: ${summary.player.landings}
  77:   - Final Row Reached: ${summary.player.finalRow}
  78:   - Final Position: (${summary.player.finalPosition.x.toFixed(1)}, ${summary.player.finalPosition.y.toFixed(1)})
  79: 
  80: Bot Telemetry:
  81:   - Detection Events: ${summary.bot.detectionEvents}
  82:   - Awareness Episodes: ${summary.bot.awarenessEpisodes}
  83:   - State Transitions -> ALERT: ${summary.bot.alertCount}, PURSUE: ${summary.bot.pursueCount}, FINAL_APPROACH: ${summary.bot.finalApproachCount}, RECOVER: ${summary.bot.recoverCount}
  84:   - Target Version Changes: ${summary.bot.targetVersionChanges}
  85:   - Replans Requested: ${summary.bot.replansRequested} | Suppressed: ${summary.bot.replansSuppressed} | Plans Adopted: ${summary.bot.plansAdopted}
  86:   - Zero Intent Events: ${summary.bot.zeroIntentEvents}
  87:   - Stale Target Warnings: ${summary.bot.staleTargetWarnings}
  88:   - Vertical Progress Failures: ${summary.bot.verticalProgressFailures}
  89:   - Captures: ${summary.bot.captures}
  90: 
  91: Failure Classification:
  92:   ${summary.failureClassification.join(', ')}
  93: ========================================
  94: `;
  95: 
  96:     return `${eventLines}\n\n${summaryBlock}`;
  97:   };
  98: 
  99:   const handleCopyFullTrace = () => {
 100:     const fullEvents = recorder.getEvents('ALL');
 101:     const text = buildExportText(fullEvents);
 102:     navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
 103:     setCopyStatus('Full trace copied!');
 104:     setTimeout(() => setCopyStatus(null), 2000);
 105:   };
 106:

 110:     navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
 111:     setCopyStatus('Failure window copied!');
 112:     setTimeout(() => setCopyStatus(null), 2000);
 113:   };
 114: 
 115:   const displayedEvents = events.slice(-30);
 116: 
 117:   return (
 118:     <div
 119:       id="unifiedConsoleOverlay"
 120:       style={{
 121:         position: 'fixed',
 122:         bottom: '12px',
 123:         right: '12px',
 124:         width: '420px',
 125:         maxHeight: '380px',
 126:         backgroundColor: '#0F172A',
 127:         color: '#E2E8F0',
 128:         border: '1px solid #334155',
 129:         borderRadius: '8px',
 130:         padding: '12px',

 132:         zIndex: 9999,

 141:         <div style={{ fontWeight: 'bold', color: '#38BDF8', fontSize: '12px', letterSpacing: '0.05em' }}>
 142:           CIRCUIT CLIMB — UNIFIED EVENT TRACE
 143:         </div>
 144:         <div style={{ fontSize: '10px', color: isRecording ? '#4ADE80' : '#F87171' }}>
 145:           {isRecording ? '● REC' : '○ PAUSED'}
 146:         </div>
 147:       </div>
 148:

 150:         <button
 151:           onClick={handleToggleRecording}
 152:           style={{
 153:             padding: '4px 8px',
 154:             borderRadius: '4px',
 155:             border: '1px solid #475569',

 256:             <option value="ERRORS">ERRORS</option>
 257:           </select>

 259:       </div>
 260:

 262: 
 263:       <div
 264:         ref={logContainerRef}
 265:         style={{
 266:           flex: 1,
 267:           backgroundColor: '#020617',
 268:           border: '1px solid #1E293B',
 269:           borderRadius: '4px',

 274:           display: 'flex',

 277:         }}
 278:       >
 279:         {displayedEvents.length === 0 ? (
 280:           <div style={{ color: '#64748B', fontStyle: 'italic' }}>No events recorded.</div>
 281:         ) : (
 282:           displayedEvents.map((evt) => {
 283:             const isError = evt.source === 'ERROR' || evt.source === 'RUNTIME';
 284:             const isFailure =
 285:               evt.event.includes('STOPPED') ||
 286:               evt.event.includes('STALE') ||
 287:               evt.event.includes('REJECTED') ||
 288:               evt.event.includes('THRASH');
 289: 
 290:             const color = isError ? '#EF4444' : isFailure ? '#F59E0B' : '#94A3B8';
 291: 
 292:             return (
 293:               <div key={evt.sequence} style={{ color, whiteSpace: 'nowrap', overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis' }}>
 294:                 {formatEventLine(evt)}
 295:               </div>
 296:             );
 297:           })
 298:         )}
 299:       </div>
 300:     </div>
 301:   );
 302: };

 486:             <button id="againButton" className="primaryButton" type="button" onClick={restartGame}>
 487:               Climb again
 488:             </button>
 489:             <button
 490:               className="primaryButton"
 491:               type="button"
 492:               style={{
 493:                 marginTop: '10px',
 494:                 background: 'linear-gradient(180deg, #334155, #1e293b)',
 495:                 border: '1px solid rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.3)',
 496:                 color: '#cbd5e1',
 497:                 boxShadow: 'none',
 498:               }}
 499:               onClick={onExit}
 500:             >
 501:               Back to Menu
 502:             </button>
 503:           </div>
 504:         </section>
 505:       )}
 506: 
 507:       {/* C. Paused screen */}
 508:       {started && alive && paused && (
 509:         <section id="pauseOverlay" className="overlay">
 510:           <div className="overlayPanel">
 511:             <div className="eyebrow">Circuit suspended</div>
 512:             <h1>Paused</h1>
 513:             <button id="resumeButton" className="primaryButton" type="button" onClick={() => togglePause(false)}>
 514:               Resume
 515:             </button>
 516:             <button
 517:               className="primaryButton"
 518:               type="button"
 519:               style={{
 520:                 marginTop: '10px',
 521:                 background: 'linear-gradient(180deg, #334155, #1e293b)',
 522:                 border: '1px solid rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.3)',
 523:                 color: '#cbd5e1',
 524:                 boxShadow: 'none',
 525:               }}
 526:               onClick={onExit}
 527:             >
 528:               Exit to Menu
 529:             </button>
 530:           </div>
 531:         </section>
 532:       )}
 533:       {/* D. Unified Event Console */}
 534:       {showV2Telemetry && <CircuitClimbUnifiedConsole />}
 535:     </div>
 536:   );
 537: };
 538: 
 539: export default CircuitClimbSurface;

 204:       if (!value) return false;
 205:       if (typeof value !== 'string') return false;
 206:       if (value.includes('var(')) return false;
 207:       return true;

 292:     function clamp(value: number, min: number, max: number) {
 293:       return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value));
 294:     }
 295: 
 296:     function lerp(a: number, b: number, t: number) {
 297:       return a + (b - a) * t;
 298:     }
 299: 
 300:     function roundTo(value: number, digits = 1) {
 301:       const multiplier = 10 ** digits;
 302:       return Math.round(value * multiplier) / multiplier;
 303:     }

 305:     function readSavedViewScale() {
 306:       try {
 307:         const saved = Number(window.localStorage.getItem('circuitClimbViewScale'));
 308:         if (Number.isFinite(saved)) {
 309:           return clamp(saved, 80, 120);
 310:         }
 311:       } catch {
 312:         // Safe fallback
 313:       }
 314:       return 100;
 315:     }
 316: 
 317:     function readSavedRouteTurns() {
 318:       try {
 319:         const saved = Number(window.localStorage.getItem('circuitClimbRouteTurns'));
 320:         if (Number.isFinite(saved)) {
 321:           return clamp(Math.round(saved / 2) * 2, 6, 12);
 322:         }
 323:       } catch {
 324:         // Safe fallback
 325:       }
 326:       return 8;
 327:     }

 329:     function saveViewScale() {
 330:       try {
 331:         window.localStorage.setItem('circuitClimbViewScale', String(viewScalePercentInternal));
 332:         window.localStorage.setItem('circuitClimbRouteTurns', String(routeTurnCountInternal));
 333:       } catch {

 335:       }

 338:     function refreshTravelMetrics(activeTravel: any) {
 339:       if (!activeTravel || !activeTravel.points || activeTravel.points.length < 2) {
 340:         return;
 341:       }
 342:       const progress = activeTravel.total > 0
 343:         ? clamp(activeTravel.distance / activeTravel.total, 0, 1)
 344:         : 0;
 345:       const metrics = pathMetrics(activeTravel.points);
 346:       activeTravel.lengths = metrics.lengths;
 347:       activeTravel.total = metrics.total;
 348:       activeTravel.distance = metrics.total * progress;
 349:     }
 350: 
 351:     function reflowWorldForView(oldRowGap: number) {
 352:       if (!rows.length || !oldRowGap || oldRowGap === CONFIG.rowGap) {
 353:         return;
 354:       }
 355:       obstacleRevision += 1;
 356:       const verticalRatio = CONFIG.rowGap / oldRowGap;
 357: 
 358:       rows.forEach((row) => {
 359:         row.y = -row.index * CONFIG.rowGap;
 360:         row.platforms.forEach((platform: any) => {
 361:           platform.y = row.y;
 362:           platform.width = Math.min(CONFIG.platformWidth, width * 0.30);
 363:           platform.height = CONFIG.platformHeight;
 364:         });
 365:       });
 366: 
 367:       traces.forEach((trace) => {
 368:         trace.points.forEach((point: any) => { point.y *= verticalRatio; });
 369:       });
 370: 
 371:       particles.forEach((particle) => {
 372:         particle.y *= verticalRatio;
 373:         particle.vy *= verticalRatio;
 374:       });
 375: 
 376:       botTrail.forEach((point) => { point.y *= verticalRatio; });
 377:       echoes.forEach((echo) => {
 378:         echo.y0 *= verticalRatio;
 379:         echo.y1 *= verticalRatio;
 380:       });
 381: 
 382:       if (travel) {
 383:         if (travel.points) {
 384:           travel.points.forEach((point: any) => { point.y *= verticalRatio; });
 385:           refreshTravelMetrics(travel);
 386:         }
 387:         if (travel.from) travel.from.y *= verticalRatio;
 388:         if (travel.to) travel.to.y *= verticalRatio;
 389:       }
 390: 
 391:       player.y *= verticalRatio;
 392:       timerLineY *= verticalRatio;
 393: 
 394:       if (bot) {
 395:         bot.y *= verticalRatio;
 396:         bot.patrolY *= verticalRatio;
 397:         if (bot.travel) {
 398:           bot.travel.points.forEach((point: any) => { point.y *= verticalRatio; });
 399:           refreshTravelMetrics(bot.travel);
 400:         }
 401:       }
 402: 
 403:       if (!travel && player.platform) {
 404:         player.x = player.platform.x;
 405:         player.y = landingPoint(player.platform).y;
 406:       }
 407:       cameraY = player.y - height * CONFIG.cameraAnchor;
 408:     }
 409: 
 410:     function updateViewReadouts() {
 411:       setViewScalePercent(viewScalePercentInternal);
 412:       setRouteTurnCount(routeTurnCountInternal);
 413:     }
 414: 
 415:     function applyViewScale(percent: number, { reflow = true, persist = true } = {}) {
 416:       const nextPercent = clamp(Math.round(Number(percent) || 100), 80, 120);
 417:       const oldRowGap = CONFIG.rowGap;
 418:       const zoom = nextPercent / 100;
 419: 
 420:       viewScalePercentInternal = nextPercent;
 421: 
 422:       CONFIG.rowGap = BASE_VIEW.rowGap * zoom;
 423:       CONFIG.platformWidth = BASE_VIEW.platformWidth * (0.98 + 0.02 * zoom);
 424:       CONFIG.platformHeight = BASE_VIEW.platformHeight * Math.pow(zoom, 0.48);
 425:       CONFIG.playerRadius = BASE_VIEW.playerRadius * zoom;
 426:       CONFIG.botRadius = BASE_VIEW.botRadius * zoom;
 427:       CONFIG.routeSegmentGrid = BASE_VIEW.routeSegmentGrid * zoom;
 428:       CONFIG.routeMaxStraightRun = BASE_VIEW.routeMaxStraightRun * zoom;
 429:       CONFIG.routeHorizontalJitter = BASE_VIEW.routeHorizontalJitter * zoom;
 430:       CONFIG.routePlatformPadding = BASE_VIEW.routePlatformPadding;
 431:       CONFIG.hopHeight = BASE_VIEW.hopHeight * zoom;
 432:       CONFIG.wrongPenalty = BASE_VIEW.wrongPenalty * zoom;
 433: 
 434:       // Dynamic difficulty profile lookup and scaling
 435:       const diffMode = CONFIG.difficulty || 'NORMAL';
 436:       const profile = BOT_DETECTION_PROFILES[diffMode as 'EASY' | 'NORMAL' | 'HARD'] || BOT_DETECTION_PROFILES.NORMAL;
 437: 
 438:       CONFIG.nearDetectionGapPx = profile.nearDetectionGapPx * zoom;
 439:       CONFIG.scanMaxRadius = profile.radarMaxRadiusPx * zoom;
 440:       CONFIG.scanPeriodMs = profile.radarPeriodMs;
 441:       CONFIG.radarWaveThicknessPx = profile.radarWaveThicknessPx * zoom;
 442:       CONFIG.awarenessMemoryMs = profile.awarenessMemoryMs;
 443:       CONFIG.loseDistancePx = profile.loseDistancePx * zoom;
 444:       CONFIG.reacquireCooldownMs = profile.reacquireCooldownMs;
 445: 
 446:       // Proximity/contact radius are derived from the profile gaps/radii to match perfectly
 447:       CONFIG.proximityRadius = (CONFIG.botRadius + CONFIG.playerRadius + CONFIG.nearDetectionGapPx);
 448:       CONFIG.contactRadius = BASE_VIEW.contactRadius * zoom;
 449: 
 450:       CONFIG.routeTurnCount = routeTurnCountInternal;
 451:       CONFIG.cameraAnchor = lerp(0.585, 0.615, (nextPercent - 80) / 40);
 452: 
 453:       const hudScale = lerp(0.93, 1.03, (nextPercent - 80) / 40);
 454:       document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--live-ui-scale', hudScale.toFixed(3));
 455: 
 456:       if (reflow) {
 457:         reflowWorldForView(oldRowGap);
 458:       }
 459:       updateViewReadouts();
 460:       if (persist) {
 461:         saveViewScale();
 462:       }
 463:     }
 464: 
 465:     function applyRouteTurnCount(value: number, { persist = true } = {}) {
 466:       routeTurnCountInternal = clamp(Math.round(Number(value) / 2) * 2 || 8, 6, 12);
 467:       CONFIG.routeTurnCount = routeTurnCountInternal;
 468:       updateViewReadouts();
 469:       if (persist) {
 470:         saveViewScale();
 471:       }
 472:     }

 482:           platformHeight: roundTo(CONFIG.platformHeight),
 483:           playerRadius: roundTo(CONFIG.playerRadius),
 484:           botRadius: roundTo(CONFIG.botRadius),

 486:           routeTurnCount: CONFIG.routeTurnCount,
 487:           routeMaxStraightRun: roundTo(CONFIG.routeMaxStraightRun),
 488:           cameraAnchor: roundTo(CONFIG.cameraAnchor, 3),
 489:           proximityRadius: roundTo(CONFIG.proximityRadius),
 490:           contactRadius: roundTo(CONFIG.contactRadius),
 491:           scanMaxRadius: roundTo(CONFIG.scanMaxRadius),
 492:         },
 493:         instruction: 'Paste this JSON back into the project-manager chat so the framing and corner count can be frozen into the next build.',
 494:       };
 495:     }
