 501: 
 502:     function randomInt(min: number, max: number) {
 503:       return Math.floor(min + Math.random() * (max - min + 1));
 504:     }
 505: 
 506:     function shuffle(values: any[]) {
 507:       for (let i = values.length - 1; i > 0; i -= 1) {
 508:         const j = randomInt(0, i);
 509:         [values[i], values[j]] = [values[j], values[i]];
 510:       }
 511:       return values;
 512:     }
 513: 
 514:     function roundedRectPath(context: CanvasRenderingContext2D, x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number, radius: number) {
 515:       const r = Math.min(radius, w / 2, h / 2);
 516:       context.beginPath();
 517:       context.moveTo(x + r, y);
 518:       context.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, r);
 519:       context.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, r);
 520:       context.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, r);
 521:       context.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, r);
 522:       context.closePath();
 523:     }
 524: 
 525:     function targetBandFor(rowIndex: number) {
 526:       return Math.floor(Math.max(0, rowIndex - 1) / 6);
 527:     }
 528:     function targetFor(rowIndex: number) {
 529:       return Math.min(20, 10 + 2 * targetBandFor(rowIndex));
 530:     }
 531: 
 532: 
 533:     function makeRow(index: number) {
 534:       const y = -index * CONFIG.rowGap;
 535:       const platforms = CONFIG.columns.map((fraction, column) => ({
 536:         row: index,
 537:         column,
 538:         x: fraction * width,
 539:         y,
 540:         width: Math.min(CONFIG.platformWidth, width * 0.30),
 541:         height: CONFIG.platformHeight,
 542:         value: null as number | null,
 543:         correct: false,
 544:         dead: false,
 545:         powered: false,
 546:         selected: false,
 547:         litAt: -1000,
 548:       }));
 549: 
 550:       const row: any = {
 551:         index,
 552:         y,
 553:         platforms,
 554:         id: `row-${index}`,
 555:         disabledOptionIndexes: [] as number[],
 556:         status: 'future' as 'buffer' | 'future' | 'active' | 'resolved-correct' | 'resolved-wrong' | 'passed',
 557:       };
 558: 
 559:       if (index >= 1) {
 560:         const targetEventId = targetBandFor(index);
 561:         const maxTargetValue = Math.min(20, 10 + 2 * targetEventId);
 562: 
 563:         let incomingPlayerValue = 0;
 564:         if (index === 1) {
 565:           incomingPlayerValue = randomInt(1, Math.max(2, maxTargetValue - 1));
 566:         } else {
 567:           const prev = rows[index - 1];
 568:           if (prev && prev.problemSnapshot) {
 569:             incomingPlayerValue = randomInt(1, Math.max(2, maxTargetValue - 1));
 570:           } else {
 571:             incomingPlayerValue = randomInt(1, Math.max(2, maxTargetValue - 1));
 572:           }
 573:         }
 574:         
 575:         let snapshot = CircuitClimbMathAdapter.requestAdditionProblem(
 576:           index,
 577:           incomingPlayerValue,
 578:           maxTargetValue,
 579:           targetEventId
 580:         );
 581: 
 582:         if (!snapshot) {
 583:           console.error("CIRCUIT_CLIMB_MATH_ENGINE_FAILURE", { index, incomingPlayerValue, maxTargetValue });
 584:           snapshot = {
 585:             problemId: `fallback-${index}`,
 586:             operation: "addition",
 587:             rowIndex: index,
 588:             targetEventId,
 589:             playerValue: incomingPlayerValue,
 590:             targetValue: incomingPlayerValue + 1,
 591:             choices: [1, 2, 3],
 592:             correctChoiceIndex: 0,
 593:             correctPlatformValue: 1
 594:           };
 595:           snapshot.choices[0] = 1;
 596:           snapshot.choices[1] = 2;
 597:           snapshot.choices[2] = 3;
 598:         }
 599: 
 600:         row.problemSnapshot = snapshot;
 601:         row.targetEventId = snapshot.targetEventId;
 602:         row.targetValue = snapshot.targetValue;
 603:         row.incomingPlayerValue = snapshot.playerValue;
 604:         row.correctPlatformValue = snapshot.correctPlatformValue;
 605:         row.correctOptionIndex = snapshot.correctChoiceIndex;
 606:         row.options = snapshot.choices;
 607: 
 608:         row.platforms.forEach((platform: any, colIdx: number) => {
 609:           if (colIdx < 3) {
 610:             platform.value = snapshot!.choices[colIdx];
 611:             platform.correct = colIdx === snapshot!.correctChoiceIndex;
 612:           }
 613:         });
 614:       }
 615: 
 616:       return row;
 617:     }
 618: 
 619:     function ensureRows() {
 620:       let added = false;
 621:       while (nextRowIndex <= player.row + 6) {
 622:         rows.push(makeRow(nextRowIndex));
 623:         nextRowIndex += 1;
 624:         added = true;
 625:       }
 626:       if (added) {
 627:         obstacleRevision += 1;
 628:       }
 629:     }
 630: 
 631:     function getRow(index: number) {
 632:       return rows.find((row) => row.index === index) || null;
 633:     }
 634: 
 635:     function rowAbove() {
 636:       return getRow(player.row + 1);
 637:     }
 638: 
 639: 
 640:     function validateActiveCircuitClimbRow(row: any, committedPlayerValue: number) {
 641:       if (!row) return;
 642:       let error = null;
 643:       if (row.incomingPlayerValue !== committedPlayerValue) error = 'incomingPlayerValue mismatch';
 644:       else if (row.correctPlatformValue !== row.targetValue - row.incomingPlayerValue) error = 'correctPlatformValue mismatch';
 645:       else if (row.options[row.correctOptionIndex] !== row.correctPlatformValue) error = 'correctOptionIndex mismatch';
 646:       else if (row.options.filter((o: number) => o === row.correctPlatformValue).length !== 1) error = 'multiple or zero correct options';
 647:       
 648:       if (error) {
 649:         console.error('CIRCUIT_CLIMB_INVALID_ACTIVE_ROW', {
 650:           id: row.id,
 651:           index: row.index,
 652:           targetEventId: row.targetEventId,
 653:           targetValue: row.targetValue,
 654:           incomingPlayerValue: row.incomingPlayerValue,
 655:           options: row.options,
 656:           correctOptionIndex: row.correctOptionIndex,
 657:           computedRequiredOption: row.targetValue - row.incomingPlayerValue,
 658:           reason: error
 659:         });
 660:       }
 661:     }
 662: 
 663:     function armNextRow() {
 664:       const row = rowAbove();
 665:       if (!row) return;
 666:       validateActiveCircuitClimbRow(row, player.value);
 667:       row.platforms.forEach((platform) => {
 668:         platform.dead = false;
 669:         platform.selected = false;
 670:       });
 671:     }
 672: 
 673:     function updateHud() {
 674:       const activeRow = getRow(player.row + 1);
 675:       if (!activeRow) return;
 676:       
 677:       const target = activeRow.targetValue;
 678:       const targetEventId = activeRow.targetEventId !== undefined ? activeRow.targetEventId : targetBandFor(player.row + 1);
 679: 
 680:       if (targetEventId !== targetPresentation.targetEventId) {
 681:         targetPresentation.targetValue = target;
 682:         targetPresentation.targetEventId = targetEventId;
 683:         targetPresentation.phase = 'dominant-enter';
 684:         targetPresentation.phaseStartedAt = elapsed;
 685:       }
 686:       setPlayerValue(player.value);
 687:       setTargetValue(target);
 688:       setScore(player.row);
 689:       setMovementMode(engineMovementMode);
 690:     }
 691: 
 692:     function setMessage(text: string, tone: 'neutral' | 'success' | 'error' = 'neutral', duration = 0) {
 693:       setMessageText(text);
 694:       setMessageTone(tone);
 695:       messageTimer = duration > 0 ? elapsed + duration : 0;
 696:     }
 697: 
 698:     function getAudioContext() {
 699:       if (!engineSoundEnabled) return null;
 700:       if (!audioContext) {
 701:         try {
 702:           audioContext = new (window.AudioContext || (window as any).webkitAudioContext)();
 703:         } catch {
 704:           engineSoundEnabled = false;
 705:           setSoundEnabled(false);
 706:           return null;
 707:         }
 708:       }
 709:       if (audioContext.state === 'suspended') audioContext.resume();
 710:       return audioContext;
 711:     }
 712: 
 713:     function tone(frequency: number, duration = 0.16, volume = 0.05, type: OscillatorType = 'sine', delay = 0) {
 714:       const audio = getAudioContext();
 715:       if (!audio) return;
 716:       const start = audio.currentTime + delay;
 717:       const oscillator = audio.createOscillator();
 718:       const gain = audio.createGain();
 719:       oscillator.type = type;
 720:       oscillator.frequency.setValueAtTime(frequency, start);
 721:       gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, start);
 722:       gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(volume, start + 0.015);
 723:       gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, start + duration);
 724:       oscillator.connect(gain);
 725:       gain.connect(audio.destination);
 726:       oscillator.start(start);
 727:       oscillator.stop(start + duration + 0.03);
 728:     }
 729: 
 730:     const sound = {
 731:       launch() {
 732:         tone(310, 0.12, 0.045, 'sine');
 733:         tone(465, 0.18, 0.035, 'triangle', 0.05);
 734:       },
 735:       corner() {
 736:         tone(1300, 0.04, 0.018, 'triangle');
 737:       },
 738:       correct() {
 739:         tone(392, 0.36, 0.05, 'sine');
 740:         tone(494, 0.38, 0.045, 'sine', 0.07);
 741:         tone(659, 0.48, 0.05, 'sine', 0.15);
 742:       },
 743:       wrong() {
 744:         tone(230, 0.28, 0.055, 'sawtooth');
 745:         tone(174, 0.35, 0.045, 'triangle', 0.08);
 746:       },
 747:       scan() {
 748:         tone(760, 0.34, 0.026, 'sine');
 749:         tone(770, 0.38, 0.018, 'sine');
 750:       },
 751:       lock() {
 752:         tone(1046, 0.16, 0.048, 'triangle');
 753:         tone(1318, 0.22, 0.035, 'sine', 0.05);
 754:       },
 755:       danger() {
 756:         tone(90, 0.7, 0.07, 'sine');
 757:         tone(55, 0.9, 0.05, 'triangle');
 758:       },
 759:     };
 760: 
 761:     function resize() { console.log("resize CALLED!", {app: !!app, rect: app?.getBoundingClientRect()});
 762:       const rect = app.getBoundingClientRect();
 763:       if (!rect.width || !rect.height) return;
 764: 
 765:       const oldWidth = width;
 766:       width = rect.width;
 767:       height = rect.height;
 768:       dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
 769:       canvas.width = Math.round(width * dpr);
 770:       canvas.height = Math.round(height * dpr);
 771:       canvas.style.width = `${width}px`;
 772:       canvas.style.height = `${height}px`;
 773:       ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
 774: 
 775:       if (rows.length > 0) {
 776:         const ratio = oldWidth > 0 ? width / oldWidth : 1;
 777:         rows.forEach((row) => {
 778:           row.platforms.forEach((platform: any) => {
 779:             platform.x = CONFIG.columns[platform.column] * width;
 780:             platform.width = Math.min(CONFIG.platformWidth, width * 0.30);
 781:           });
 782:         });
 783: 
 784:         if (oldWidth > 0 && ratio !== 1) {
 785:           traces.forEach((trace) => trace.points.forEach((point: any) => { point.x *= ratio; }));
 786:           particles.forEach((particle) => { particle.x *= ratio; });
 787:           botTrail.forEach((point) => { point.x *= ratio; });
 788:           echoes.forEach((echo) => { echo.x0 *= ratio; echo.x1 *= ratio; });
 789:           if (bot) {
 790:             bot.x *= ratio;
 791:             bot.patrolX *= ratio;
 792:             if (bot.travel) bot.travel.points.forEach((point: any) => { point.x *= ratio; });
 793:           }
 794:           if (travel) {
 795:             if (travel.points) travel.points.forEach((point: any) => { point.x *= ratio; });
 796:             if (travel.from) travel.from.x *= ratio;
 797:             if (travel.to) travel.to.x *= ratio;
 798:           }
 799:         }
 800:         if (!travel && player.platform) player.x = player.platform.x;
 801:         else player.x *= ratio;
 802:       }
 803:       if (!engineStarted || player.row === 0) cameraY = player.y - height * CONFIG.cameraAnchor;
 804:     }
 805: 
 806:     function isBotPositionBlocked(x: number, y: number) {
 807:       const margin = CONFIG.botRadius + 4; // botRadius + navMarginPx
 808:       return rows.some((row) => row.platforms.some((platform: any) => {
 809:         if (platform.row === 0 && platform.column !== 1) return false;
 810:         const left = platform.x - platform.width / 2 - margin;
 811:         const right = platform.x + platform.width / 2 + margin;
 812:         const top = platform.y - margin;
 813:         const bottom = platform.y + platform.height + margin;
 814:         return x > left && x < right && y > top && y < bottom;
 815:       }));
 816:     }
 817: 
 818:     function calculateRepairedBotSpawn(forceBringUp = false) {
 819:       const isBringUp = forceBringUp || CONFIG.bringUpStage !== 'NORMAL';
 820:       if (isBringUp) {
 821:         return {
 822:           x: width / 2,
 823:           y: player.y + 140
 824:         };
 825:       }
 826: 
 827:       // 1. Desired dramatic spawn below the player
 828:       const desiredY = player.y + (CONFIG.botSpawnOffsetRows + CONFIG.botInitialRowGap) * CONFIG.rowGap;
 829: 
 830:       // 2. Lowest visible legal bot center (derived from viewport constraints)
 831:       // visibleBottom is height - 68. We want screenY to be above visibleBottom, and inside visible viewport.
 832:       // So maxScreenY = height - 68 - CONFIG.botRadius - 10 (safe padding).
 833:       // Since screenY = worldY - cameraY, maxWorldY = cameraY + maxScreenY.
 834:       const maxScreenY = height - 68 - CONFIG.botRadius - 10;
 835:       const maxVisibleWorldY = cameraY + maxScreenY;
 836: 
 837:       // Desired spawn clamped to the visible legal range.
 838:       // Let's also ensure it is below the player (with some buffer like playerRadius + botRadius + 30).
 839:       const minWorldY = player.y + CONFIG.playerRadius + CONFIG.botRadius + 30;
 840:       let clampedY = clamp(desiredY, minWorldY, maxVisibleWorldY);
 841: 
 842:       // Columns are: CONFIG.columns[randomInt(0, CONFIG.columns.length - 1)] * width
 843:       const columnsX = CONFIG.columns.map(c => c * width);
 844:       
 845:       // Try to find a completely unblocked spawn location in a search grid
 846:       let bestX = columnsX[randomInt(0, columnsX.length - 1)];
 847:       let bestY = clampedY;
 848:       let found = false;
 849: 
 850:       const searchOffsetsY = [0, -15, 15, -30, 30, -45, 45, -60, 60];
 851:       const candidatesX = [columnsX[1], columnsX[0], columnsX[2], width / 2];
 852: 
 853:       for (const dy of searchOffsetsY) {
 854:         const testY = clampedY + dy;
 855:         if (testY < minWorldY || testY > maxVisibleWorldY) continue;
 856: 
 857:         for (const testX of candidatesX) {
 858:           if (!isBotPositionBlocked(testX, testY)) {
 859:             bestX = testX;
 860:             bestY = testY;
 861:             found = true;
 862:             break;
 863:           }
 864:         }
 865:         if (found) break;
 866:       }
 867: 
 868:       return { x: bestX, y: bestY };
 869:     }
 870: 
 871:     function restart({ preserveOverlay = false } = {}) {
 872:       setShowViewSettings(false);
 873:       rows = [];
 874:       obstacleRevision = 0;
 875:       firstFrameTraced = false;
 876:       traces = [];
 877:       particles = [];
 878:       travel = null;
 879:       resolveAt = 0;
 880:       nextRowIndex = 0;
 881:       elapsed = 0;
 882:       lastTimestamp = 0;
 883:       
 884:       
 885:       engineAlive = true;
 886:       enginePaused = false;
 887:       setAlive(true);
 888:       setPaused(false);
 889: 
 890:       ensureRows();
 891:       const baseRow = getRow(0);
 892:       const basePlatform = baseRow.platforms[1];
 893:       baseRow.platforms[0].dead = true;
 894:       baseRow.platforms[2].dead = true;
 895:       basePlatform.value = null;
 896:       basePlatform.powered = true;
 897: 
 898:       player.row = 0;
 899:       player.platform = basePlatform;
 900:       player.x = basePlatform.x;
 901:       player.y = basePlatform.y - CONFIG.playerRadius - 3;
 902:       const firstRow = getRow(1);
 903:       player.value = firstRow && firstRow.problemSnapshot ? firstRow.problemSnapshot.playerValue : 4;
 904:       player.pulseAt = -1000;
 905: 
 906:       playerNumberPresentation.phase = 'visible';
 907:       playerNumberPresentation.phaseStartedAt = 0;
 908:       playerNumberPresentation.displayedValue = player.value;
 909:       playerNumberPresentation.pendingValue = null;
 910:       targetPresentation.targetValue = firstRow && firstRow.problemSnapshot ? firstRow.problemSnapshot.targetValue : 10;
 911:       targetPresentation.targetEventId = targetBandFor(1);
 912:       targetPresentation.phase = 'dominant-enter';
 913:       targetPresentation.phaseStartedAt = 0;
 914:       targetPresentation.progress = 0;
 915: 
 916: 
 917:       timerSpeed = CONFIG.timerBaseSpeed;
 918:       timerLineY = player.y + CONFIG.rowGap * CONFIG.timerStartRows;
 919:       cameraY = player.y - height * CONFIG.cameraAnchor;
 920: 
 921:       const spawnPos = calculateRepairedBotSpawn();
 922: 
 923:       bot = initBotAIState(spawnPos.x, spawnPos.y, player.x);
 924:       botContextV2 = CONFIG.aiImplementation !== 'LEGACY' ? createBotContextV2() : null;
 925:       botTrail = [];
 926:       echoes = [];
 927: 
 928:       BotFlightRecorder.getInstance().recordEvent(
 929:         0,
 930:         CONFIG.aiImplementation as 'GREENFIELD_V2' | 'LEGACY',
 931:         botContextV2?.currentState || 'SEARCH',
 932:         'NONE',
 933:         'LIFECYCLE',
 934:         'SIMULATION_RESTARTED',
 935:         `Simulation restarted. Resetting game states, player position, platforms, and bot context.`,
 936:         spawnPos,
 937:         { x: player.x, y: player.y },
 938:         CONFIG.botRadius,
 939:         CONFIG.playerRadius,
 940:         botContextV2?.awareness?.id || 0,
 941:         obstacleRevision,
 942:         botContextV2?.debug?.plannerStatus || '',
 943:         botContextV2?.pathIndex || 0
 944:       );
 945: 
 946:       ensureRows();
 947:       armNextRow();
 948:       updateHud();
 949:       setMessage('Tap the platform that completes the equation.');
 950: 
 951:       if (!preserveOverlay) {
 952:         setStarted(true);
 953:         engineStarted = true;
 954:       }
 955:     }
 956: 
 957:     function beginGame() {
 958:       setStarted(true);
 959:       engineStarted = true;
 960:       restart();
 961:       try { getAudioContext(); } catch { engineSoundEnabled = false; setSoundEnabled(false); }
 962:     }
 963: 
 964:     function landingPoint(platform: any) {
 965:       return {
 966:         x: platform.x,
 967:         y: platform.y - CONFIG.playerRadius - 3,
 968:       };
 969:     }
 970: 
 971:     function platformCollisionRects() {
 972:       const pad = CONFIG.routePlatformPadding;
 973:       const rects: any[] = [];
 974:       rows.forEach((row) => row.platforms.forEach((platform) => {
 975:         if (platform.row === 0 && platform.column !== 1) return;
 976:         rects.push({
 977:           left: platform.x - platform.width / 2 - pad,
 978:           right: platform.x + platform.width / 2 + pad,
 979:           top: platform.y - pad,
 980:           bottom: platform.y + platform.height + pad,
 981:         });
 982:       }));
 983:       return rects;
 984:     }
 985: 
 986:     function segmentHitsRect(a: any, b: any, rect: any) {
 987:       if (a.x === b.x) {
 988:         if (a.x <= rect.left || a.x >= rect.right) return false;
 989:         const top = Math.min(a.y, b.y);
 990:         const bottom = Math.max(a.y, b.y);
 991:         return bottom > rect.top && top < rect.bottom;
 992:       }
 993:       if (a.y === b.y) {
 994:         if (a.y <= rect.top || a.y >= rect.bottom) return false;
 995:         const left = Math.min(a.x, b.x);
 996:         const right = Math.max(a.x, b.x);
 997:         return right > rect.left && left < rect.right;
 998:       }
 999:       return true;
1000:     }
1001: 
1002:     function pathIsClear(points: any[]) {
1003:       const rects = platformCollisionRects();
1004:       for (let i = 1; i < points.length; i += 1) {
1005:         for (const rect of rects) {
1006:           if (segmentHitsRect(points[i - 1], points[i], rect)) return false;
1007:         }
1008:       }
1009:       return true;
1010:     }
1011: 
1012:     function cleanCircuitPath(points: any[]) {
1013:       const out = [points[0]];
1014:       for (let i = 1; i < points.length; i += 1) {
1015:         const point = points[i];
1016:         const last = out[out.length - 1];
1017:         if (point.x === last.x && point.y === last.y) continue;
1018:         if (out.length >= 2) {
1019:           const previous = out[out.length - 2];
1020:           if ((previous.x === last.x && last.x === point.x) ||
1021:               (previous.y === last.y && last.y === point.y)) {
1022:             out[out.length - 1] = point;
1023:             continue;
1024:           }
1025:         }
1026:         out.push(point);
1027:       }
1028:       return out;
1029:     }
1030: 
1031:     function destinationCorridors(row: any) {
1032:       const padding = CONFIG.routePlatformPadding;
1033:       const rectangles = row.platforms
1034:         .map((platform: any) => ({
1035:           left: platform.x - platform.width / 2 - padding,
1036:           right: platform.x + platform.width / 2 + padding,
1037:         }))
1038:         .sort((first: any, second: any) => first.left - second.left);
1039: 
1040:       const corridors: any[] = [];
1041:       let cursor = 2;
1042: 
1043:       rectangles.forEach((rectangle: any) => {
1044:         if (rectangle.left - cursor >= 12) {
1045:           corridors.push({
1046:             left: cursor,
1047:             right: rectangle.left,
1048:             center: (cursor + rectangle.left) / 2,
1049:           });
1050:         }
1051:         cursor = Math.max(cursor, rectangle.right);
1052:       });
1053: 
1054:       const rightEdge = width - 2;
1055:       if (rightEdge - cursor >= 12) {
1056:         corridors.push({
1057:           left: cursor,
1058:           right: rightEdge,
1059:           center: (cursor + rightEdge) / 2,
1060:         });
1061:       }
1062:       return corridors;
1063:     }
1064: 
1065:     function chooseDestinationCorridor(row: any, targetX: number, startX: number) {
1066:       const corridors = destinationCorridors(row);
1067:       if (!corridors.length) {
1068:         const edge = targetX < width / 2 ? width * 0.965 : width * 0.035;
1069:         return {
1070:           left: edge - 12,
1071:           right: edge + 12,
1072:           center: edge,
1073:         };
1074:       }
1075:       return corridors
1076:         .slice()
1077:         .sort((first, second) => {
1078:           const firstScore =
1079:             Math.abs(first.center - targetX) * 0.72 +
1080:             Math.abs(first.center - startX) * 0.28;
1081:           const secondScore =
1082:             Math.abs(second.center - targetX) * 0.72 +
1083:             Math.abs(second.center - startX) * 0.28;
1084:           return firstScore - secondScore;
1085:         })[0];
1086:     }
1087: 
1088:     function buildSteppedRoute(from: any, to: any, destinationPlatform: any, corridor: any) {
1089:       const turns = clamp(Math.round(CONFIG.routeTurnCount / 2) * 2, 6, 12);
1090:       const horizontalCount = turns / 2;
1091:       const verticalCount = horizontalCount + 1;
1092:       const destinationRow = getRow(destinationPlatform.row);
1093: 
1094:       const landingY = to.y;
1095:       const apexY =
1096:         destinationRow.y -
1097:         CONFIG.playerRadius -
1098:         Math.max(18, CONFIG.routePlatformPadding * 1.8);
1099: 
1100:       const crossingStartY =
1101:         destinationRow.y +
1102:         CONFIG.platformHeight +
1103:         CONFIG.routePlatformPadding +
1104:         9;
1105: 
1106:       const midCrossY =
1107:         destinationRow.y +
1108:         CONFIG.platformHeight * 0.34;
1109: 
1110:       const verticalEndpoints = [];
1111:       const preCorridorVerticalCount = verticalCount - 3;
1112: 
1113:       for (let index = 1; index <= preCorridorVerticalCount; index += 1) {
1114:         verticalEndpoints.push(
1115:           lerp(from.y, crossingStartY, index / preCorridorVerticalCount),
1116:         );
1117:       }
1118:       verticalEndpoints.push(midCrossY, apexY, landingY);
1119: 
1120:       const corridorInset = Math.min(
1121:         16,
1122:         Math.max(3, (corridor.right - corridor.left) * 0.2),
1123:       );
1124: 
1125:       let corridorA = clamp(
1126:         corridor.center - corridorInset,
1127:         corridor.left + 2,
1128:         corridor.right - 2,
1129:       );
1130: 
1131:       let corridorB = clamp(
1132:         corridor.center + corridorInset,
1133:         corridor.left + 2,
1134:         corridor.right - 2,
1135:       );
1136: 
1137:       if (Math.abs(corridorB - corridorA) < 4) {
1138:         corridorA = corridor.center;
1139:         corridorB = clamp(
1140:           corridor.center + (to.x < corridor.center ? -4 : 4),
1141:           corridor.left + 1.5,
1142:           corridor.right - 1.5,
1143:         );
1144:       }
1145: 
1146:       if (to.x < corridor.center) {
1147:         [corridorA, corridorB] = [corridorB, corridorA];
1148:       }
1149: 
1150:       const horizontalEndpoints = [];
1151:       const freeHorizontalCount = horizontalCount - 3;
1152:       let currentX = from.x;
1153: 
1154:       for (let index = 0; index < freeHorizontalCount; index += 1) {
1155:         const progress = (index + 1) / (freeHorizontalCount + 1);
1156:         const guide = lerp(from.x, corridorA, progress);
1157:         const alternatingDirection = index % 2 === 0 ? -1 : 1;
1158:         const targetDirection = Math.sign(corridorA - from.x) || 1;
1159: 
1160:         let candidate =
1161:           guide +
1162:           alternatingDirection *
1163:             targetDirection *
1164:             CONFIG.routeHorizontalJitter;
1165: 
1166:         candidate = clamp(
1167:           candidate,
1168:           width * 0.045,
1169:           width * 0.955,
1170:         );
1171: 
1172:         const deltaX = candidate - currentX;
1173:         const maximumRun = CONFIG.routeMaxStraightRun;
1174:         const minimumRun = Math.min(24, maximumRun * 0.42);
1175: 
1176:         if (Math.abs(deltaX) > maximumRun) {
1177:           candidate = currentX + Math.sign(deltaX) * maximumRun;
1178:         } else if (Math.abs(deltaX) < minimumRun) {
1179:           candidate = currentX + alternatingDirection * minimumRun;
1180:           candidate = clamp(candidate, width * 0.045, width * 0.955);
1181:         }
1182: 
1183:         horizontalEndpoints.push(candidate);
1184:         currentX = candidate;
1185:       }
1186: 
1187:       horizontalEndpoints.push(corridorA, corridorB, to.x);
1188: 
1189:       const points = [{ x: from.x, y: from.y }];
1190:       let currentPoint = points[0];
1191: 
1192:       for (let segmentIndex = 0; segmentIndex < horizontalCount; segmentIndex += 1) {
1193:         const nextY = verticalEndpoints[segmentIndex];
1194:         if (nextY !== currentPoint.y) {
1195:           currentPoint = { x: currentPoint.x, y: nextY };
1196:           points.push(currentPoint);
1197:         }
1198:         const nextX = horizontalEndpoints[segmentIndex];
1199:         if (nextX !== currentPoint.x) {
1200:           currentPoint = { x: nextX, y: currentPoint.y };
1201:           points.push(currentPoint);
1202:         }
1203:       }
1204: 
1205:       const finalY = verticalEndpoints[verticalEndpoints.length - 1];
1206:       if (finalY !== currentPoint.y) {
1207:         points.push({ x: currentPoint.x, y: finalY });
1208:       }
1209:       return cleanCircuitPath(points);
1210:     }
1211: 
1212:     function buildCircuitPath(from: any, to: any, destinationPlatform: any = null) {
1213:       const platform = destinationPlatform || rowAbove()?.platforms.find(
1214:         (candidate) => candidate.x === to.x,
1215:       );
1216:       const destinationRow = platform ? getRow(platform.row) : rowAbove();
1217: 
1218:       if (!platform || !destinationRow) {
1219:         return cleanCircuitPath([
1220:           { x: from.x, y: from.y },
1221:           { x: from.x, y: to.y - 24 },
1222:           { x: to.x, y: to.y - 24 },
1223:           { x: to.x, y: to.y },
1224:         ]);
1225:       }
1226: 
1227:       const corridors = destinationCorridors(destinationRow);
1228:       const preferred = chooseDestinationCorridor(
1229:         destinationRow,
1230:         platform.x,
1231:         from.x,
1232:       );
1233: 
1234:       const orderedCorridors = [
1235:         preferred,
1236:         ...corridors.filter((corridor) => corridor !== preferred),
1237:       ];
1238: 
1239:       for (const corridor of orderedCorridors) {
1240:         const candidate = buildSteppedRoute(
1241:           from,
1242:           to,
1243:           platform,
1244:           corridor,
1245:         );
1246:         if (pathIsClear(candidate)) {
1247:           return candidate;
1248:         }
1249:       }
1250: 
1251:       const edgeCorridor = {
1252:         left: from.x < width / 2 ? 1 : width - 15,
1253:         right: from.x < width / 2 ? 15 : width - 1,
1254:         center: from.x < width / 2 ? 8 : width - 8,
1255:       };
1256: 
1257:       return buildSteppedRoute(
1258:         from,
1259:         to,
1260:         platform,
1261:         edgeCorridor,
1262:       );
1263:     }
1264: 
1265:     function pathMetrics(points: any[]) {
1266:       const lengths = [];
1267:       let total = 0;
1268:       for (let i = 1; i < points.length; i += 1) {
1269:         const length = Math.abs(points[i].x - points[i - 1].x) + Math.abs(points[i].y - points[i - 1].y);
1270:         lengths.push(length);
1271:         total += length;
1272:       }
1273:       return { lengths, total };
1274:     }
1275: 
1276:     function pointOnPath(currentTravel: any) {
1277:       let distance = currentTravel.distance;
1278:       for (let i = 0; i < currentTravel.lengths.length; i += 1) {
1279:         const length = currentTravel.lengths[i];
1280:         if (distance <= length || i === currentTravel.lengths.length - 1) {
1281:           const a = currentTravel.points[i];
1282:           const b = currentTravel.points[i + 1];
1283:           const amount = length === 0 ? 1 : clamp(distance / length, 0, 1);
1284:           return { x: lerp(a.x, b.x, amount), y: lerp(a.y, b.y, amount), segment: i };
1285:         }
1286:         distance -= length;
1287:       }
1288:       const end = currentTravel.points[currentTravel.points.length - 1];
1289:       return { x: end.x, y: end.y, segment: currentTravel.lengths.length - 1 };
1290:     }
1291: 
1292:     function obstacleRectsNear(y0: number, y1: number) {
1293:       const padding = 6;
1294:       const rects: any[] = [];
1295:       rows.forEach((row) => {
1296:         if (row.y < y0 - CONFIG.rowGap || row.y > y1 + CONFIG.rowGap) return;
1297:         row.platforms.forEach((platform: any) => {
1298:           if (platform.row === 0 && platform.column !== 1) return;
1299:           rects.push({
1300:             left: platform.x - platform.width / 2 - padding,
1301:             right: platform.x + platform.width / 2 + padding,
1302:             top: platform.y - padding,
1303:             bottom: platform.y + platform.height + padding,
1304:           });
1305:         });
1306:       });
1307:       return rects;
1308:     }
1309: 
1310:     function cellBlocked(x: number, y: number, rects: any[]) {
1311:       return rects.some((rect) => x > rect.left && x < rect.right && y > rect.top && y < rect.bottom);
1312:     }
1313: 
1314: 
1315: 
1316:     function updateTimerLine(delta: number) {
1317:       timerSpeed += CONFIG.timerRamp * delta;
1318:       const gap = timerLineY - player.y;
1319:       const hurry = gap > CONFIG.rowGap * CONFIG.timerCatchGapRows ? 2.1 : 1;
1320:       timerLineY -= timerSpeed * hurry * delta;
1321: 
1322:       const closestLine = player.y + (CONFIG.botBaseOffsetRows + 0.65) * CONFIG.rowGap;
1323:       if (timerLineY < closestLine) timerLineY = closestLine;
1324:     }
1325: 
1326:      function updateBot(delta: number) {
1327:       if (!bot || !engineAlive) return;
1328: 
1329:       let legacyCalled = false;
1330:       let v2Called = false;
1331: 
1332:       if (CONFIG.bringUpStage !== 'NORMAL') {
1333:         v2Called = true;
1334:         if ((bot as any).v2IntendedDisplacement) {
1335:           bot.x += (bot as any).v2IntendedDisplacement.x;
1336:           bot.y += (bot as any).v2IntendedDisplacement.y;
1337:           botTrail.push({ x: bot.x, y: bot.y, born: elapsed });
1338:           if (botContextV2) {
1339:             bot.mode = botContextV2.currentState as any;
1340:           }
1341:           (bot as any).v2IntendedDisplacement = null;
1342:           (bot as any).v2Events = null;
1343:         }
1344:       } else if (CONFIG.aiImplementation === 'LEGACY') {
1345:         legacyCalled = true;
1346:         const res = updateBotAI(bot, player, rows, timerLineY, CONFIG, delta, elapsed, width, height);
1347:         bot = res.state;
1348:         botTrail.push({ x: bot.x, y: bot.y, born: elapsed });
1349: 
1350:         res.events.forEach((evt) => {
1351:           if (evt === 'scan') {
1352:             sound.scan();
1353:           } else if (evt === 'lock') {
1354:             echoes.push({ x0: player.x, y0: player.y, x1: bot!.x, y1: bot!.y, born: elapsed });
1355:             sound.lock();
1356:             setMessage('RED SPARK LOCKED — move before it reaches that position.', 'error', 1050);
1357:           } else if (evt === 'recover') {
1358:             sound.danger();
1359:             setMessage('STALL DETECTED — timing spark recovering...', 'neutral', 1200);
1360:           }
1361:         });
1362:       } else if (botContextV2 && (bot as any).v2IntendedDisplacement) {
1363:         v2Called = true;
1364:         // Greenfield V2 - displacement and events were generated at start of update()
1365:         bot.x += (bot as any).v2IntendedDisplacement.x;
1366:         bot.y += (bot as any).v2IntendedDisplacement.y;
1367:         botTrail.push({ x: bot.x, y: bot.y, born: elapsed });
1368: 
1369:         // Update legacy bot state for drawing
1370:         bot.mode = botContextV2.currentState as any;
1371: 
1372:         const events = (bot as any).v2Events || [];
1373:         events.forEach((evt: any) => {
1374:           if (evt.type === 'PLAY_EXCITEMENT_SOUND') {
1375:              sound.scan();
1376:           } else if (evt.type === 'SHOW_ALERT_REACTION') {
1377:              echoes.push({ x0: player.x, y0: player.y, x1: bot!.x, y1: bot!.y, born: elapsed });
1378:              sound.lock();
1379:              setMessage('RED SPARK ALERT!', 'error', 1050);
1380:           } else if (evt.type === 'CAPTURE') {
1381:              endGame('Captured');
1382:           }
1383:         });
1384:         
1385:         // Clear them so they aren't processed twice
1386:         (bot as any).v2IntendedDisplacement = null;
1387:         (bot as any).v2Events = null;
1388:       }
1389: 
1390:       if (legacyCalled && CONFIG.aiImplementation !== 'LEGACY') {
1391:          BotFlightRecorder.getInstance().recordEvent(
1392:            elapsed,
1393:            'GREENFIELD_V2',
1394:            botContextV2?.currentState || 'SEARCH',
1395:            'NONE',
1396:            'LIFECYCLE',
1397:            'LEGACY_UPDATE_CALLED_WHILE_V2_SELECTED',
1398:            `CONFLICT: Legacy update AI tick was executed while Greenfield V2 implementation is active!`,
1399:            { x: bot.x, y: bot.y },
1400:            { x: player.x, y: player.y },
1401:            CONFIG.botRadius,
1402:            CONFIG.playerRadius,
1403:            botContextV2?.awareness?.id || 0,
1404:            obstacleRevision,
1405:            botContextV2?.debug?.plannerStatus || '',
1406:            botContextV2?.pathIndex || 0
1407:          );
1408:       }
1409: 
1410:       if (legacyCalled && v2Called) {
1411:         console.error('DEVELOPMENT ERROR: Both LEGACY_UPDATE_CALLED and GREENFIELD_V2_UPDATE_CALLED occurred during one frame.');
1412:       } else if (!legacyCalled && !v2Called) {
1413:         console.error('DEVELOPMENT ERROR: Neither LEGACY_UPDATE_CALLED nor GREENFIELD_V2_UPDATE_CALLED occurred during one active gameplay frame.');
1414:       }
1415:     }
1416: 
1417:     function selectPlatform(platform: any) {
1418:       if (!engineStarted || !engineAlive || enginePaused || travel || resolveAt || platform.dead) return;
1419:       if (platform.row !== player.row + 1) return;
1420: 
1421:       getAudioContext();
1422:       platform.selected = true;
1423:       const destination = landingPoint(platform);
1424:       const from = { x: player.x, y: player.y };
1425: 
1426:       if (engineMovementMode === 'circuit') {
1427:         const points = buildCircuitPath(from, destination);
1428:         const metrics = pathMetrics(points);
1429:         travel = {
1430:           type: 'circuit',
1431:           platform,
1432:           points,
1433:           lengths: metrics.lengths,
1434:           total: metrics.total,
1435:           distance: 0,
1436:           segment: 0,
1437:           correct: platform.correct,
1438:         };
1439:       } else {
1440:         travel = {
1441:           type: 'hop',
1442:           platform,
1443:           from,
1444:           to: destination,
1445:           duration: CONFIG.hopDuration,
1446:           time: 0,
1447:           correct: platform.correct,
1448:         };
1449:       }
1450: 
1451:       if (platform.correct) {
1452:         playerNumberPresentation.phase = 'clearing';
1453:         playerNumberPresentation.phaseStartedAt = elapsed;
1454:         const nextActiveRow = getRow(platform.row + 1);
1455:         if (nextActiveRow && nextActiveRow.problemSnapshot) {
1456:           playerNumberPresentation.pendingValue = nextActiveRow.problemSnapshot.playerValue;
1457:         } else {
1458:           // Fallback if somehow not prepared
1459:           playerNumberPresentation.pendingValue = 4;
1460:         }
1461:       }
1462: 
1463:       setMessage(`${player.value}) + ${platform.value} = ${player.value + platform.value}`, platform.correct ? 'success' : 'error', 800);
1464:       sound.launch();
1465:     }
1466: 
1467:     function spawnBurst(x: number, y: number, color: string, amount = 24, speed = 0.18) {
1468:       for (let i = 0; i < amount; i += 1) {
1469:         const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
1470:         const velocity = speed * (0.45 + Math.random());
1471:         particles.push({
1472:           x,
1473:           y,
1474:           vx: Math.cos(angle) * velocity,
1475:           vy: Math.sin(angle) * velocity,
1476:           radius: 1.5 + Math.random() * 2.8,
1477:           life: 0,
1478:           maxLife: 350 + Math.random() * 420,
1479:           color,
1480:         });
1481:       }
1482:     }
1483: 
1484:     function arrive(currentTravel: any) {
1485:       const platform = currentTravel.platform;
1486:       const destination = landingPoint(platform);
1487:       player.x = destination.x;
1488:       player.y = destination.y;
1489: 
1490:       if (currentTravel.correct) {
1491:         platform.powered = true;
1492:         platform.litAt = elapsed;
1493:         platform.selected = false;
1494: 
1495:         if (currentTravel.type === 'circuit') {
1496:           traces.push({
1497:             points: currentTravel.points.map((point: any) => ({ ...point })),
1498:             born: elapsed,
1499:           });
1500:         }
1501: 
1502:         player.row += 1;
1503:         player.platform = platform;
1504:         player.pulseAt = elapsed;
1505:         engineBestRow = Math.max(engineBestRow, player.row);
1506:         setBestRow(engineBestRow);
1507:         try {
1508:           window.localStorage.setItem('circuitClimbPrototypeBest', String(engineBestRow));
1509:         } catch {
1510:           // Safe fallback
1511:         }
1512:         
1513:         travel = null;
1514:         resolveAt = 0; // Clear resolve block to allow immediate input
1515: 
1516:         ensureRows();
1517:         const nextActiveRow = getRow(player.row + 1);
1518:         if (nextActiveRow && nextActiveRow.problemSnapshot) {
1519:           player.value = nextActiveRow.problemSnapshot.playerValue;
1520:         } else {
1521:           player.value = 4;
1522:         }
1523: 
1524:         if (playerNumberPresentation.pendingValue !== null) {
1525:           playerNumberPresentation.displayedValue = playerNumberPresentation.pendingValue;
1526:           playerNumberPresentation.pendingValue = null;
1527:         } else {
1528:           playerNumberPresentation.displayedValue = player.value;
1529:         }
1530:         
1531:         // Immediately visible on exact landing frame
1532:         playerNumberPresentation.phase = 'visible';
1533:         playerNumberPresentation.phaseStartedAt = elapsed;
1534: 
1535:         armNextRow();
1536:         updateHud();
1537: 
1538:         spawnBurst(player.x, player.y, COLORS.lime, 30, 0.22);
1539:         sound.correct();
1540:         return;
1541:       }
1542: 
1543:       platform.dead = true;
1544:       platform.selected = false;
1545:       timerLineY -= CONFIG.wrongPenalty;
1546:       if (bot) bot.lastRepath = -1e9;
1547:       spawnBurst(player.x, player.y, COLORS.red, 32, 0.25);
1548:       sound.wrong();
1549:       setMessage('Short circuit. The red timing spark gained ground.', 'error', 1300);
1550: 
1551:       const back = landingPoint(player.platform);
1552:       travel = {
1553:         type: 'return',
1554:         from: { x: destination.x, y: destination.y },
1555:         to: back,
1556:         time: 0,
1557:         duration: CONFIG.returnDuration,
1558:       };
1559:     }
1560: 
1561:     function updateTravel(delta: number) {
1562:       if (!travel) return;
1563: 
1564:       if (travel.type === 'circuit') {
1565:         travel.distance += CONFIG.routeSpeed * delta;
1566:         const point = pointOnPath(travel);
1567:         if (point.segment > travel.segment) {
1568:           travel.segment = point.segment;
1569:           sound.corner();
1570:         }
1571:         player.x = point.x;
1572:         player.y = point.y;
1573:         if (travel.distance >= travel.total) arrive(travel);
1574:         return;
1575:       }
1576: 
1577:       if (travel.type === 'hop') {
1578:         travel.time += delta;
1579:         const amount = clamp(travel.time / travel.duration, 0, 1);
1580:         player.x = lerp(travel.from.x, travel.to.x, amount);
1581:         player.y = lerp(travel.from.y, travel.to.y, amount) - Math.sin(amount * Math.PI) * CONFIG.hopHeight;
1582:         if (amount >= 1) arrive(travel);
1583:         return;
1584:       }
1585: 
1586:       if (travel.type === 'return') {
1587:         travel.time += delta;
1588:         const amount = clamp(travel.time / travel.duration, 0, 1);
1589:         player.x = lerp(travel.from.x, travel.to.x, amount);
1590:         player.y = lerp(travel.from.y, travel.to.y, amount) - Math.sin(amount * Math.PI) * 72;
1591:         if (amount >= 1) {
1592:           player.x = travel.to.x;
1593:           player.y = travel.to.y;
1594:           travel = null;
1595:         }
1596:       }
1597:     }
1598: 
1599:     function updateParticles(delta: number) {
1600:       particles.forEach((particle) => {
1601:         particle.life += delta;
1602:         particle.x += particle.vx * delta;
1603:         particle.y += particle.vy * delta;
1604:         particle.vy += 0.00042 * delta;
1605:       });
1606:       particles = particles.filter((particle) => particle.life < particle.maxLife);
1607:     }
1608: 
1609:     function cullWorld() {
1610:       const bottom = cameraY + height + CONFIG.cullMargin;
1611:       const originalCount = rows.length;
1612:       rows = rows.filter((row) => row.y < bottom || row.index >= player.row - 2);
1613:       if (rows.length !== originalCount) {
1614:         obstacleRevision += 1;
1615:       }
1616:       traces = traces.filter((trace) => Math.min(...trace.points.map((point: any) => point.y)) < bottom);
1617:       botTrail = botTrail.filter((point) => elapsed - point.born < 900 && point.y < bottom);
1618:       echoes = echoes.filter((echo) => elapsed - echo.born < 300);
1619:     }
1620: 
1621:     function getIntendedPlayer(delta: number) {
1622:       if (!travel) return { x: player.x, y: player.y };
1623:       if (travel.type === 'circuit') {
1624:         const nextDistance = travel.distance + CONFIG.routeSpeed * delta;
1625:         if (nextDistance >= travel.total) {
1626:            const dest = landingPoint(travel.platform);
1627:            return { x: dest.x, y: dest.y };
1628:         }
1629:         return pointOnPath({ ...travel, distance: nextDistance });
1630:       }
1631:       if (travel.type === 'hop') {
1632:         const nextTime = travel.time + delta;
1633:         const amount = clamp(nextTime / travel.duration, 0, 1);
1634:         return {
1635:           x: lerp(travel.from.x, travel.to.x, amount),
1636:           y: lerp(travel.from.y, travel.to.y, amount) - Math.sin(amount * Math.PI) * CONFIG.hopHeight
1637:         };
1638:       }
1639:       if (travel.type === 'return') {
1640:         const nextTime = travel.time + delta;
1641:         const amount = clamp(nextTime / travel.duration, 0, 1);
1642:         return {
1643:           x: lerp(travel.from.x, travel.to.x, amount),
1644:           y: lerp(travel.from.y, travel.to.y, amount) - Math.sin(amount * Math.PI) * 72
1645:         };
1646:       }
1647:       return { x: player.x, y: player.y };
1648:     }
1649: 
1650:     function getIntendedBot(delta: number) {
1651:       if (!bot || !engineAlive) return { x: 0, y: 0 };
1652:       
1653:       const isV2 = CONFIG.aiImplementation !== 'LEGACY' || CONFIG.bringUpStage !== 'NORMAL';
1654:       if (isV2) {
1655:         if ((bot as any).v2IntendedDisplacement) {
1656:             return {
1657:                 x: bot.x + (bot as any).v2IntendedDisplacement.x,
1658:                 y: bot.y + (bot as any).v2IntendedDisplacement.y
1659:             };
1660:         }
1661:         return { x: bot.x, y: bot.y };
1662:       }
1663: 
1664:       if ((bot.mode !== 'CHASE' && bot.mode !== 'SEARCH') || !bot.travel) {
1665:         return { x: bot ? bot.x : 0, y: bot ? bot.y : 0 };
1666:       }
1667:       const speed = bot.mode === 'CHASE' ? CONFIG.botLockSpeed : CONFIG.botPatrolSpeed;
1668:       const nextDistance = bot.travel.distance + speed * delta;
1669:       if (nextDistance >= bot.travel.total) {
1670:         return pointOnPath({ ...bot.travel, distance: bot.travel.total });
1671:       }
1672:       return pointOnPath({ ...bot.travel, distance: nextDistance });
1673:     }
1674: 
1675:     function sweptCollision(px0: number, py0: number, px1: number, py1: number, bx0: number, by0: number, bx1: number, by1: number, r: number) {
1676:         const vx = (bx1 - bx0) - (px1 - px0);
1677:         const vy = (by1 - by0) - (py1 - py0);
1678:         const sx = bx0 - px0;
1679:         const sy = by0 - py0;
1680:         
1681:         const a = vx*vx + vy*vy;
1682:         const b = 2 * (sx*vx + sy*vy);
1683:         const c = sx*sx + sy*sy - r*r;
1684:         
1685:         if (c <= 0) return 0; // already intersecting
1686:         if (a === 0) return -1; // no relative movement
1687:         
1688:         const disc = b*b - 4*a*c;
1689:         if (disc < 0) return -1;
1690:         
1691:         const t = (-b - Math.sqrt(disc)) / (2*a);
1692:         if (t >= 0 && t <= 1) return t;
1693:         return -1;
1694:     }
1695: 
1696: 
1697:     function triggerCapture(t: number, px0: number, py0: number, px1: number, py1: number, bx0: number, by0: number, bx1: number, by1: number, delta: number) {
1698:       if (!engineAlive) return;
1699:       
1700:       const r = CONFIG.playerRadius + CONFIG.botRadius;
1701:       console.log('ENEMY_PLAYER_FIRST_TOUCH', {
1702:         playerPrevious: { x: px0, y: py0 },
1703:         playerIntended: { x: px1, y: py1 },
1704:         enemyPrevious: { x: bx0, y: by0 },
1705:         enemyIntended: { x: bx1, y: by1 },
1706:         playerPhysicalRadius: CONFIG.playerRadius,
1707:         enemyPhysicalRadius: CONFIG.botRadius,
1708:         combinedRadius: r,
1709:         startingCenterDistance: Math.hypot(px0 - bx0, py0 - by0),
1710:         intendedEndingCenterDistance: Math.hypot(px1 - bx1, py1 - by1),
1711:         timeOfImpact: t,
1712:         playerState: travel ? travel.type : 'resting',
1713:         enemyState: bot ? bot.mode : 'unknown',
1714:         frameDelta: delta,
1715:         detectionMethod: t === 0 ? 'static' : 'swept',
1716:         movementClamped: t < 1
1717:       });
1718: 
1719:       BotFlightRecorder.getInstance().recordEvent(
1720:         elapsed,
1721:         CONFIG.aiImplementation as 'GREENFIELD_V2' | 'LEGACY',
1722:         botContextV2?.currentState || 'SEARCH',
1723:         'NONE',
1724:         'LIFECYCLE',
1725:         'LIFECYCLE_CAPTURE_TRIGGERED',
1726:         `Lifecycle capture triggered on swept/static collision touch. Time of impact: ${t}. Center distance: ${Math.hypot(px0 - bx0, py0 - by0).toFixed(1)}px.`,
1727:         { x: bx1, y: by1 },
1728:         { x: px1, y: py1 },
1729:         CONFIG.botRadius,
1730:         CONFIG.playerRadius,
1731:         botContextV2?.awareness?.id || 0,
1732:         obstacleRevision,
1733:         botContextV2?.debug?.plannerStatus || '',
1734:         botContextV2?.pathIndex || 0,
1735:         { sweptT: t, preDist: Math.hypot(px0 - bx0, py0 - by0), postDist: Math.hypot(px1 - bx1, py1 - by1) }
1736:       );
1737: 
1738:       spawnBurst(player.x, player.y, COLORS.enemy, 60, 0.35);
1739:       sound.wrong();
1740:       endGame('Red timing spark caught you');
1741:     }
1742: 
1743: 
1744:     function update(delta: number) {
1745:       elapsed += delta;
1746:       if (messageTimer && elapsed >= messageTimer) {
1747:         messageTimer = 0;
1748:         if (!resolveAt) setMessage('Tap the platform that completes the equation.');
1749:       }
1750: 
1751:       if (!firstFrameTraced && bot && engineAlive && engineStarted) {
1752:         firstFrameTraced = true;
1753:         const scanValues = (CONFIG.aiImplementation === 'LEGACY') ? (
1754:           bot.detected ? {
1755:             detected: bot.detected,
1756:             lastDetectedAt: bot.lastDetectedAt
1757:           } : null
1758:         ) : (
1759:           botContextV2?.awareness ? {
1760:             detected: true,
1761:             lastDetectedAt: botContextV2.awareness.lastConfirmedAtMs
1762:           } : null
1763:         );
1764:         const bIntended = getIntendedBot(delta);
1765:         const bDisplacement = { x: bIntended.x - bot.x, y: bIntended.y - bot.y };
1766:         
1767:         console.log('--- PRODUCTION FRAME TRACE (FIRST ACTIVE FRAME) ---', {
1768:           viewport: { width, height },
1769:           player: { world: { x: player.x, y: player.y }, screen: { x: player.x, y: worldToScreenY(player.y) } },
1770:           bot: { world: { x: bot.x, y: bot.y }, screen: { x: bot.x, y: worldToScreenY(bot.y) } },
1771:           cameraScrollY: cameraY,
1772:           deltaTimeMs: delta,
1773:           scanValues,
1774:           rawPhysicsDisplacement: bDisplacement
1775:         });
1776:       }
1777: 
1778:       if (bot && engineAlive) {
1779:         if (CONFIG.aiImplementation !== 'LEGACY' && botContextV2) {
1780:           const platforms: any[] = [];
1781:           const viewTop = cameraY - height;
1782:           const viewBottom = cameraY + height * 2;
1783:           rows.forEach(r => {
1784:             if (r.y > viewTop && r.y < viewBottom) {
1785:                r.platforms.forEach((p: any) => {
1786:                  platforms.push({
1787:                    id: p.id || (r.y + '-' + p.x),
1788:                    rect: {
1789:                      left: p.x - CONFIG.platformWidth / 2,
1790:                      right: p.x + CONFIG.platformWidth / 2,
1791:                      top: r.y - CONFIG.platformHeight / 2,
1792:                      bottom: r.y + CONFIG.platformHeight / 2,
1793:                    }
1794:                  });
1795:                });
1796:             }
1797:           });
1798: 
1799:           let playerMovementState: 'SETTLED' | 'MOVE_STARTED' | 'IN_TRANSIT' | 'LANDING' | 'WRONG_RETURN' | 'CAPTURED' = 'SETTLED';
1800:           if (!engineAlive) {
1801:             playerMovementState = 'CAPTURED';
1802:           } else if (!travel) {
1803:             playerMovementState = 'SETTLED';
1804:           } else if (travel.type === 'return') {
1805:             playerMovementState = 'WRONG_RETURN';
1806:           } else {
1807:             const progress = travel.type === 'circuit'
1808:               ? (travel.total > 0 ? travel.distance / travel.total : 0)
1809:               : (travel.duration > 0 ? travel.time / travel.duration : 0);
1810:             if (progress === 0) {
1811:               playerMovementState = 'MOVE_STARTED';
1812:             } else if (progress >= 1) {
1813:               playerMovementState = 'LANDING';
1814:             } else {
1815:               playerMovementState = 'IN_TRANSIT';
1816:             }
1817:           }
1818: 
1819:           const playerSettledPlatformId = !travel && player.platform ? (player.platform.id || `${player.platform.row}-${player.platform.column}`) : null;
1820:           const playerDestinationPlatformId = travel && travel.platform ? (travel.platform.id || `${travel.platform.row}-${travel.platform.column}`) : null;
1821:           
1822:           let playerRoutePolyline: any[] | null = null;
1823:           let playerRouteStartPosition: any | null = null;
1824:           let playerRouteDestination: any | null = null;
1825:           let playerRouteProgress = 0;
1826:           let playerEstimatedRemainingTransitTimeMs = 0;
1827: 
1828:           if (travel) {
1829:             if (travel.type === 'circuit') {
1830:               playerRoutePolyline = travel.points;
1831:               playerRouteStartPosition = travel.points[0];
1832:               playerRouteDestination = travel.points[travel.points.length - 1];
1833:               playerRouteProgress = travel.total > 0 ? Math.min(1, Math.max(0, travel.distance / travel.total)) : 0;
1834:               playerEstimatedRemainingTransitTimeMs = travel.total > 0 ? Math.max(0, (travel.total - travel.distance) / CONFIG.routeSpeed * 16.666) : 0;
1835:             } else {
1836:               playerRoutePolyline = [travel.from, travel.to];
1837:               playerRouteStartPosition = travel.from;
1838:               playerRouteDestination = travel.to;
1839:               playerRouteProgress = travel.duration > 0 ? Math.min(1, Math.max(0, travel.time / travel.duration)) : 0;
1840:               playerEstimatedRemainingTransitTimeMs = Math.max(0, travel.duration - travel.time);
1841:             }
1842:           }
1843: 
1844:           // Record player movement state events
1845:           if (playerMovementState !== (bot as any).lastPlayerStateRecorded) {
1846:             const prevState = (bot as any).lastPlayerStateRecorded || 'SETTLED';
1847:             (bot as any).lastPlayerStateRecorded = playerMovementState;
1848:             
1849:             let eventName = '';
1850:             let triggerReason = '';
1851:             
1852:             if (playerMovementState === 'MOVE_STARTED') {
1853:               BotFlightRecorder.getInstance().recordEvent(
1854:                 elapsed,
1855:                 CONFIG.aiImplementation as 'GREENFIELD_V2' | 'LEGACY',
1856:                 botContextV2?.currentState || 'SEARCH',
1857:                 'NONE',
1858:                 'MOVEMENT',
1859:                 'PLAYER_MOVE_STARTED',
1860:                 `Player started moving. From platform: ${playerSettledPlatformId}, Destination: ${playerDestinationPlatformId}`,
1861:                 { x: bot.x, y: bot.y },
1862:                 { x: player.x, y: player.y },
1863:                 CONFIG.botRadius,
1864:                 CONFIG.playerRadius,
1865:                 botContextV2?.awareness?.id || 0,
1866:                 obstacleRevision,
1867:                 botContextV2?.debug?.plannerStatus || '',
1868:                 botContextV2?.pathIndex || 0
1869:               );
1870:               
1871:               BotFlightRecorder.getInstance().recordEvent(
1872:                 elapsed,
1873:                 CONFIG.aiImplementation as 'GREENFIELD_V2' | 'LEGACY',
1874:                 botContextV2?.currentState || 'SEARCH',
1875:                 'NONE',
1876:                 'MOVEMENT',
1877:                 'PLAYER_DESTINATION_IDENTIFIED',
1878:                 `Player destination identified: ${playerDestinationPlatformId}`,
1879:                 { x: bot.x, y: bot.y },
1880:                 { x: player.x, y: player.y },
1881:                 CONFIG.botRadius,
1882:                 CONFIG.playerRadius,
1883:                 botContextV2?.awareness?.id || 0,
1884:                 obstacleRevision,
1885:                 botContextV2?.debug?.plannerStatus || '',
1886:                 botContextV2?.pathIndex || 0,
1887:                 { destinationId: playerDestinationPlatformId }
1888:               );
1889:             } else if (playerMovementState === 'IN_TRANSIT') {
1890:               eventName = 'PLAYER_IN_TRANSIT';
1891:               triggerReason = `Player is in transit towards ${playerDestinationPlatformId}`;
1892:             } else if (playerMovementState === 'LANDING') {
1893:               eventName = 'PLAYER_LANDED';
1894:               triggerReason = `Player is landing on platform ${playerDestinationPlatformId}`;
1895:             } else if (playerMovementState === 'SETTLED') {
1896:               eventName = 'PLAYER_LANDED';
1897:               triggerReason = `Player settled on platform ${playerSettledPlatformId}`;
1898:             }
1899: 
1900:             if (eventName) {
1901:               BotFlightRecorder.getInstance().recordEvent(
1902:                 elapsed,
1903:                 CONFIG.aiImplementation as 'GREENFIELD_V2' | 'LEGACY',
1904:                 botContextV2?.currentState || 'SEARCH',
1905:                 'NONE',
1906:                 'MOVEMENT',
1907:                 eventName,
1908:                 triggerReason,
1909:                 { x: bot.x, y: bot.y },
1910:                 { x: player.x, y: player.y },
1911:                 CONFIG.botRadius,
1912:                 CONFIG.playerRadius,
1913:                 botContextV2?.awareness?.id || 0,
1914:                 obstacleRevision,
1915:                 botContextV2?.debug?.plannerStatus || '',
1916:                 botContextV2?.pathIndex || 0,
1917:                 { destination: playerDestinationPlatformId, settled: playerSettledPlatformId }
1918:               );
1919:             }
1920:           }
1921: 
1922:           const snapshot = {
1923:             simTimeMs: elapsed,
1924:             deltaMs: delta,
1925:             playerPosition: { x: player.x, y: player.y },
1926:             playerRadius: CONFIG.playerRadius,
1927:             playerRowId: player.row,
1928:             playerSupportingPlatformId: player.platform ? (player.platform.id || `${player.platform.row}-${player.platform.column}`) : null,
1929:             botPosition: { x: bot.x, y: bot.y },
1930:             botRadius: CONFIG.botRadius,
1931:             platforms,
1932:             navigationBounds: { left: 0, right: width, top: player.y - height * 2, bottom: player.y + height * 2 },
1933:             obstacleRevision: obstacleRevision,
1934:             paused: enginePaused,
1935:             gameOver: !engineAlive,
1936:             difficulty: CONFIG.difficulty as 'EASY' | 'NORMAL' | 'HARD',
1937:             rowGap: CONFIG.rowGap,
1938:             botBaseOffsetRows: CONFIG.botBaseOffsetRows,
1939:             playerMovementState,
1940:             playerSettledPlatformId,
1941:             playerDestinationPlatformId,
1942:             playerRoutePolyline,
1943:             playerRouteStartPosition,
1944:             playerRouteDestination,
1945:             playerRouteProgress,
1946:             playerEstimatedRemainingTransitTimeMs
1947:           };
1948: 
1949:           const runV2Update = CONFIG.bringUpStage !== 'STAGE_A' && CONFIG.bringUpStage !== 'STAGE_B';
1950: 
1951:           if (runV2Update) {
1952:             const res = updateBotV2(snapshot, botContextV2);
1953:             if (CONFIG.bringUpStage === 'STAGE_C' || CONFIG.bringUpStage === 'STAGE_D') {
1954:               (bot as any).v2IntendedDisplacement = { x: 0, y: 0 };
1955:             } else {
1956:               (bot as any).v2IntendedDisplacement = res.intendedDisplacement;
1957:             }
1958:             (bot as any).v2Events = res.events;
1959:           } else if (CONFIG.bringUpStage === 'STAGE_A') {
1960:             bot.x = width / 2;
1961:             bot.y = player.y + 140;
1962:             (bot as any).v2IntendedDisplacement = { x: 0, y: 0 };
1963:             (bot as any).v2Events = [];
1964:           } else if (CONFIG.bringUpStage === 'STAGE_B') {
1965:             if (typeof (bot as any).diagDirection === 'undefined') {
1966:               (bot as any).diagDirection = 1;
1967:             }
1968:             bot.y = player.y + 140;
1969:             const speedPxPerMs = 0.12;
1970:             let dx = (bot as any).diagDirection * speedPxPerMs * delta;
1971:             const nextX = bot.x + dx;
1972:             if (nextX > width * 0.8) {
1973:               (bot as any).diagDirection = -1;
1974:               dx = (width * 0.8) - bot.x;
1975:             } else if (nextX < width * 0.2) {
1976:               (bot as any).diagDirection = 1;
1977:               dx = (width * 0.2) - bot.x;
1978:             }
1979:             (bot as any).v2IntendedDisplacement = { x: dx, y: 0 };
1980:             (bot as any).v2Events = [];
1981:           }
1982:         }
1983: 
1984:         // Pre-check for existing static contact or sweeping contact
1985:         const r = CONFIG.playerRadius + CONFIG.botRadius;
1986:         
1987:         const pIntended = getIntendedPlayer(delta);
1988:         const bIntended = getIntendedBot(delta);
1989:         
1990:         const t = sweptCollision(player.x, player.y, pIntended.x, pIntended.y, bot.x, bot.y, bIntended.x, bIntended.y, r);
1991:         
1992:         if (t >= 0 && t <= 1) {
1993:           // Collision happened at fraction t of the frame.
1994:           // Advance simulation only up to time of impact.
1995:           const impactDelta = delta * t;
1996:           updateTravel(impactDelta);
1997:           updateBot(impactDelta);
1998:           triggerCapture(t, player.x, player.y, pIntended.x, pIntended.y, bot.x, bot.y, bIntended.x, bIntended.y, delta);
1999:           return;
2000:         }
2001:       }
2002: 
2003:       if (playerNumberPresentation.phase === 'clearing') {
2004:         if (elapsed - playerNumberPresentation.phaseStartedAt >= 110) {
2005:           playerNumberPresentation.phase = 'hidden-transit';
2006:           playerNumberPresentation.phaseStartedAt += 110;
2007:         }
2008:       }
2009: 
2010:       const targetAge = elapsed - targetPresentation.phaseStartedAt;
2011:       if (targetPresentation.phase === 'dominant-enter' && targetAge >= 180) {
2012:         targetPresentation.phase = 'dominant-hold';
2013:       } else if (targetPresentation.phase === 'dominant-hold' && targetAge >= 650) {
2014:         targetPresentation.phase = 'receding';
2015:       } else if (targetPresentation.phase === 'receding' && targetAge >= 1500) {
2016:         targetPresentation.phase = 'resting';
2017:       }
2018:       targetPresentation.progress = clamp(targetAge / 1500, 0, 1);
2019: 
2020:       updateTravel(delta);
2021:       updateTimerLine(delta);
2022:       updateBot(delta);
2023:       updateParticles(delta);
2024: 
2025:       const desiredCamera = player.y - height * CONFIG.cameraAnchor;
2026:       cameraY += (desiredCamera - cameraY) * (1 - Math.pow(0.0008, delta / 1000));
2027: 
2028:       cullWorld();
2029:       updateHud();
2030:     }
2031: 
2032:     function worldToScreenY(worldY: number) {
2033:       return worldY - cameraY;
2034:     }
2035: 
2036:     function parallaxOffset(factor: number, period: number) {
2037:       return ((-cameraY * factor) % period + period) % period;
2038:     }
2039: 
2040:     function drawFarParallax() {
2041:       const offset = parallaxOffset(CONFIG.farParallax, 260);
2042:       ctx.save();
2043:       ctx.globalAlpha = 0.18;
2044:       ctx.strokeStyle = COLORS.bgDepth;
2045:       ctx.lineWidth = 2;
2046:       for (let i = -1; i < 6; i += 1) {
2047:         const y = i * 260 + offset;
2048:         ctx.beginPath();
2049:         ctx.arc(width * 0.18, y + 70, 78, 0, Math.PI * 2);
2050:         ctx.stroke();
2051:         ctx.beginPath();
2052:         ctx.arc(width * 0.82, y + 160, 112, 0, Math.PI * 2);
2053:         ctx.stroke();
2054:       }
2055:       ctx.restore();
2056:     }
2057: 
2058: 
2059:     function roundRect(x, y, w, h, r) {
2060:       ctx.beginPath();
2061:       ctx.moveTo(x + r, y);
2062:       ctx.lineTo(x + w - r, y);
2063:       ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + r);
2064:       ctx.lineTo(x + w, y + h - r);
2065:       ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x + w, y + h, x + w - r, y + h);
2066:       ctx.lineTo(x + r, y + h);
2067:       ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x, y + h, x, y + h - r);
2068:       ctx.lineTo(x, y + r);
2069:       ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x, y, x + r, y);
2070:       ctx.closePath();
2071:     }
2072: 
2073:     function drawTargetPresentation() {
2074:       const p = targetPresentation;
2075:       const targetAge = elapsed - p.phaseStartedAt;
2076:       let alpha = 0.22;
2077:       let sizeScale = 1;
2078:       let yOffset = 0;
2079:       
2080:       const restSize = Math.min(width * 0.62, height * 0.31);
2081:       const domSize = Math.min(width * 0.75, height * 0.45);
2082:       let size = restSize;
2083:       
2084:       let flash = 0;
2085:       let haloAlpha = 0;
2086:       
2087:       if (p.phase === 'dominant-enter') {
2088:         const pr = clamp(targetAge / 180, 0, 1);
2089:         alpha = 0.22 + pr * 0.68; // up to ~0.9
2090:         size = domSize;
2091:         flash = pr;
2092:         yOffset = (1 - pr) * 20; // slight drop in
2093:         haloAlpha = pr * 0.6;
2094:       } else if (p.phase === 'dominant-hold') {
2095:         alpha = 0.9;
2096:         size = domSize;
2097:         flash = 1;
2098:         haloAlpha = 0.6;
2099:       } else if (p.phase === 'receding') {
2100:         const pr = clamp((targetAge - 650) / 850, 0, 1);
2101:         const ease = pr < 0.5 ? 2 * pr * pr : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * pr + 2, 2) / 2;
2102:         alpha = 0.9 - ease * (0.9 - 0.22);
2103:         size = domSize - ease * (domSize - restSize);
2104:         flash = 1 - ease;
2105:         haloAlpha = 0.6 - ease * 0.6;
2106:       } else {
2107:         alpha = 0.22;
2108:         size = restSize;
2109:         flash = 0;
2110:         haloAlpha = 0;
2111:       }
2112: 
2113:       const prefersReducedMotion = window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
2114:       if (prefersReducedMotion) {
2115:         yOffset = 0;
2116:         size = restSize + (size - restSize) * 0.3; // Less scale
2117:       }
2118:       const drift = prefersReducedMotion ? 0 : parallaxOffset(CONFIG.farParallax, 420) - 210;
2119:       const baseY = height * 0.42 + drift * 0.10 + yOffset;
2120:       
2121:       ctx.save();
2122:       ctx.textAlign = 'center';
2123:       ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
2124:       ctx.font = `900 ${size}px ui-monospace, monospace`;
2125:       ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
2126:       ctx.fillStyle = flash > 0.25 ? COLORS.text : '#bacde6';
2127:       ctx.shadowColor = flash > 0.25 ? COLORS.targetGlow : COLORS.gridDot;
2128:       ctx.shadowBlur = 12 + flash * 46;
2129:       ctx.fillText(String(p.targetValue), width / 2, baseY);
2130:       ctx.restore();
2131: 
2132:       // SUM TO cue
2133:       if (showSumToCue) {
2134:         let cueAlpha = alpha * 1.5;
2135:         if (cueAlpha > 1) cueAlpha = 1;
2136:         
2137:         const floatDrift = prefersReducedMotion ? 0 : Math.sin(elapsed / 800) * 4;
2138:         const cueY = baseY - size * 0.55 - 30 + floatDrift;
2139:         
2140:         ctx.save();
2141:         ctx.globalAlpha = cueAlpha;
2142:         
2143:         // Background chip
2144:         const chipW = 90;
2145:         const chipH = 28;
2146:         const chipX = width / 2 - chipW / 2;
2147:         const chipY = cueY - chipH / 2;
2148:         
2149:         ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.15)';
2150:         ctx.shadowBlur = 8;
2151:         ctx.shadowOffsetY = 4;
2152:         
2153:         ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.cueSurface;
2154:         roundRect(chipX, chipY, chipW, chipH, 14);
2155:         ctx.fill();
2156:         
2157:         ctx.shadowColor = 'transparent';
2158:         ctx.strokeStyle = COLORS.cueBorder;
2159:         ctx.lineWidth = 1;
2160:         ctx.stroke();
2161: 
2162:         ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.text;
2163:         ctx.font = '900 12px ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif';
2164:         ctx.textAlign = 'center';
2165:         ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
2166:         ctx.letterSpacing = '1px';
2167:         ctx.fillText('SUM TO', width / 2, cueY + 1);
2168:         ctx.restore();
2169:       }
2170:     }
2171: 
2172: 
2173:     function drawMidParallax() {
2174:       const grid = 30;
2175:       const offsetY = parallaxOffset(CONFIG.midParallax, grid);
2176:       ctx.strokeStyle = COLORS.gridLine;
2177:       ctx.lineWidth = 1;
2178:       ctx.beginPath();
2179:       for (let x = 0; x <= width; x += grid) {
2180:         ctx.moveTo(x + 0.5, 0);
2181:         ctx.lineTo(x + 0.5, height);
2182:       }
2183:       for (let y = offsetY; y <= height; y += grid) {
2184:         ctx.moveTo(0, y + 0.5);
2185:         ctx.lineTo(width, y + 0.5);
2186:       }
2187:       ctx.stroke();
2188: 
2189:       ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.gridDot;
2190:       for (let x = 15; x < width; x += grid) {
2191:         for (let y = offsetY + 15; y < height; y += grid) {
2192:           ctx.globalAlpha = 0.24 + 0.10 * Math.sin((x + y + elapsed * 0.02) * 0.04);
2193:           ctx.beginPath();
2194:           ctx.arc(x, y, 1.15, 0, Math.PI * 2);
2195:           ctx.fill();
2196:         }
2197:       }
2198:       ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
2199:     }
2200:

2202:       const offset = parallaxOffset(CONFIG.foregroundParallax, 190);

2231:     function drawTrace(points: any[], alpha = 1, color = '#007BFF') {
2232:       if (!points || points.length < 2) return;
2233:       ctx.save();
2234:       ctx.translate(0, -cameraY);
2235:       ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
2236:       ctx.lineCap = 'round';
2237: 
2238:       ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
2239:       
2240:       // Outline
2241:       ctx.strokeStyle = '#0E1B33';
2242:       ctx.lineWidth = 6;
2243:       ctx.beginPath();
2244:       ctx.moveTo(points[0].x, points[0].y);
2245:       points.slice(1).forEach((point) => ctx.lineTo(point.x, point.y));
2246:       ctx.stroke();
2247: 
2248:       // Inner fill
2249:       ctx.strokeStyle = color;
2250:       ctx.lineWidth = 4;
2251:       ctx.beginPath();
2252:       ctx.moveTo(points[0].x, points[0].y);
2253:       points.slice(1).forEach((point) => ctx.lineTo(point.x, point.y));
2254:       ctx.stroke();
2255: 
2256:       ctx.restore();
2257:     }

2259:     function drawTraces() {
2260:       traces.forEach((trace) => {
2261:         const age = elapsed - trace.born;
2262:         const alpha = clamp(age / 240, 0.32, 0.88);
2263:         drawTrace(trace.points, alpha, '#007BFF');
2264:       });
2265: 
2266:       if (travel && travel.type === 'circuit') {
2267:         const current = pointOnPath(travel);
2268:         const partial = [];
2269:         let remaining = travel.distance;
2270:         partial.push({ ...travel.points[0] });
2271:         for (let i = 0; i < travel.lengths.length; i += 1) {
2272:           const length = travel.lengths[i];
2273:           if (remaining >= length) {
2274:             partial.push({ ...travel.points[i + 1] });
2275:             remaining -= length;
2276:           } else {
2277:             partial.push({ x: current.x, y: current.y });
2278:             break;
2279:           }
2280:         }
2281:         drawTrace(partial, 1, '#007BFF');
2282:       }
2283:     }

2285:     function drawPlatform(platform: any, activeRow: number) {
2286:       if (platform.row === 0 && platform.column !== 1) {
2287:         return;
2288:       }
2289:       const x = platform.x - platform.width / 2;
2290:       const y = worldToScreenY(platform.y);
2291: 
2292:       if (y < -110 || y > height + 110) {
2293:         return;
2294:       }
2295: 
2296:       const active = platform.row === activeRow;
2297:       const bob = active && !travel && !platform.dead
2298:         ? Math.sin(elapsed * 0.003 + platform.column) * 1.5
2299:         : 0;
2300: 
2301:       const drawY = y + bob;
2302:       const cornerRadius = 4;
2303: 
2304:       ctx.save();
2305:       
2306:       let fill = '#ffffff';
2307:       let border = '#D8E4F7';
2308:       let bottomBar = '#D8E4F7';
2309:       let textColor = '#0E1B33';
2310:       let shadowColor = 'rgba(14, 27, 51, 0.04)';
2311:       
2312:       if (platform.dead) {
2313:         fill = '#f1f5f9';
2314:         border = '#cbd5e1';
2315:         bottomBar = '#cbd5e1';
2316:         textColor = '#94a3b8';
2317:         shadowColor = 'transparent';
2318:       } else if (platform.powered || platform.selected) {
2319:         fill = '#ffffff';
2320:         border = '#007BFF';
2321:         bottomBar = '#007BFF';
2322:         textColor = '#007BFF';
2323:         shadowColor = 'rgba(0, 123, 255, 0.1)';
2324:       } else if (active) {
2325:         fill = '#ffffff';
2326:         border = '#D8E4F7';
2327:         bottomBar = '#007BFF';
2328:         textColor = '#0E1B33';
2329:         shadowColor = 'rgba(14, 27, 51, 0.08)';
2330:       }
2331: 
2332:       ctx.shadowColor = shadowColor;
2333:       ctx.shadowBlur = platform.dead ? 0 : 8;
2334:       ctx.shadowOffsetY = platform.dead ? 0 : 4;
2335: 
2336:       // Draw Main Box
2337:       roundedRectPath(ctx, x, drawY, platform.width, platform.height, cornerRadius);
2338:       ctx.fillStyle = fill;
2339:       ctx.fill();
2340:       
2341:       ctx.lineWidth = 2;
2342:       ctx.strokeStyle = border;
2343:       ctx.stroke();
2344: 
2345:       // Draw Bottom Bar
2346:       ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
2347:       ctx.shadowOffsetY = 0;
2348:       ctx.fillStyle = bottomBar;
2349:       
2350:       ctx.beginPath();
2351:       ctx.moveTo(x + cornerRadius, drawY + platform.height - 4);
2352:       ctx.lineTo(x + platform.width - cornerRadius, drawY + platform.height - 4);
2353:       ctx.lineTo(x + platform.width, drawY + platform.height - cornerRadius);
2354:       ctx.lineTo(x + platform.width, drawY + platform.height - cornerRadius);
2355:       ctx.arcTo(x + platform.width, drawY + platform.height, x + platform.width - cornerRadius, drawY + platform.height, cornerRadius);
2356:       ctx.lineTo(x + cornerRadius, drawY + platform.height);
2357:       ctx.arcTo(x, drawY + platform.height, x, drawY + platform.height - cornerRadius, cornerRadius);
2358:       ctx.lineTo(x, drawY + platform.height - 4);
2359:       ctx.fill();
2360: 
2361:       // Draw Text
2362:       if (platform.value !== null && platform.row > 0) {
2363:         if (platform.dead) {
2364:           ctx.strokeStyle = '#ef4444';
2365:           ctx.lineWidth = 1.6;
2366:           ctx.beginPath();
2367:           ctx.moveTo(platform.x - 18, drawY + 13);
2368:           ctx.lineTo(platform.x - 4, drawY + 26);
2369:           ctx.lineTo(platform.x - 12, drawY + platform.height - 11);
2370:           ctx.moveTo(platform.x + 16, drawY + 12);
2371:           ctx.lineTo(platform.x + 3, drawY + 25);
2372:           ctx.lineTo(platform.x + 14, drawY + platform.height - 11);
2373:           ctx.stroke();
2374:         }
2375:         ctx.textAlign = 'center';
2376:         ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
2377:         ctx.font = `700 ${Math.max(22, Math.min(29, platform.width * 0.26))}px ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif`;
2378:         ctx.fillStyle = textColor;
2379:         ctx.fillText(String(platform.value), platform.x, drawY + platform.height / 2);
2380:       }
2381: 
2382:       ctx.restore();
2383:     }

2385:     function drawPlatforms() {
2386:       const activeRow = player.row + 1;

2390:     function drawBotTrail() {
2391:       botTrail.forEach((point) => {
2392:         const age = elapsed - point.born;
2393:         const alpha = clamp(1 - age / 760, 0, 1);
2394:         if (alpha <= 0) return;
2395:         ctx.globalAlpha = alpha * 0.72;
2396:         ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.enemy;
2397:         ctx.beginPath();

2399:         ctx.fill();
2400:       });
2401:       ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
2402:     }

2404:     function drawBotScanRing() {
2405:       if (!bot) return;
2406:       const scanTimeInCycle = bot.scanTime % CONFIG.scanPeriodMs;
2407:       if (scanTimeInCycle >= CONFIG.scanDurationMs) return;
2408:       const progress = scanTimeInCycle / CONFIG.scanDurationMs;
2409:       const radius = progress * CONFIG.scanMaxRadius;
2410:       ctx.save();
2411:       ctx.strokeStyle = COLORS.red;
2412:       ctx.globalAlpha = (1 - progress) * 0.48;
2413:       ctx.lineWidth = 2.2;
2414:       ctx.shadowColor = COLORS.red;
2415:       ctx.shadowBlur = 10;
2416:       ctx.beginPath();
2417:       ctx.arc(bot.x, worldToScreenY(bot.y), radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
2418:       ctx.stroke();
2419:       ctx.restore();
2420:     }

2422:     function drawBotEchoes() {
2423:       echoes.forEach((echo) => {
2424:         const progress = clamp((elapsed - echo.born) / 250, 0, 1);
2425:         const x = lerp(echo.x0, echo.x1, progress);
2426:         const y = lerp(echo.y0, echo.y1, progress);
2427:         ctx.globalAlpha = 1 - progress;
2428:         ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.red;
2429:         ctx.beginPath();
2430:         ctx.arc(x, worldToScreenY(y), 5 - progress * 1.8, 0, Math.PI * 2);
2431:         ctx.fill();
2432:       });
2433:       ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
2434:     }

2436:     function drawBot() {

2458:          const scanTimeInCycle = bot.scanTime % CONFIG.scanPeriodMs;

2460:            const progress = scanTimeInCycle / CONFIG.scanDurationMs;
2461:            const radius = progress * CONFIG.scanMaxRadius;
2462:            const thickness = CONFIG.radarWaveThicknessPx !== undefined ? CONFIG.radarWaveThicknessPx : 16;
2463:            ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.35)';

2467:            ctx.stroke();

2471:          ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.55)';
