2713:         } else if (bursting) {
2714:            outer += 8 * (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(elapsed * 0.02 + i));
2715:         }
2716:         ctx.beginPath();
2717:         ctx.moveTo(x + Math.cos(angle) * inner, y + Math.sin(angle) * inner);
2718:         ctx.lineTo(x + Math.cos(angle) * outer, y + Math.sin(angle) * outer);
2719:         ctx.stroke();
2720:       }
2721: 
2722:       if (locked) {
2723:         ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
2724:         ctx.strokeStyle = '#ff3830';
2725:         ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
2726:         ctx.setLineDash([4, 5]);
2727:         ctx.beginPath();
2728:         ctx.arc(x, y, radius + 11, 0, Math.PI * 2);
2729:         ctx.stroke();
2730:         ctx.setLineDash([]);
2731:         ctx.fillStyle = '#ff3830';
2732:         ctx.font = '900 9px ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif';
2733:         ctx.textAlign = 'center';
2734:         ctx.textBaseline = 'bottom';
2735:         ctx.fillText('CHASE', x, y - radius - 15);
2736:       } else if (bursting) {
2737:         ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
2738:         ctx.strokeStyle = '#ff3830';
2739:         ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
2740:         ctx.beginPath();
2741:         ctx.arc(x, y, radius + 14 + Math.sin(elapsed * 0.03) * 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
2742:         ctx.stroke();
2743:         ctx.fillStyle = '#ff3830';
2744:         ctx.font = '900 9px ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif';
2745:         ctx.textAlign = 'center';
2746:         ctx.textBaseline = 'bottom';
2747:         ctx.fillText('LOCK', x, y - radius - 15);
2748:       }
2749: 
2750:       if (bot.contactTime > 0) {
2751:         const progress = clamp(bot.contactTime / CONFIG.contactFuseMs, 0, 1);
2752:         ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
2753:         ctx.strokeStyle = COLORS.white;
2754:         ctx.lineWidth = 3;
2755:         ctx.beginPath();
2756:         ctx.arc(x, y, radius + 6, -Math.PI / 2, -Math.PI / 2 + progress * Math.PI * 2);
2757:         ctx.stroke();
2758:       }
2759: 
2760:       ctx.restore();
2761:     }
2762: 
2763:     function drawPlayer() {
2764:       // draw hitbox if enabled
2765:       if (showCollisionHitboxes) {
2766:          ctx.save();
2767:          ctx.strokeStyle = '#00FF00';
2768:          ctx.lineWidth = 2;
2769:          ctx.beginPath();
2770:          ctx.arc(player.x, worldToScreenY(player.y), CONFIG.playerRadius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
2771:          ctx.stroke();
2772:          ctx.restore();
2773:       }
2774: 
2775:       const screenY = worldToScreenY(player.y);
2776:       const pulseAge = elapsed - player.pulseAt;
2777:       const pulse = pulseAge >= 0 && pulseAge < 700 ? Math.sin((pulseAge / 700) * Math.PI) : 0;
2778:       const radius = CONFIG.playerRadius + pulse * 5;
2779: 
2780:       ctx.save();
2781:       ctx.translate(player.x, screenY);
2782: 
2783:       if (engineMovementMode === 'hop') {
2784:         ctx.shadowColor = COLORS.lime;
2785:         ctx.shadowBlur = 24;
2786:         ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.bgDepth;
2787:         ctx.strokeStyle = COLORS.lime;
2788:         ctx.lineWidth = 2.4;
2789:         roundedRectPath(ctx, -radius, -radius * 0.84, radius * 2, radius * 1.68, radius * 0.68);
2790:         ctx.fill();
2791:         ctx.stroke();
2792:       } else {
2793:         ctx.shadowColor = '#00e5ff';
2794:         ctx.shadowBlur = 24;
2795:         ctx.fillStyle = '#007bff';
2796:         ctx.strokeStyle = '#00e5ff';
2797:         ctx.lineWidth = 3;
2798:         ctx.beginPath();
2799:         ctx.arc(0, 0, radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
2800:         ctx.fill();
2801:         ctx.stroke();
2802:       }
2803: 
2804:       ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
2805:       ctx.textAlign = 'center';
2806:       ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
2807:       ctx.font = '1000 24px ui-monospace, monospace';
2808:       ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.white;
2809: 
2810:       if (playerNumberPresentation.phase !== 'hidden-transit' && playerNumberPresentation.displayedValue !== null) {
2811:         let opacity = 1;
2812:         let scale = 1;
2813:         const phaseElapsed = elapsed - playerNumberPresentation.phaseStartedAt;
2814: 
2815:         if (playerNumberPresentation.phase === 'clearing') {
2816:           const progress = clamp(phaseElapsed / 110, 0, 1);
2817:           opacity = 1 - progress;
2818:           scale = 1 - progress * 0.12;
2819:         }
2820: 
2821:         if (opacity > 0) {
2822:           ctx.save();
2823:           ctx.globalAlpha = opacity;
2824:           if (scale !== 1) {
2825:             ctx.scale(scale, scale);
2826:           }
2827:           ctx.fillText(String(playerNumberPresentation.displayedValue), 0, engineMovementMode === 'hop' ? 2 : 1);
2828:           ctx.restore();
2829:         }
2830:       }
2831: 
2832:       ctx.restore();
2833:     }
2834: 
2835:     function drawParticles() {
2836:       particles.forEach((particle) => {
2837:         const alpha = 1 - particle.life / particle.maxLife;
2838:         ctx.save();
2839:         ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
2840:         ctx.fillStyle = particle.color;
2841:         ctx.shadowColor = particle.color;
2842:         ctx.shadowBlur = 10;
2843:         ctx.beginPath();
2844:         ctx.arc(particle.x, worldToScreenY(particle.y), particle.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
2845:         ctx.fill();
2846:         ctx.restore();
2847:       });
2848:     }
2849: 
2850:     function drawNextRowIndicator() {
2851:       const row = rowAbove();
2852:       if (!row) return;
2853:       const y = worldToScreenY(row.y) - 23;
2854:       if (y < 105 || y > height - 80) return;
2855:       ctx.save();
2856:       ctx.textAlign = 'center';
2857:       ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
2858:       ctx.font = '800 9px ui-monospace, monospace';
2859:       ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.structure;
2860:       ctx.fillText(`TARGET ${row.targetValue}`, width / 2, y);
2861:       ctx.restore();
2862:     }
2863: 
2864:     function render() {
2865:       drawBackground();
2866:       drawBotTrail();
2867:       drawTraces();
2868:       drawPlatforms();
2869:       drawNextRowIndicator();
2870:       drawBotScanRing();
2871:       drawBotEchoes();
2872:       drawParticles();
2873:       drawBot();
2874:       drawPlayer();
2875:       drawForegroundParallax();
2876:     }
2877: 
2878:     function endGame(title: string) {
2879:       if (!engineAlive) return;
2880:       engineAlive = false;
2881:       travel = null;
2882:       setAlive(false);
2883:       sound.danger();
2884:     }
2885: 
2886:     function togglePause(force?: boolean) {
2887:       if (!showViewSettings) {
2888:         closeViewSettings();
2889:       }
2890:       if (!engineStarted || !engineAlive) return;
2891:       enginePaused = typeof force === 'boolean' ? force : !enginePaused;
2892:       setPaused(enginePaused);
2893:       if (!enginePaused) lastTimestamp = performance.now();
2894: 
2895:       BotFlightRecorder.getInstance().recordEvent(
2896:         elapsed,
2897:         CONFIG.aiImplementation as 'GREENFIELD_V2' | 'LEGACY',
2898:         botContextV2?.currentState || 'SEARCH',
2899:         'NONE',
2900:         'LIFECYCLE',
2901:         enginePaused ? 'SIMULATION_PAUSED' : 'SIMULATION_RESUMED',
2902:         `Simulation ${enginePaused ? 'paused' : 'resumed'}.`,
2903:         { x: bot?.x || 0, y: bot?.y || 0 },
2904:         { x: player.x, y: player.y },
2905:         CONFIG.botRadius,
2906:         CONFIG.playerRadius,
2907:         botContextV2?.awareness?.id || 0,
2908:         obstacleRevision,
2909:         botContextV2?.debug?.plannerStatus || '',
2910:         botContextV2?.pathIndex || 0
2911:       );
2912:     }
2913: 
2914:     function toggleMode() {
2915:       if (travel || resolveAt) {
2916:         setMessage('Change movement after the current jump.');
2917:         return;
2918:       }
2919:       engineMovementMode = engineMovementMode === 'circuit' ? 'hop' : 'circuit';
2920:       setMovementMode(engineMovementMode);
2921:       setMessage(
2922:         engineMovementMode === 'circuit'
2923:           ? 'Circuit mode routes the spark through powered traces.'
2924:           : 'Hop mode shows the same math loop as a direct platform jump.',
2925:         'neutral',
2926:         1600,
2927:       );
2928:     }
2929: 
2930:     function chooseByIndex(index: number) {
2931:       const row = rowAbove();
2932:       if (!row) return;
2933:       const platform = row.platforms[index];
2934:       if (platform) selectPlatform(platform);
2935:     }
2936: 
2937:     function pointerPosition(event: any) {
2938:       const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
2939:       const touch = event.touches && event.touches[0];
2940:       const clientX = touch ? touch.clientX : event.clientX;
2941:       const clientY = touch ? touch.clientY : event.clientY;
2942:       return {
2943:         x: clientX - rect.left,
2944:         y: clientY - rect.top,
2945:       };
2946:     }
2947: 
2948:     function handlePointer(event: any) {
2949:       if (!engineStarted || !engineAlive || enginePaused || travel || resolveAt) return;
2950:       const point = pointerPosition(event);
2951:       const row = rowAbove();
2952:       if (!row) return;
2953: 
2954:       for (const platform of row.platforms) {
2955:         const screenY = worldToScreenY(platform.y);
2956:         const left = platform.x - platform.width / 2 - 9;
2957:         const right = platform.x + platform.width / 2 + 9;
2958:         const top = screenY - 12;
2959:         const bottom = screenY + platform.height + 18;
2960:         if (point.x >= left && point.x <= right && point.y >= top && point.y <= bottom) {
2961:           selectPlatform(platform);
2962:           event.preventDefault();
2963:           return;
2964:         }
2965:       }
2966:     }
2967: 
2968:     function frame(timestamp: number) {
2969:       animationFrame = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
2970:       if (!lastTimestamp) lastTimestamp = timestamp;
2971:       const delta = clamp(timestamp - lastTimestamp, 0, 34);
2972:       lastTimestamp = timestamp;
2973: 
2974:       if (engineStarted && engineAlive && !enginePaused) update(delta);
2975:       render();
2976:     }
2977: 
2978:     function closeViewSettings() {
2979:       setShowViewSettings(false);
2980:       enginePaused = settingsWasPausedRef.current;
2981:       setPaused(enginePaused);
2982:       if (!enginePaused) {
2983:         lastTimestamp = performance.now();
2984:       }
2985:     }
2986: 
2987:     // Connect Engine callbacks to React Ref controllers
2988:     loopControlRef.current = {
2989:       beginGame,
2990:       restart,
2991:       togglePause,
2992:       toggleMode,
2993:       toggleSound() {
2994:         engineSoundEnabled = !engineSoundEnabled;
2995:         setSoundEnabled(engineSoundEnabled);
2996:         if (engineSoundEnabled) getAudioContext();
2997:       },
2998:       selectByIndex(idx) {
2999:         chooseByIndex(idx);
3000:       },
3001:       applyViewScale(val, opts) {
3002:         applyViewScale(val, opts);
3003:       },
3004:       applyRouteTurnCount(val, opts) {
3005:         applyRouteTurnCount(val, opts);
3006:       },
3007:       applyDifficulty(diff) {
3008:         setDifficultyState(diff);
3009:         CONFIG.difficulty = diff;
3010:         applyViewScale(viewScalePercentInternal, { reflow: false, persist: false });
3011:       },
3012:       applyAiImplementation(impl) {
3013:         setAiImplementationState(impl);
3014:         CONFIG.aiImplementation = impl;
3015:         // Re-init bot contexts to clear state
3016:         if (bot) {
3017:           const spawnPos = calculateRepairedBotSpawn();
3018:           bot = initBotAIState(spawnPos.x, spawnPos.y, player.x);
3019:         }
3020:         botContextV2 = impl !== 'LEGACY' ? createBotContextV2() : null;
3021:       },
3022:       applyBringUpStage(stage) {
3023:         setBringUpStageState(stage);
3024:         CONFIG.bringUpStage = stage;
3025:         // Re-initialize bot and reset game loop elements to let the user immediately see the selected stage behavior!
3026:         if (bot) {
3027:           const spawnPos = calculateRepairedBotSpawn(stage !== 'NORMAL');
3028:           bot = initBotAIState(spawnPos.x, spawnPos.y, player.x);
3029:           botContextV2 = CONFIG.aiImplementation !== 'LEGACY' ? createBotContextV2() : null;
3030:           botTrail = [];
3031:           echoes = [];
3032:         }
3033:       },
3034:       resetViewSettings() {
3035:         applyRouteTurnCount(8, { persist: false });
3036:         applyViewScale(100);
3037:       },
3038:       exportSettings() {
3039:         refreshConfigOutput();
3040:         setShowConfig(true);
3041:       },
3042:       closeViewSettings() {
3043:         closeViewSettings();
3044:       },
3045:       debugGetRows: () => rows,
3046:       debugGetPlayer: () => player,
3047:       debugGetPlayerPresentation: () => playerNumberPresentation,
3048:       debugGetBot: () => bot,
3049:       debugGetBotV2Debug: () => botContextV2?.debug || null,
3050:       debugGetCONFIG: () => CONFIG,
3051:       debugGetTravel: () => travel,
3052:       debugGetResolveAt: () => resolveAt,
3053:       setShowSumToCue: (v: boolean) => setShowSumToCue(v),
3054:       debugGetElapsed: () => elapsed,
3055:       debugMakeRow: makeRow,
3056:       debugEnsureRows: ensureRows,
3057:       debugGetRow: getRow,
3058:       debugArrive: arrive,
3059:       
3060:       debugSelectPlatform: selectPlatform,
3061:       debugUpdate: (delta: number) => { update(delta); },
3062:       debugDraw: () => { render(); }
3063:     };
3064: 
3065:     // DOM Event Listeners matching index.html
3066:     canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointer, { passive: false });
3067:     window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
3068: 
3069:     const observer = typeof ResizeObserver !== 'undefined'
3070:       ? new ResizeObserver(resize)
3071:       : null;
3072:     if (observer) {
3073:       observer.observe(app);
3074:     }
3075: 
3076:     // Set initial values
3077:     const savedScale = readSavedViewScale();

3079:     applyRouteTurnCount(savedTurns, { persist: false });
3080:     applyViewScale(savedScale, { reflow: false, persist: false });
3081:     resize();
3082:     restart({ preserveOverlay: true });
3083: 
3084:     animationFrame = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
3085:

3088:       canvas.removeEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointer);
3089:       window.removeEventListener('resize', resize);
3090:       if (observer) {

3092:       }

3095:       }

3100:   const beginGame = () => loopControlRef.current.beginGame?.();

3102:   const togglePause = (force?: boolean) => loopControlRef.current.togglePause?.(force);

3110:     setShowViewSettings(true);

3120:     if (showConfig) loopControlRef.current.exportSettings?.();
3121:   };

3134:   };

3137:     loopControlRef.current.applyBringUpStage?.(stage);
3138:   };
3139:

3141:     loopControlRef.current.resetViewSettings?.();
3142:     if (showConfig) loopControlRef.current.exportSettings?.();
3143:   };
